```
   109→def parse_batch_selection(raw: str | None, batch_count: int) -> list[int]:
   110→    """Parse optional 1-based CSV list of batches."""
   111→    if not raw:
   112→        return list(range(batch_count))
   113→
   114→    selected: list[int] = []
   115→    seen: set[int] = set()
   116→    for token in raw.split(","):
   117→        text = token.strip()
   118→        if not text:
   119→            continue
   120→        idx_1 = int(text)
   121→        if idx_1 < 1 or idx_1 > batch_count:
   122→            raise ValueError(f"batch index {idx_1} out of range 1..{batch_count}")
   123→        idx_0 = idx_1 - 1
   124→        if idx_0 in seen:
   125→            continue
   126→        seen.add(idx_0)
   127→        selected.append(idx_0)
   128→    return selected
   129→
   130→
   131→def extract_json_payload(raw: str, *, log_fn) -> dict[str, object] | None:
   132→    """Best-effort extraction of first JSON object from agent output text."""
   133→    text = raw.strip()
   134→    if not text:
   135→        return None
   136→
   137→    decoder = json.JSONDecoder()
   138→    last_decode_error: json.JSONDecodeError | None = None
   139→    for start, ch in enumerate(text):
   140→        if ch not in "{[":
   141→            continue
   142→        try:
   143→            obj, _ = decoder.raw_decode(text[start:])
   144→        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
   145→            last_decode_error = exc
   146→            continue
   147→        if (
   148→            isinstance(obj, dict)
   149→            and isinstance(obj.get("assessments"), dict)
   150→            and isinstance(obj.get("issues"), list)
   151→        ):
   152→            return obj
   153→    if last_decode_error is not None:
   154→        log_fn(f"  batch output JSON parse failed: {last_decode_error.msg}")
   155→    else:
   156→        log_fn("  batch output JSON parse failed: no valid payload found")
   157→    return None
   158→
   159→
   160→def _validate_dimension_note(
   161→    key: str,
   162→    note_raw: object,
   163→) -> tuple[list[object], str, str, str, str]:
   164→    """Validate a single dimension_notes entry and return parsed fields.
   165→
   166→    Returns (evidence, impact_scope, fix_scope, confidence, issues_preventing_higher_score).
   167→    Raises ValueError on invalid structure.
   168→    """
   169→    if not isinstance(note_raw, dict):
   170→        raise ValueError(
   171→            f"dimension_notes missing object for assessed dimension: {key}"
   172→        )
   173→    evidence = note_raw.get("evidence")
   174→    impact_scope = note_raw.get("impact_scope")
   175→    fix_scope = note_raw.get("fix_scope")
   176→    if not isinstance(evidence, list) or not evidence:
   177→        raise ValueError(
   178→            f"dimension_notes.{key}.evidence must be a non-empty array"
   179→        )
   180→    if not isinstance(impact_scope, str) or not impact_scope.strip():
   181→        raise ValueError(
   182→            f"dimension_notes.{key}.impact_scope must be a non-empty string"
   183→        )
   184→    if not isinstance(fix_scope, str) or not fix_scope.strip():
   185→        raise ValueError(
   186→            f"dimension_notes.{key}.fix_scope must be a non-empty string"
   187→        )
   188→
   189→    confidence_raw = str(note_raw.get("confidence", "medium")).strip().lower()
   190→    confidence = (
   191→        confidence_raw if confidence_raw in {"high", "medium", "low"} else "medium"
   192→    )
   193→    issues_note = str(note_raw.get(DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY, "")).strip()
   194→    if not issues_note:
   195→        issues_note = str(note_raw.get(LEGACY_DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY, "")).strip()
   196→    return evidence, impact_scope, fix_scope, confidence, issues_note
   197→
   198→
   199→def _normalize_abstraction_sub_axes(
   200→    note_raw: dict[str, object],
   201→    abstraction_sub_axes: tuple[str, ...],
   202→) -> dict[str, float]:
   203→    """Extract and clamp abstraction_fitness sub-axis scores from a note."""
   204→    sub_axes_raw = note_raw.get("sub_axes")
   205→    if sub_axes_raw is not None and not isinstance(sub_axes_raw, dict):
   206→        raise ValueError(
   207→            "dimension_notes.abstraction_fitness.sub_axes must be an object"
   208→        )
   209→    if not isinstance(sub_axes_raw, dict):
   210→        return {}
   211→
   212→    normalized: dict[str, float] = {}
   213→    for axis in abstraction_sub_axes:
   214→        axis_value = sub_axes_raw.get(axis)
   215→        if axis_value is None:
   216→            continue
   217→        if isinstance(axis_value, bool) or not isinstance(
   218→            axis_value, int | float
   219→        ):
   220→            raise ValueError(
   221→                f"dimension_notes.abstraction_fitness.sub_axes.{axis} "
   222→                "must be numeric"
   223→            )
   224→        normalized[axis] = round(
   225→            max(0.0, min(100.0, float(axis_value))),
   226→            1,
   227→        )
   228→    return normalized
   229→
   230→
   231→def _normalize_issues(
   232→    raw_issues: object,
   233→    dimension_notes: dict[str, BatchDimensionNotePayload],
   234→    *,
   235→    max_batch_issues: int,
   236→    allowed_dims: set[str],
   237→    low_score_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   238→) -> list[NormalizedBatchIssue]:
   239→    """Validate and normalize the issues array from a batch payload."""
   240→    if not isinstance(raw_issues, list):
   241→        raise ValueError("issues must be an array")
   242→
   243→    issues: list[NormalizedBatchIssue] = []
   244→    errors: list[str] = []
   245→    for idx, item in enumerate(raw_issues):
   246→        issue: ReviewIssuePayload | None
   247→        issue, issue_errors = validate_review_issue_payload(
   248→            item,
   249→            label=f"issues[{idx}]",
   250→            allowed_dimensions=allowed_dims,
   251→            allow_dismissed=False,
   252→        )
   253→        if issue_errors:
   254→            errors.extend(issue_errors)
   255→            continue
   256→        if issue is None:
   257→            raise ValueError(
   258→                "batch issue payload missing after validation succeeded"
   259→            )
   260→
   261→        dim = issue["dimension"]
   262→        note = dimension_notes.get(dim, {})
   263→        impact_scope = str(
   264→            (item if isinstance(item, dict) else {}).get(
   265→                "impact_scope", note.get("impact_scope", "")
   266→            )
   267→        ).strip()
   268→        fix_scope = str(
   269→            (item if isinstance(item, dict) else {}).get(
   270→                "fix_scope", note.get("fix_scope", "")
   271→            )
   272→        ).strip()
   273→        if not impact_scope or not fix_scope:
   274→            errors.append(
   275→                f"issues[{idx}] requires impact_scope and fix_scope "
   276→                "(or dimension_notes defaults)"
   277→            )
   278→            continue
   279→        issues.append(
   280→            NormalizedBatchIssue(
   281→                dimension=issue["dimension"],
   282→                identifier=issue["identifier"],
   283→                summary=issue["summary"],
   284→                confidence=issue["confidence"],
   285→                suggestion=issue["suggestion"],
   286→                related_files=list(issue.get("related_files", [])),
   287→                evidence=list(issue.get("evidence", [])),
   288→                impact_scope=impact_scope,
   289→                fix_scope=fix_scope,
   290→                reasoning=str(issue.get("reasoning", "")),
   291→                evidence_lines=list(issue.get("evidence_lines", []))
   292→                if isinstance(issue.get("evidence_lines"), list)
   293→                else None,
   294→            )
   295→        )
   296→    if errors:
   297→        visible = errors[:10]
   298→        remaining = len(errors) - len(visible)
   299→        if remaining > 0:
   300→            visible.append(f"... {remaining} additional issue schema error(s) omitted")
   301→        raise ValueError("; ".join(visible))
   302→    if len(issues) <= max_batch_issues:
   303→        return issues
   304→
   305→    required_dims = set(low_score_dimensions or set())
   306→    if not required_dims:
   307→        return issues[:max_batch_issues]
   308→
   309→    # Preserve at least one issue per low-score dimension before trimming.
   310→    selected: list[NormalizedBatchIssue] = []
   311→    selected_indexes: set[int] = set()
   312→    covered: set[str] = set()
   313→    for idx, issue in enumerate(issues):
   314→        if len(selected) >= max_batch_issues:
   315→            break
   316→        dim = issue.dimension.strip()
   317→        if dim not in required_dims or dim in covered:
   318→            continue
   319→        selected.append(issue)
   320→        selected_indexes.add(idx)
   321→        covered.add(dim)
   322→
   323→    for idx, issue in enumerate(issues):
   324→        if len(selected) >= max_batch_issues:
   325→            break
   326→        if idx in selected_indexes:
   327→            continue
   328→        selected.append(issue)
   329→    return selected
   330→
   331→
   332→def _low_score_dimensions(assessments: dict[str, float]) -> set[str]:
   333→    """Return assessed dimensions requiring explicit defect issues."""
   334→    return {
   335→        dim
   336→        for dim, score in assessments.items()
   337→        if score < LOW_SCORE_ISSUE_THRESHOLD
   338→    }
   339→
   340→
   341→def _enforce_low_score_issues(
   342→    *,
   343→    assessments: dict[str, float],
   344→    issues: list[NormalizedBatchIssue],
   345→) -> None:
   346→    """Fail closed when low scores do not report explicit issues."""
   347→    required_dims = _low_score_dimensions(assessments)
   348→    if not required_dims:
   349→        return
   350→    issue_dims = {
   351→        issue.dimension.strip() for issue in issues
   352→    }
   353→    missing = sorted(dim for dim in required_dims if dim not in issue_dims)
   354→    if not missing:
   355→        return
   356→    joined = ", ".join(missing)
   357→    raise ValueError(
   358→        "low-score dimensions must include at least one explicit issue: "
   359→        f"{joined} (threshold {LOW_SCORE_ISSUE_THRESHOLD:.1f})"
   360→    )
   361→
   362→
   363→def _compute_batch_quality(
   364→    assessments: dict[str, float],
   365→    issues: list[NormalizedBatchIssue],
   366→    dimension_notes: dict[str, BatchDimensionNotePayload],
   367→    allowed_dims: set[str],
   368→    high_score_missing_issue_note: float,
   369→) -> BatchQualityPayload:
   370→    """Compute quality metrics for a single batch result."""
   371→    return {
   372→        "dimension_coverage": round(
   373→            len(assessments) / max(len(allowed_dims), 1),
   374→            3,
   375→        ),
   376→        "evidence_density": round(
   377→            sum(len(note.get("evidence", [])) for note in dimension_notes.values())
   378→            / max(len(issues), 1),
   379→            3,
   380→        ),
   381→        "high_score_missing_issue_note": high_score_missing_issue_note,
   382→    }
   383→
   384→
   385→def normalize_batch_result(
   386→    payload: dict[str, object],
   387→    allowed_dims: set[str],
   388→    *,
   389→    max_batch_issues: int,
   390→    abstraction_sub_axes: tuple[str, ...],
   391→) -> tuple[
   392→    dict[str, float],
   393→    list[BatchIssuePayload],
   394→    dict[str, BatchDimensionNotePayload],
   395→    BatchQualityPayload,
   396→]:
   397→    """Validate and normalize one batch payload."""
   398→    if "assessments" not in payload:
   399→        raise ValueError("payload missing required key: assessments")
   400→    key_error = normalize_legacy_findings_alias(
   401→        payload,
   402→        missing_issues_error="payload missing required key: issues",
   403→    )
   404→    if key_error is not None:
   405→        raise ValueError(key_error)
   406→
   407→    raw_assessments = payload.get("assessments")
   408→    if not isinstance(raw_assessments, dict):
   409→        raise ValueError("assessments must be an object")
   410→
   411→    raw_dimension_notes = payload.get("dimension_notes", {})
   412→    if not isinstance(raw_dimension_notes, dict):
   413→        raise ValueError("dimension_notes must be an object")
   414→
   415→    assessments: dict[str, float] = {}
   416→    dimension_notes: dict[str, BatchDimensionNotePayload] = {}
   417→    high_score_missing_issue_note = 0.0
   418→    for key, value in raw_assessments.items():
   419→        if not isinstance(key, str) or not key:
   420→            continue
   421→        if key not in allowed_dims:
   422→            continue
   423→        if isinstance(value, bool):
   424→            continue
   425→        if not isinstance(value, int | float):
   426→            continue
   427→        score = round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))), 1)
   428→
   429→        note_raw = raw_dimension_notes.get(key)
   430→        evidence, impact_scope, fix_scope, confidence, issues_note = (
   431→            _validate_dimension_note(key, note_raw)
   432→        )
   433→        if not isinstance(note_raw, dict):
   434→            raise ValueError(
   435→                f"dimension_notes missing object for assessed dimension: {key}"
   436→            )
   437→        if score > HIGH_SCORE_ISSUES_NOTE_THRESHOLD and not issues_note:
   438→            high_score_missing_issue_note += 1
   439→
   440→        normalized_sub_axes: dict[str, float] = {}
   441→        if key == "abstraction_fitness":
   442→            normalized_sub_axes = _normalize_abstraction_sub_axes(
   443→                note_raw, abstraction_sub_axes
   444→            )
   445→
   446→        assessments[key] = score
   447→        dimension_notes[key] = {
   448→            "evidence": [str(item).strip() for item in evidence if str(item).strip()],
   449→            "impact_scope": impact_scope.strip(),
   450→            "fix_scope": fix_scope.strip(),
   451→            "confidence": confidence,
   452→            "issues_preventing_higher_score": issues_note,
   453→        }
   454→        if normalized_sub_axes:
   455→            dimension_notes[key]["sub_axes"] = normalized_sub_axes
   456→
   457→    issues = _normalize_issues(
   458→        payload.get("issues"),
   459→        dimension_notes,
   460→        max_batch_issues=max_batch_issues,
   461→        allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   462→        low_score_dimensions=_low_score_dimensions(assessments),
   463→    )
   464→    _enforce_low_score_issues(assessments=assessments, issues=issues)
   465→
   466→    quality = _compute_batch_quality(
   467→        assessments,
   468→        issues,
   469→        dimension_notes,
   470→        allowed_dims,
   471→        high_score_missing_issue_note,
   472→    )
   473→    return (
   474→        assessments,
   475→        [issue.to_payload() for issue in issues],
   476→        dimension_notes,
   477→        quality,
   478→    )
   479→
   480→
   481→def assessment_weight(
   482→    *,
   483→    dimension: str,
   484→    issues: list[BatchIssuePayload],
   485→    dimension_notes: dict[str, BatchDimensionNotePayload],
   486→) -> float:
   487→    """Evidence-weighted assessment score weight with a neutral floor.
   488→
   489→    Weighting is evidence-based and score-independent: the raw score does not
   490→    influence how much weight a batch contributes during merge.
   491→    """
   492→    note = dimension_notes.get(dimension, {})
   493→    note_evidence = len(note.get("evidence", [])) if isinstance(note, dict) else 0
   494→    issue_count = sum(
   495→        1
   496→        for issue in issues
   497→        if str(issue.get("dimension", "")).strip() == dimension
   498→    )
   499→    return float(1 + note_evidence + issue_count)
   500→
   501→
   502→def _issue_pressure_by_dimension(
   503→    issues: list[BatchIssuePayload],
   504→    *,
   505→    dimension_notes: dict[str, BatchDimensionNotePayload],
   506→) -> tuple[dict[str, float], dict[str, int]]:
   507→    """Summarize how strongly issues should pull dimension scores down."""
   508→    return _DIMENSION_SCORER.issue_pressure_by_dimension(
   509→        issues,
   510→        dimension_notes=dimension_notes,
   511→    )
   512→
   513→
   514→def _accumulate_batch_scores(
   515→    result: BatchResultPayload,
   516→    *,
   517→    score_buckets: dict[str, list[tuple[float, float]]],
   518→    score_raw_by_dim: dict[str, list[float]],
   519→    merged_dimension_notes: dict[str, BatchDimensionNotePayload],
   520→    abstraction_axis_scores: dict[str, list[tuple[float, float]]],
   521→    abstraction_sub_axes: tuple[str, ...],
   522→) -> None:
   523→    """Accumulate assessment scores, dimension notes, and sub-axis data from one batch."""
   524→    result_issues = result.get("issues", [])
   525→    result_notes = result.get("dimension_notes", {})
   526→    for key, score in result.get("assessments", {}).items():
   527→        if isinstance(score, bool):
   528→            continue
   529→        score_value = float(score)
   530→        weight = assessment_weight(
   531→            dimension=key,
   532→            issues=result_issues,
   533→            dimension_notes=result_notes,
   534→        )
   535→        score_buckets.setdefault(key, []).append((score_value, weight))
   536→        score_raw_by_dim.setdefault(key, []).append(score_value)
   537→
   538→        note = result_notes.get(key)
   539→        existing = merged_dimension_notes.get(key)
   540→        existing_evidence = (
   541→            len(existing.get("evidence", [])) if isinstance(existing, dict) else -1
   542→        )
   543→        current_evidence = (
   544→            len(note.get("evidence", [])) if isinstance(note, dict) else -1
   545→        )
   546→        if current_evidence > existing_evidence and note is not None:
   547→            merged_dimension_notes[key] = note
   548→
   549→        if key == "abstraction_fitness" and isinstance(note, dict):
   550→            sub_axes = note.get("sub_axes")
   551→            if isinstance(sub_axes, dict):
   552→                for axis in abstraction_sub_axes:
   553→                    axis_score = sub_axes.get(axis)
   554→                    if isinstance(axis_score, bool) or not isinstance(
   555→                        axis_score, int | float
   556→                    ):
   557→                        continue
   558→                    abstraction_axis_scores[axis].append(
   559→                        (float(axis_score), weight)
   560→                    )
   561→
   562→
   563→def _issue_identity_key(issue: BatchIssuePayload) -> str:
   564→    """Build a stable concept key; prefer dimension+identifier when available."""
   565→    dim = str(issue.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   566→    ident = str(issue.get("identifier", "")).strip()
   567→    if ident:
   568→        return f"{dim}::{ident}"
   569→    summary = str(issue.get("summary", "")).strip()
   570→    summary_terms = sorted(normalize_word_set(summary))
   571→    if summary_terms:
   572→        return f"{dim}::summary::{','.join(summary_terms[:8])}"
   573→    return f"{dim}::{summary}"
   574→
   575→
   576→def _merge_issue_payload(
   577→    existing: BatchIssuePayload, incoming: BatchIssuePayload
   578→) -> None:
   579→    """Merge two concept-equivalent issues into the existing payload."""
   580→    merge_list_fields(existing, incoming, ("related_files", "evidence"))
   581→    # Prefer richer summary/suggestion text when they differ.
   582→    pick_longer_text(existing, incoming, "summary")
   583→    pick_longer_text(existing, incoming, "suggestion")
   584→    track_merged_from(existing, str(incoming.get("identifier", "")).strip())
   585→
   586→
   587→def _should_merge_issues(
   588→    existing: BatchIssuePayload, incoming: BatchIssuePayload
   589→) -> bool:
   590→    """Check whether two key-matched issues are similar enough to merge."""
   591→    existing_summary = normalize_word_set(str(existing.get("summary", "")))
   592→    incoming_summary = normalize_word_set(str(incoming.get("summary", "")))
   593→    if existing_summary and incoming_summary:
   594→        overlap = len(existing_summary & incoming_summary)
   595→        union = len(existing_summary | incoming_summary)
   596→        if union and overlap / union >= 0.3:
   597→            return True
   598→    # Fall back to related-file overlap
   599→    existing_files = set(cast(list[str], existing.get("related_files", [])))
   600→    incoming_files = set(cast(list[str], incoming.get("related_files", [])))
   601→    if existing_files and incoming_files:
   602→        return bool(existing_files & incoming_files)
   603→    # When no corroborating signal is available, allow merge
   604→    return not existing_summary or not incoming_summary
   605→
   606→
   607→def _accumulate_batch_quality(
   608→    result: BatchResultPayload,
   609→    *,
   610→    coverage_values: list[float],
   611→    evidence_density_values: list[float],
   612→) -> float:
   613→    """Accumulate quality metrics from one batch. Returns high-score-missing-issues delta."""
   614→    quality: object = result.get("quality", {})
   615→    if not isinstance(quality, dict):
   616→        return 0.0
   617→    coverage = quality.get("dimension_coverage")
   618→    density = quality.get("evidence_density")
   619→    missing_issue_note = quality.get(REVIEW_QUALITY_HIGH_SCORE_MISSING_ISSUES_KEY)
   620→    if not isinstance(missing_issue_note, int | float):
   621→        missing_issue_note = quality.get(
   622→            LEGACY_REVIEW_QUALITY_HIGH_SCORE_MISSING_ISSUES_KEY
   623→        )
   624→    if isinstance(coverage, int | float):
   625→        coverage_values.append(float(coverage))
   626→    if isinstance(density, int | float):
   627→        evidence_density_values.append(float(density))
   628→    return (
   629→        float(missing_issue_note)
   630→        if isinstance(missing_issue_note, int | float)
   631→        else 0.0
   632→    )
   633→
   634→
   635→def _compute_merged_assessments(
   636→    score_buckets: dict[str, list[tuple[float, float]]],
   637→    score_raw_by_dim: dict[str, list[float]],
   638→    issue_pressure_by_dim: dict[str, float],
   639→    issue_count_by_dim: dict[str, int],
   640→) -> dict[str, float]:
   641→    """Compute pressure-adjusted weighted mean for each dimension."""
   642→    return _DIMENSION_SCORER.merge_scores(
   643→        score_buckets,
   644→        score_raw_by_dim,
   645→        issue_pressure_by_dim,
   646→        issue_count_by_dim,
   647→    )
   648→
   649→
   650→def _compute_abstraction_components(
   651→    merged_assessments: dict[str, float],
   652→    abstraction_axis_scores: dict[str, list[tuple[float, float]]],
   653→    *,
   654→    abstraction_sub_axes: tuple[str, ...],
   655→    abstraction_component_names: dict[str, str],
   656→) -> dict[str, float] | None:
   657→    """Compute weighted abstraction sub-axis component scores.
   658→
   659→    Returns component_scores dict, or None if abstraction_fitness is not assessed.
   660→    """
   661→    abstraction_score = merged_assessments.get("abstraction_fitness")
   662→    if abstraction_score is None:
   663→        return None
   664→
   665→    component_scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   666→    for axis in abstraction_sub_axes:
   667→        weighted = abstraction_axis_scores.get(axis, [])
   668→        if not weighted:
   669→            continue
   670→        numerator = sum(score * weight for score, weight in weighted)
   671→        denominator = sum(weight for _, weight in weighted)
   672→        if denominator <= 0:
   673→            continue
   674→        component_scores[abstraction_component_names[axis]] = round(
   675→            max(0.0, min(100.0, numerator / denominator)),
   676→            1,
   677→        )
   678→    return component_scores if component_scores else None
   679→
   680→
   681→def merge_batch_results(
   682→    batch_results: list[BatchResultPayload],
   683→    *,
   684→    abstraction_sub_axes: tuple[str, ...],
   685→    abstraction_component_names: dict[str, str],
   686→) -> dict[str, object]:
   687→    """Deterministically merge assessments/issues across batch outputs."""
   688→    from .merge import merge_batch_results as _merge_batch_results
   689→
   690→    return _merge_batch_results(
   691→        batch_results,
   692→        abstraction_sub_axes=abstraction_sub_axes,
   693→        abstraction_component_names=abstraction_component_names,
   694→    )
   695→
   696→
   697→def build_batch_prompt(
   698→    *,
   699→    repo_root: Path,
   700→    packet_path: Path,
   701→    batch_index: int,
   702→    batch: dict[str, object],
   703→) -> str:
   704→    """Render one subagent prompt for a holistic investigation batch."""
   705→    return render_batch_prompt(
   706→        repo_root=repo_root,
   707→        packet_path=packet_path,
   708→        batch_index=batch_index,
   709→        batch=batch,
   710→    )
   711→
   712→
   713→__all__ = [
   714→    "assessment_weight",
   715→    "build_batch_prompt",
   716→    "extract_json_payload",
   717→    "merge_batch_results",
   718→    "normalize_batch_result",
   719→    "parse_batch_selection",
   720→]
   721→
```

```
   367→        )
   368→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   369→        for idx in selected_indexes:
   370→            key = str(idx + 1)
   371→            state = batch_status.setdefault(
   372→                key,
   373→                {"position": batch_positions.get(idx, 0), "status": "pending"},
   374→            )
   375→            if state.get("status") in {"pending", "queued", "running"}:
   376→                state["status"] = "interrupted"
   377→        write_run_summary(
   378→            successful_batches=[],
   379→            failed_batches=[],
   380→            interrupted=True,
   381→            interruption_reason="keyboard_interrupt",
   382→        )
   383→        append_run_log("run-interrupted reason=keyboard_interrupt")
   384→        raise SystemExit(130) from None
   385→
   386→    allowed_dims = {
   387→        str(dim) for dim in packet.get("dimensions", []) if isinstance(dim, str)
   388→    }
   389→    batch_results, failures = collect_batch_results_fn(
   390→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   391→        failures=execution_failures,
   392→        output_files=output_files,
   393→        allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   394→    )
   395→
   396→    execution_failure_set = set(execution_failures)
   397→    failure_set = set(failures)
   398→    successful_indexes = sorted(idx for idx in selected_indexes if idx not in failure_set)
   399→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   400→        key = str(idx + 1)
   401→        state = batch_status.setdefault(
   402→            key,
   403→            {"position": batch_positions.get(idx, 0), "status": "pending"},
   404→        )
   405→        if idx not in failure_set:
   406→            state["status"] = "succeeded"
   407→            continue
   408→        if idx in execution_failure_set:
   409→            state["status"] = "failed"
   410→            continue
   411→        if not output_files[idx].exists():
   412→            state["status"] = "missing_output"
   413→            continue
   414→        state["status"] = "parse_failed"
   415→
   416→    write_run_summary(
   417→        successful_batches=[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes],
   418→        failed_batches=[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)],
   419→    )
   420→
   421→    if failures and (not allow_partial or not batch_results):
   422→        append_run_log(
   423→            f"run-finished failures={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)]} mode=exit"
   424→        )
   425→        print_failures_and_raise_fn(
   426→            failures=failures,
   427→            packet_path=immutable_packet_path,
   428→            logs_dir=logs_dir,
   429→            colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   430→        )
   431→    elif failures:
   432→        print(
   433→            colorize_fn(
   434→                "  Partial completion enabled: importing successful batches and keeping failed batches open.",
   435→                "yellow",
   436→            )
   437→        )
   438→        print_failures_fn(
   439→            failures=failures,
   440→            packet_path=immutable_packet_path,
   441→            logs_dir=logs_dir,
   442→            colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   443→        )
   444→        append_run_log(
   445→            "run-partial "
   446→            f"successful={[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes]} "
   447→            f"failed={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)]}"
   448→        )
   449→
   450→    merged = merge_batch_results_fn(batch_results)
   451→    reviewed_files = collect_reviewed_files_from_batches(
   452→        batches=batches,
   453→        selected_indexes=successful_indexes,
   454→    )
   455→    full_sweep_included = any(
   456→        str(batch.get("name", "")).strip().lower() == "full codebase sweep"
   457→        for idx in successful_indexes
   458→        if 0 <= idx < len(batches)
   459→        for batch in [batches[idx]]
   460→        if isinstance(batch, dict)
   461→    )
   462→    review_scope: dict[str, object] = {
   463→        "reviewed_files_count": len(reviewed_files),
   464→        "successful_batch_count": len(successful_indexes),
   465→        "full_sweep_included": full_sweep_included,
   466→    }
   467→    total_files = packet.get("total_files")
   468→    if isinstance(total_files, int) and not isinstance(total_files, bool) and total_files > 0:
   469→        review_scope["total_files"] = total_files
   470→    merged["review_scope"] = review_scope
   471→    if reviewed_files:
   472→        merged["reviewed_files"] = reviewed_files
   473→        print(
   474→            colorize_fn(
   475→                f"  Reviewed files captured for cache refresh: {len(reviewed_files)}",
   476→                "dim",
   477→            )
   478→        )
   479→    merged["provenance"] = build_import_provenance_fn(
   480→        runner=runner,
   481→        blind_packet_path=prompt_packet_path,
   482→        run_stamp=stamp,
   483→        batch_indexes=successful_indexes,
   484→    )
   485→    merged_assessment_dims = normalize_dimension_list(
   486→        list((merged.get("assessments") or {}).keys())
   487→    )
   488→    merged_issue_dims = normalize_dimension_list(
   489→        [
   490→            issue.get("dimension")
   491→            for issue in (merged.get("issues") or [])
   492→            if isinstance(issue, dict)
   493→        ]
   494→    )
   495→    merged_imported_dims = normalize_dimension_list(
   496→        merged_assessment_dims + merged_issue_dims
   497→    )
   498→    review_scope["imported_dimensions"] = merged_imported_dims
   499→    missing_after_import = print_import_dimension_coverage_notice(
   500→        assessed_dims=merged_assessment_dims,
   501→        scored_dims=scored_dimensions,
   502→        scan_path=scan_path,
   503→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   504→    )
   505→    merged["assessment_coverage"] = {
   506→        "scored_dimensions": scored_dimensions,
   507→        "selected_dimensions": packet_dimensions,
   508→        "imported_dimensions": merged_assessment_dims,
   509→        "missing_dimensions": missing_after_import,
   510→    }
   511→    merged_path = run_dir / "holistic_issues_merged.json"
   512→    safe_write_text_fn(merged_path, json.dumps(merged, indent=2) + "\n")
   513→    print(colorize_fn(f"\n  Merged outputs: {merged_path}", "bold"))
   514→    print_review_quality(merged.get("review_quality", {}), colorize_fn=colorize_fn)
   515→
   516→    try:
   517→        do_import_fn(
   518→            str(merged_path),
   519→            state,
   520→            lang,
   521→            state_file,
   522→            config=config,
   523→            allow_partial=allow_partial,
   524→            trusted_assessment_source=True,
   525→            trusted_assessment_label="trusted internal run-batches import",
   526→        )
   527→    except SystemExit as exc:
   528→        append_run_log(f"run-finished import-failed code={exc.code}")
   529→        raise
   530→    except Exception as exc:
   531→        append_run_log(f"run-finished import-error error={exc}")
   532→        raise
   533→    append_run_log(
   534→        "run-finished "
   535→        f"successful={[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes]} "
   536→        f"failed={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)]} imported={str(merged_path)}"
   537→    )
   538→
   539→    if getattr(args, "scan_after_import", False):
   540→        followup_code = run_followup_scan_fn(
   541→            lang_name=lang.name,
   542→            scan_path=str(args.path),
   543→        )
   544→        if followup_code != 0:
   545→            raise CommandError(
   546→                f"Error: follow-up scan failed with exit code {followup_code}.",
   547→                exit_code=followup_code,
   548→            )
   549→
   550→
   551→__all__ = ["do_run_batches"]
   552→
```

```
1→"""Payload parsing helpers for review import workflows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.intelligence.review.importing.contracts import (
     9→    ReviewImportPayload,
    10→    ReviewIssuePayload,
    11→)
    12→
    13→LEGACY_FINDINGS_ALIAS_SUNSET_DATE = "2026-12-31"
    14→ALLOW_LEGACY_FINDINGS_ALIAS = True
    15→
    16→
    17→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    18→class ReviewImportEnvelope:
    19→    """Validated shared payload shape for review imports."""
    20→
    21→    issues: list[ReviewIssuePayload]
    22→    assessments: dict[str, Any] | None
    23→    reviewed_files: list[str]
    24→
    25→
    26→def normalize_legacy_findings_alias(
    27→    payload: dict[str, Any],
    28→    *,
    29→    missing_issues_error: str,
    30→    allow_legacy_findings: bool = ALLOW_LEGACY_FINDINGS_ALIAS,
    31→) -> str | None:
    32→    """Normalize legacy ``findings`` into canonical ``issues`` in one place.
    33→
    34→    ``allow_legacy_findings`` is the compatibility cutoff flag; once flipped to
    35→    ``False`` only canonical ``issues`` payloads are accepted.
    36→    """
    37→    if "issues" in payload:
    38→        return None
    39→    if "findings" not in payload:
    40→        return missing_issues_error
    41→    if not allow_legacy_findings:
    42→        return (
    43→            "legacy key 'findings' is no longer accepted; use 'issues' "
    44→            f"(support sunset: {LEGACY_FINDINGS_ALIAS_SUNSET_DATE})"
    45→        )
    46→    payload["issues"] = payload.pop("findings")
    47→    return None
    48→
    49→
    50→def extract_reviewed_files(data: list[dict] | dict) -> list[str]:
    51→    """Parse optional reviewed-file list from import payload."""
    52→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
    53→        return []
    54→    raw = data.get("reviewed_files")
    55→    if not isinstance(raw, list):
    56→        return []
    57→
    58→    reviewed: list[str] = []
    59→    seen: set[str] = set()
    60→    for item in raw:
    61→        if not isinstance(item, str):
    62→            continue
    63→        path = item.strip()
    64→        if not path or path in seen:
    65→            continue
    66→        seen.add(path)
    67→        reviewed.append(path)
    68→    return reviewed
    69→
    70→
    71→def parse_review_import_payload(
    72→    data: ReviewImportPayload | dict[str, Any],
    73→    *,
    74→    mode_name: str,
    75→) -> ReviewImportEnvelope:
    76→    """Parse shared review import payload shape for per-file/holistic flows."""
    77→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
    78→        raise ValueError(f"{mode_name} review import payload must be a JSON object")
    79→
    80→    missing_issues_error = f"{mode_name} review import payload must contain 'issues'"
    81→    key_error = normalize_legacy_findings_alias(
    82→        data,
    83→        missing_issues_error=missing_issues_error,
    84→    )
    85→    if key_error is not None:
    86→        raise ValueError(key_error)
    87→
    88→    issues_list = data.get("issues")
    89→    if not isinstance(issues_list, list):
    90→        raise ValueError(f"{mode_name} review import payload 'issues' must be a list")
    91→    for idx, entry in enumerate(issues_list):
    92→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    93→            raise ValueError(
    94→                f"{mode_name} review import payload 'issues[{idx}]' must be an object"
    95→            )
    96→
    97→    assessments = data.get("assessments")
    98→    if assessments is not None and not isinstance(assessments, dict):
    99→        raise ValueError(
   100→            f"{mode_name} review import payload 'assessments' must be an object"
   101→        )
   102→    return ReviewImportEnvelope(
   103→        issues=issues_list,
   104→        assessments=assessments,
   105→        reviewed_files=extract_reviewed_files(data),
   106→    )
   107→
   108→
   109→def normalize_review_confidence(value: object) -> str:
   110→    """Normalize review confidence labels to high/medium/low."""
   111→    confidence = str(value).strip().lower()
   112→    return confidence if confidence in {"high", "medium", "low"} else "low"
   113→
   114→
   115→def review_tier(confidence: str, *, holistic: bool) -> int:
   116→    """Derive natural tier from review confidence and scope."""
   117→    if confidence == "high":
   118→        return 1 if holistic else 3
   119→    if confidence == "medium":
   120→        return 2 if holistic else 3
   121→    return 3
   122→
```

```
1→"""Validation and normalization helpers for review issue import entries."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from .contracts_types import (
     6→    REVIEW_ISSUE_REQUIRED_FIELDS,
     7→    VALID_REVIEW_CONFIDENCE,
     8→    ReviewIssuePayload,
     9→)
    10→
    11→
    12→def _normalized_non_empty_text(value: object) -> str | None:
    13→    if not isinstance(value, str):
    14→        return None
    15→    text = value.strip()
    16→    return text if text else None
    17→
    18→
    19→def _normalized_non_empty_text_list(value: object) -> list[str] | None:
    20→    if not isinstance(value, list):
    21→        return None
    22→    cleaned = [str(item).strip() for item in value if isinstance(item, str) and item.strip()]
    23→    return cleaned if cleaned else None
    24→
    25→
    26→def validate_review_issue_payload(
    27→    issue: object,
    28→    *,
    29→    label: str,
    30→    allowed_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    31→    allow_dismissed: bool = True,
    32→) -> tuple[ReviewIssuePayload | None, list[str]]:
    33→    """Validate and normalize one review issue payload entry."""
    34→    if not isinstance(issue, dict):
    35→        return None, [f"{label} must be an object"]
    36→
    37→    dismissed = issue.get("concern_verdict") == "dismissed"
    38→    if dismissed and not allow_dismissed:
    39→        return None, [f"{label}.concern_verdict='dismissed' is not allowed here"]
    40→
    41→    if dismissed:
    42→        fingerprint = _normalized_non_empty_text(issue.get("concern_fingerprint"))
    43→        if fingerprint is None:
    44→            return (
    45→                None,
    46→                [
    47→                    f"{label}.concern_fingerprint is required when concern_verdict='dismissed'"
    48→                ],
    49→            )
    50→        normalized: ReviewIssuePayload = {
    51→            "concern_verdict": "dismissed",
    52→            "concern_fingerprint": fingerprint,
    53→        }
    54→        concern_type = _normalized_non_empty_text(issue.get("concern_type"))
    55→        if concern_type is not None:
    56→            normalized["concern_type"] = concern_type
    57→        concern_file = _normalized_non_empty_text(issue.get("concern_file"))
    58→        if concern_file is not None:
    59→            normalized["concern_file"] = concern_file
    60→        reasoning = _normalized_non_empty_text(issue.get("reasoning"))
    61→        if reasoning is not None:
    62→            normalized["reasoning"] = reasoning
    63→        return normalized, []
    64→
    65→    errors: list[str] = []
    66→    missing = [field for field in REVIEW_ISSUE_REQUIRED_FIELDS if field not in issue]
    67→    if missing:
    68→        errors.append(f"{label} missing required fields: {', '.join(missing)}")
    69→        return None, errors
    70→
    71→    dimension = _normalized_non_empty_text(issue.get("dimension"))
    72→    if dimension is None:
    73→        errors.append(f"{label}.dimension must be a non-empty string")
    74→    elif allowed_dimensions is not None and dimension not in allowed_dimensions:
    75→        errors.append(f"{label}.dimension '{dimension}' is not allowed")
    76→
    77→    identifier = _normalized_non_empty_text(issue.get("identifier"))
    78→    if identifier is None:
    79→        errors.append(f"{label}.identifier must be a non-empty string")
    80→
    81→    summary = _normalized_non_empty_text(issue.get("summary"))
    82→    if summary is None:
    83→        errors.append(f"{label}.summary must be a non-empty string")
    84→
    85→    suggestion = _normalized_non_empty_text(issue.get("suggestion"))
    86→    if suggestion is None:
    87→        errors.append(f"{label}.suggestion must be a non-empty string")
    88→
    89→    confidence = _normalized_non_empty_text(issue.get("confidence"))
    90→    confidence_text = confidence.lower() if confidence is not None else ""
    91→    if confidence_text not in VALID_REVIEW_CONFIDENCE:
    92→        errors.append(f"{label}.confidence must be one of: high, medium, low")
    93→
    94→    related_files = _normalized_non_empty_text_list(issue.get("related_files"))
    95→    if related_files is None:
    96→        errors.append(f"{label}.related_files must contain at least one file path string")
    97→
    98→    evidence = _normalized_non_empty_text_list(issue.get("evidence"))
    99→    if evidence is None:
   100→        errors.append(f"{label}.evidence must contain at least one concrete evidence string")
   101→
   102→    if errors:
   103→        return None, errors
   104→
   105→    normalized_payload: ReviewIssuePayload = {
   106→        "dimension": dimension or "",
   107→        "identifier": identifier or "",
   108→        "summary": summary or "",
   109→        "confidence": confidence_text,
   110→        "suggestion": suggestion or "",
   111→        "related_files": related_files or [],
   112→        "evidence": evidence or [],
   113→    }
   114→    reasoning = _normalized_non_empty_text(issue.get("reasoning"))
   115→    if reasoning is not None:
   116→        normalized_payload["reasoning"] = reasoning
   117→    concern_type = _normalized_non_empty_text(issue.get("concern_type"))
   118→    if concern_type is not None:
   119→        normalized_payload["concern_type"] = concern_type
   120→    concern_file = _normalized_non_empty_text(issue.get("concern_file"))
   121→    if concern_file is not None:
   122→        normalized_payload["concern_file"] = concern_file
   123→    file_path = _normalized_non_empty_text(issue.get("file"))
   124→    if file_path is not None:
   125→        normalized_payload["file"] = file_path
   126→    evidence_lines = issue.get("evidence_lines")
   127→    if isinstance(evidence_lines, list):
   128→        normalized_lines = [line for line in evidence_lines if isinstance(line, int)]
   129→        if normalized_lines:
   130→            normalized_payload["evidence_lines"] = normalized_lines
   131→    return normalized_payload, []
   132→
   133→
   134→__all__ = ["validate_review_issue_payload"]
   135→
```

```
1→"""Shared subjective-review contract for prompts, normalization, and import validation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→LOW_SCORE_ISSUE_THRESHOLD = 85.0
     6→ASSESSMENT_FEEDBACK_THRESHOLD = 100.0
     7→HIGH_SCORE_ISSUES_NOTE_THRESHOLD = 85.0
     8→[REDACTED]
     9→LEGACY_DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY = "unreported_risk"
    10→[REDACTED]
    11→[REDACTED]
    12→DEFAULT_MAX_BATCH_ISSUES = 10
    13→_PROMPT_CONTRACT_HEADER = "GLOBAL REVIEW CONTRACT (applies to every dimension):"
    14→TRUSTED_IMPORT_COVERAGE_OVERRIDE_FLAG = "--allow-partial"
    15→
    16→
    17→def max_batch_issues_for_dimension_count(dimension_count: int) -> int:
    18→    """Return the normalized max issues budget for one batch payload."""
    19→    safe_count = max(0, int(dimension_count))
    20→    return max(DEFAULT_MAX_BATCH_ISSUES, safe_count)
    21→
    22→
    23→def score_requires_dimension_issue(score: float) -> bool:
    24→    """Return True when score requires at least one explicit issue."""
    25→    return float(score) < LOW_SCORE_ISSUE_THRESHOLD
    26→
    27→
    28→def score_requires_explicit_feedback(score: float) -> bool:
    29→    """Return True when score requires a issue or dimension-note evidence."""
    30→    return float(score) < ASSESSMENT_FEEDBACK_THRESHOLD
    31→
    32→
    33→def global_prompt_contract() -> str:
    34→    """Render shared review contract text injected into all review prompts."""
    35→    return (
    36→        f"{_PROMPT_CONTRACT_HEADER}\n"
    37→        "- Scope breadth: report any material issues supported by evidence "
    38→        "(structural, architectural, boundary, readability, lifecycle), "
    39→        "not only low-level nits.\n"
    40→        "- Dimension boundaries are guidance, not a gag-order: if an issue "
    41→        "spans dimensions, report it under the most impacted dimension.\n"
    42→        "- Do not default to 100. Reserve 100 for genuinely exemplary code "
    43→        "with clear positive evidence; if there is uncertainty or residual "
    44→        "issues, score below 100.\n"
    45→        "- Do not suppress valid issues to keep scores high.\n"
    46→        f"- Scores below {LOW_SCORE_ISSUE_THRESHOLD:.1f} MUST include at least one "
    47→        "issue for that same dimension.\n"
    48→        f"- Scores below {ASSESSMENT_FEEDBACK_THRESHOLD:.1f} MUST include explicit "
    49→        "feedback for that same dimension (issue with suggestion or "
    50→        "dimension_notes evidence).\n"
    51→        f"- Scores above {HIGH_SCORE_ISSUES_NOTE_THRESHOLD:.1f} MUST include a "
    52→        "non-empty `issues_preventing_higher_score` note for that dimension.\n"
    53→        "- Issues must always describe defects that need change, never positive observations.\n"
    54→        "- Think structurally: when individual issues form a pattern, consider what is\n"
    55→        "  causing them. If several issues stem from a shared root cause (missing abstraction,\n"
    56→        "  repeated pattern, inconsistent convention), say so in the issues — explain the\n"
    57→        "  deeper issue and use root_cause_cluster to connect related symptoms."
    58→    )
    59→
    60→
    61→def ensure_prompt_contract(system_prompt: str) -> str:
    62→    """Append shared contract text once to a system prompt."""
    63→    text = (system_prompt or "").strip()
    64→    if _PROMPT_CONTRACT_HEADER in text:
    65→        return text
    66→    suffix = global_prompt_contract()
    67→    if not text:
    68→        return suffix
    69→    return f"{text}\n\n{suffix}"
    70→
    71→
    72→__all__ = [
    73→    "ASSESSMENT_FEEDBACK_THRESHOLD",
    74→    "DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY",
    75→    "DEFAULT_MAX_BATCH_ISSUES",
    76→    "HIGH_SCORE_ISSUES_NOTE_THRESHOLD",
    77→    "LEGACY_DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY",
    78→    "LEGACY_REVIEW_QUALITY_HIGH_SCORE_MISSING_ISSUES_KEY",
    79→    "REVIEW_QUALITY_HIGH_SCORE_MISSING_ISSUES_KEY",
    80→    "TRUSTED_IMPORT_COVERAGE_OVERRIDE_FLAG",
    81→    "LOW_SCORE_ISSUE_THRESHOLD",
    82→    "ensure_prompt_contract",
    83→    "global_prompt_contract",
    84→    "max_batch_issues_for_dimension_count",
    85→    "score_requires_dimension_issue",
    86→    "score_requires_explicit_feedback",
    87→]
    88→
```

```
    55→    """Configuration bundle for review import/validate flows."""
    56→
    57→    config: dict | None = None
    58→    allow_partial: bool = False
    59→    trusted_assessment_source: bool = False
    60→    trusted_assessment_label: str | None = None
    61→    attested_external: bool = False
    62→    manual_override: bool = False
    63→    manual_attest: str | None = None
    64→
    65→
    66→def _build_import_load_config(
    67→    *,
    68→    lang_name: str | None,
    69→    import_config: ReviewImportConfig,
    70→    override_enabled: bool,
    71→    override_attest: str | None,
    72→) -> import_helpers_mod.ImportLoadConfig:
    73→    return import_helpers_mod.ImportLoadConfig(
    74→        lang_name=lang_name,
    75→        allow_partial=import_config.allow_partial,
    76→        trusted_assessment_source=import_config.trusted_assessment_source,
    77→        trusted_assessment_label=import_config.trusted_assessment_label,
    78→        attested_external=import_config.attested_external,
    79→        manual_override=override_enabled,
    80→        manual_attest=override_attest,
    81→    )
    82→
    83→
    84→def _validate_import_flag_combos(
    85→    *,
    86→    attested_external: bool,
    87→    allow_partial: bool,
    88→    override_enabled: bool,
    89→    override_attest: str | None,
    90→) -> None:
    91→    """Fail fast on conflicting import flags to keep behavior explicit."""
    92→    if attested_external and override_enabled:
    93→        raise ImportFlagValidationError(
    94→            "--attested-external cannot be combined with --manual-override"
    95→        )
    96→    if attested_external and allow_partial:
    97→        raise ImportFlagValidationError(
    98→            "--attested-external cannot be combined with --allow-partial"
    99→        )
   100→    if override_enabled and allow_partial:
   101→        raise ImportFlagValidationError(
   102→            "--manual-override cannot be combined with --allow-partial"
   103→        )
   104→    if override_enabled and (
   105→        not isinstance(override_attest, str) or not override_attest.strip()
   106→    ):
   107→        raise ImportFlagValidationError("--manual-override requires --attest")
   108→
   109→
   110→def _imported_assessment_keys(issues_data: ReviewImportPayload) -> set[str]:
   111→    """Return normalized assessment dimension keys from payload."""
   112→    raw_assessments = issues_data["assessments"]
   113→    keys: set[str] = set()
   114→    for raw_key in raw_assessments:
   115→        normalized = normalize_dimension_name(str(raw_key))
   116→        if normalized:
   117→            keys.add(normalized)
   118→    return keys
   119→
   120→
   121→def _mark_manual_override_assessments_provisional(
   122→    state: dict,
   123→    *,
   124→    assessment_keys: set[str],
   125→) -> int:
   126→    """Mark imported manual override assessments as provisional until next scan."""
   127→    if not assessment_keys:
   128→        return 0
   129→    store = state.get("subjective_assessments")
   130→    if not isinstance(store, dict):
   131→        return 0
   132→
   133→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   134→    expires_scan = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0) + 1
   135→    marked = 0
   136→    for key in sorted(assessment_keys):
   137→        payload = store.get(key)
   138→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   139→            continue
   140→        payload["source"] = "manual_override"
   141→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   142→        payload["provisional_override"] = True
   143→        payload["provisional_until_scan"] = expires_scan
   144→        payload.pop("placeholder", None)
   145→        marked += 1
   146→    return marked
   147→
   148→
   149→def _clear_provisional_override_flags(
   150→    state: dict,
   151→    *,
   152→    assessment_keys: set[str],
   153→) -> int:
   154→    """Clear provisional override flags when trusted internal assessments replace them."""
   155→    if not assessment_keys:
   156→        return 0
   157→    store = state.get("subjective_assessments")
   158→    if not isinstance(store, dict):
   159→        return 0
   160→
   161→    cleared = 0
   162→    for key in sorted(assessment_keys):
   163→        payload = store.get(key)
   164→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   165→            continue
   166→        if payload.pop("provisional_override", None) is not None:
   167→            cleared += 1
   168→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   169→        if payload.get("source") == "manual_override":
   170→            payload["source"] = "holistic"
   171→    return cleared
   172→
   173→
   174→
   175→def _print_review_import_sync(state: dict, result: ReviewImportSyncResult) -> None:
   176→    """Print summary of plan changes after review import sync."""
   177→    new_ids = result.new_ids
   178→    print(colorize(
   179→        f"\n  Plan updated: {len(new_ids)} new review issue(s) added to queue.",
   180→        "bold",
   181→    ))
   182→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   183→    for fid in sorted(new_ids)[:10]:
   184→        f = issues.get(fid, {})
   185→        print(f"    * [{short_issue_id(fid)}] {f.get('summary', '')}")
   186→    if len(new_ids) > 10:
   187→        print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(new_ids) - 10} more", "dim"))
   188→    print()
   189→    print(colorize("  New items added to end of queue.", "dim"))
   190→    print()
   191→    print(colorize("  View queue:            desloppify plan queue", "dim"))
   192→    print(colorize("  View newest first:     desloppify plan queue --sort recent", "dim"))
   193→    print()
   194→    print(colorize("  NEXT STEP:  desloppify plan triage", "yellow"))
   195→    print(colorize(
   196→        "  (Review new issues and decide whether to re-plan or accept current queue.)",
   197→        "dim",
   198→    ))
   199→
   200→
   201→def _sync_plan_after_import(state: dict, diff: dict, assessment_mode: str) -> None:
   202→    """All post-import plan syncs. Load once, save once.
   203→
   204→    Phase 1 (issue sync): Only when new/reopened issues arrived — adds them
   205→    to queue, auto-resolves covered subjective items.
   206→    Phase 2 (workflow items): Always — injects score-checkpoint, create-plan,
   207→    import-scores, and communicate-score workflow items as needed.
   208→    """
   209→    try:
   210→        from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   211→            sync_communicate_score_needed,
   212→            sync_import_scores_needed,
   213→        )
   214→        from desloppify.engine.plan import (
   215→            append_log_entry,
   216→            current_unscored_ids,
   217→            has_living_plan,
   218→            load_plan,
   219→            purge_ids,
   220→            save_plan,
   221→            sync_create_plan_needed,
   222→            sync_plan_after_review_import,
   223→            sync_score_checkpoint_needed,
   224→        )
   225→
   226→        if not has_living_plan():
   227→            return
   228→
   229→        plan = load_plan()
   230→        dirty = False
   231→
   232→        # Phase 1: Issue sync (only when new/reopened issues arrived)
   233→        has_new_issues = (
   234→            int(diff.get("new", 0) or 0) > 0
   235→            or int(diff.get("reopened", 0) or 0) > 0
   236→        )
   237→        import_result = None
   238→        covered_ids: list[str] = []
   239→        if has_new_issues:
   240→            import_result = sync_plan_after_review_import(plan, state)
   241→            if import_result is not None:
   242→                dirty = True
   243→
   244→            # Auto-resolve subjective dimension items that are no longer unscored
   245→            still_unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   246→            order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   247→            covered_ids = [
   248→                fid for fid in order
   249→                if fid.startswith("subjective::") and fid not in still_unscored
   250→            ]
   251→            if covered_ids:
   252→                purge_ids(plan, covered_ids)
   253→                dirty = True
   254→
   255→        # Phase 2: Workflow items (always)
   256→        injected_parts: list[str] = []
   257→
   258→        checkpoint_result = sync_score_checkpoint_needed(plan, state)
   259→        if checkpoint_result.changes:
   260→            dirty = True
   261→            injected_parts.append("`workflow::score-checkpoint`")
   262→
   263→        scores_imported = assessment_mode in (
   264→            "trusted_internal", "attested_external", "manual_override",
   265→        )
   266→        import_scores_result = sync_import_scores_needed(
   267→            plan, state, assessment_mode=assessment_mode,
   268→        )
   269→        if import_scores_result.changes:
   270→            dirty = True
   271→            injected_parts.append("`workflow::import-scores`")
   272→
   273→        communicate_result = sync_communicate_score_needed(
   274→            plan, state, scores_just_imported=scores_imported,
   275→        )
   276→        if communicate_result.changes:
   277→            dirty = True
   278→            injected_parts.append("`workflow::communicate-score`")
   279→
   280→        create_plan_result = sync_create_plan_needed(plan, state)
   281→        if create_plan_result.changes:
   282→            dirty = True
   283→            injected_parts.append("`workflow::create-plan`")
   284→
   285→        # Save once
   286→        if dirty:
   287→            if import_result is not None:
   288→                append_log_entry(
   289→                    plan,
   290→                    "review_import_sync",
   291→                    actor="system",
   292→                    detail={
   293→                        "trigger": "review_import",
   294→                        "new_ids": sorted(import_result.new_ids),
   295→                        "added_to_queue": import_result.added_to_queue,
   296→                        "diff_new": diff.get("new", 0),
   297→                        "diff_reopened": diff.get("reopened", 0),
   298→                        "covered_subjective": covered_ids,
   299→                    },
   300→                )
   301→            save_plan(plan)
   302→
   303→        # Print results
   304→        if import_result is not None:
   305→            _print_review_import_sync(state, import_result)
   306→        if injected_parts:
   307→            print(colorize(
   308→                f"  Plan: {' and '.join(injected_parts)} queued. Run `desloppify next`.",
   309→                "cyan",
   310→            ))
   311→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   312→        print(
   313→            colorize(
   314→                f"  Note: skipped plan sync after review import ({exc}).",
   315→                "dim",
   316→            )
   317→        )
   318→
   319→
   320→def do_import(
   321→    import_file,
   322→    state,
   323→    lang,
   324→    state_file,
   325→    *,
   326→    config: dict | None = None,
   327→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   328→    trusted_assessment_source: bool = False,
   329→    trusted_assessment_label: str | None = None,
   330→    attested_external: bool = False,
   331→    manual_override: bool = False,
   332→    manual_attest: str | None = None,
   333→) -> None:
   334→    """Import mode: ingest agent-produced issues."""
   335→    import_config = ReviewImportConfig(
   336→        config=config,
   337→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   338→        trusted_assessment_source=trusted_assessment_source,
   339→        trusted_assessment_label=trusted_assessment_label,
   340→        attested_external=attested_external,
   341→        manual_override=manual_override,
   342→        manual_attest=manual_attest,
   343→    )
   344→    override_enabled, override_attest = import_helpers_mod.resolve_override_context(
   345→        manual_override=import_config.manual_override,
   346→        manual_attest=import_config.manual_attest,
   347→    )
   348→    try:
   349→        _validate_import_flag_combos(
   350→            attested_external=import_config.attested_external,
   351→            allow_partial=import_config.allow_partial,
   352→            override_enabled=override_enabled,
   353→            override_attest=override_attest,
   354→        )
   355→    except ImportFlagValidationError as exc:
   356→        raise CommandError(str(exc), exit_code=1) from exc
   357→
   358→    try:
   359→        issues_data = import_helpers_mod.load_import_issues_data(
   360→            import_file,
   361→            config=_build_import_load_config(
   362→                lang_name=lang.name,
   363→                import_config=import_config,
   364→                override_enabled=override_enabled,
   365→                override_attest=override_attest,
   366→            ),
   367→        )
   368→    except import_helpers_mod.ImportPayloadLoadError as exc:
   369→        import_helpers_mod.print_import_load_errors(
   370→            exc.errors,
   371→            import_file=str(import_file),
   372→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   373→        )
   374→        raise PacketValidationError("import payload validation failed", exit_code=1) from exc
   375→    assessment_policy: AssessmentImportPolicyModel = (
   376→        import_helpers_mod.assessment_policy_model_from_payload(issues_data)
   377→    )
   378→    import_helpers_mod.print_assessment_mode_banner(
   379→        assessment_policy.to_dict(),
   380→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   381→    )
   382→    import_helpers_mod.print_assessment_policy_notice(
   383→        assessment_policy.to_dict(),
   384→        import_file=str(import_file),
   385→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   386→    )
   387→
   388→    prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   389→
   390→    # Transactional import: only persist if all post-import guards pass.
   391→    # Rebase on the latest on-disk state when available so long-running review
   392→    # sessions don't clobber newer imports/scans that completed while batches ran.
   393→    state_path = Path(state_file) if state_file is not None else None
   394→    if state_path is not None and state_path.exists():
   395→        working_state = copy.deepcopy(state_mod.load_state(state_path))
   396→    else:
   397→        working_state = copy.deepcopy(state)
   398→    diff = review_mod.import_holistic_issues(issues_data, working_state, lang.name)
   399→    label = "Holistic review"
   400→    imported_assessment_keys = _imported_assessment_keys(issues_data)
   401→    provisional_count = 0
   402→    if assessment_policy.mode == "manual_override":
   403→        provisional_count = _mark_manual_override_assessments_provisional(
   404→            working_state,
   405→            assessment_keys=imported_assessment_keys,
   406→        )
   407→    elif assessment_policy.mode in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}:
   408→        _clear_provisional_override_flags(
   409→            working_state,
   410→            assessment_keys=imported_assessment_keys,
   411→        )
   412→
   413→    if diff.get("skipped", 0) > 0 and not import_config.allow_partial:
   414→        details_lines: list[str] = []
   415→        for detail in diff.get("skipped_details", []):
   416→            reasons = "; ".join(detail.get("missing", []))
   417→            details_lines.append(
   418→                f"  #{detail.get('index', '?')} ({detail.get('identifier', '<none>')}): {reasons}"
   419→            )
   420→        msg = "import produced skipped issue(s); refusing partial import."
   421→        if details_lines:
   422→            msg += "\n" + "\n".join(details_lines)
   423→        msg += "\nFix the payload and retry, or pass --allow-partial to override."
   424→        raise CommandError(msg, exit_code=1)
   425→
   426→    if assessment_policy.assessments_present:
   427→        audit = working_state.setdefault("assessment_import_audit", [])
   428→        audit.append(
   429→            {
   430→                "timestamp": state_mod.utc_now(),
   431→                "mode": assessment_policy.mode,
   432→                "trusted": bool(assessment_policy.trusted),
   433→                "reason": assessment_policy.reason,
   434→                "override_used": bool(assessment_policy.mode == "manual_override"),
   435→                "attested_external": bool(assessment_policy.mode == "attested_external"),
   436→                "provisional": bool(assessment_policy.mode == "manual_override"),
   437→                "provisional_count": int(provisional_count),
   438→                "attest": (override_attest or "").strip(),
   439→                "import_file": str(import_file),
   440→            }
   441→        )
   442→    state.clear()
   443→    state.update(working_state)
   444→    state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
   445→
   446→    # Sync plan: issue sync (if new issues) + workflow items (always).
   447→    _sync_plan_after_import(state, diff, assessment_policy.mode)
   448→
   449→    _print_import_results(
   450→        state=state,
   451→        lang_name=lang.name,
   452→        config=import_config.config,
   453→        diff=diff,
   454→        prev=prev,
   455→        label=label,
   456→        provisional_count=provisional_count,
   457→        assessment_policy=assessment_policy,
   458→    )
   459→
   460→
   461→def _print_import_results(
   462→    *,
   463→    state: dict,
   464→    lang_name: str,
   465→    config: dict | None,
   466→    diff: dict,
   467→    prev: dict,
   468→    label: str,
   469→    provisional_count: int,
   470→    assessment_policy,
   471→) -> None:
   472→    """Print import results, scores, and write query.json."""
   473→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   474→        state, NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="review")
   475→    )
   476→
   477→    print(colorize(f"\n  {label} imported:", "bold"))
   478→    issue_count = int(diff.get("new", 0) or 0)
   479→    print(
   480→        colorize(
   481→            f"  +{issue_count} new issue{'s' if issue_count != 1 else ''} "
   482→            f"(review issues), "
   483→            f"{diff['auto_resolved']} resolved, "
   484→            f"{diff['reopened']} reopened",
   485→            "dim",
   486→        )
   487→    )
   488→    if provisional_count > 0:
   489→        print(
   490→            colorize(
   491→                "  WARNING: manual override assessments are provisional and will "
   492→                "reset on the next scan unless replaced by "
   493→                "`review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import`.",
   494→                "yellow",
   495→            )
   496→        )
   497→    import_helpers_mod.print_skipped_validation_details(diff, colorize_fn=colorize)
   498→    import_helpers_mod.print_assessments_summary(state, colorize_fn=colorize)
   499→    next_command = import_helpers_mod.print_open_review_summary(
   500→        state, colorize_fn=colorize
   501→    )
   502→    show_score_with_plan_context(state, prev)
   503→    at_target = import_helpers_mod.print_review_import_scores_and_integrity(
   504→        state,
   505→        config or {},
   506→        state_mod=state_mod,
   507→        target_strict_score_from_config_fn=target_strict_score_from_config,
   508→        subjective_at_target_fn=_SCORECARD_SUBJECTIVE_AT_TARGET,
   509→        subjective_rerun_command_fn=reporting_dimensions_mod.subjective_rerun_command,
   510→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   511→    )
   512→
   513→    print(
   514→        colorize(
   515→            f"  Next command to improve subjective scores: `{next_command}`", "dim"
   516→        )
   517→    )
   518→    write_query(
   519→        {
   520→            "command": "review",
   521→            "action": "import",
   522→            "mode": "holistic",
   523→            "diff": diff,
   524→            "next_command": next_command,
   525→            "subjective_at_target": [
   526→                {"dimension": entry["name"], "score": entry["score"]}
   527→                for entry in at_target
   528→            ],
   529→            "assessment_import": {
   530→                "mode": assessment_policy.mode,
   531→                "trusted": bool(assessment_policy.trusted),
   532→                "reason": assessment_policy.reason,
   533→            },
   534→            "narrative": narrative,
   535→        }
   536→    )
   537→
   538→
   539→def do_validate_import(
   540→    import_file,
   541→    lang,
   542→    *,
   543→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   544→    attested_external: bool = False,
   545→    manual_override: bool = False,
   546→    manual_attest: str | None = None,
   547→) -> None:
   548→    """Validate import payload/policy and print mode without mutating state."""
   549→    import_config = ReviewImportConfig(
   550→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   551→        attested_external=attested_external,
   552→        manual_override=manual_override,
   553→        manual_attest=manual_attest,
   554→    )
   555→    override_enabled, override_attest = import_helpers_mod.resolve_override_context(
   556→        manual_override=import_config.manual_override,
   557→        manual_attest=import_config.manual_attest,
   558→    )
   559→    try:
   560→        _validate_import_flag_combos(
   561→            attested_external=import_config.attested_external,
   562→            allow_partial=import_config.allow_partial,
   563→            override_enabled=override_enabled,
   564→            override_attest=override_attest,
   565→        )
   566→    except ImportFlagValidationError as exc:
   567→        raise CommandError(str(exc), exit_code=1) from exc
   568→
   569→    try:
   570→        issues_data = import_helpers_mod.load_import_issues_data(
   571→            import_file,
   572→            config=_build_import_load_config(
   573→                lang_name=lang.name,
   574→                import_config=import_config,
   575→                override_enabled=override_enabled,
   576→                override_attest=override_attest,
   577→            ),
   578→        )
   579→    except import_helpers_mod.ImportPayloadLoadError as exc:
   580→        import_helpers_mod.print_import_load_errors(
   581→            exc.errors,
   582→            import_file=str(import_file),
   583→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   584→        )
   585→        raise PacketValidationError("import payload validation failed", exit_code=1) from exc
   586→    assessment_policy = import_helpers_mod.assessment_policy_model_from_payload(
   587→        issues_data
   588→    )
   589→    import_helpers_mod.print_assessment_mode_banner(
   590→        assessment_policy.to_dict(),
   591→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   592→    )
   593→    import_helpers_mod.print_assessment_policy_notice(
   594→        assessment_policy.to_dict(),
   595→        import_file=str(import_file),
   596→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   597→    )
   598→
   599→    issues_count = len(issues_data["issues"])
   600→    print(colorize("\n  Import payload validation passed.", "bold"))
   601→    print(colorize(f"  Issues parsed: {issues_count}", "dim"))
   602→    if assessment_policy.assessments_present:
   603→        count = int(assessment_policy.assessment_count)
   604→        print(colorize(f"  Assessment entries in payload: {count}", "dim"))
   605→    print(colorize("  No state changes were made (--validate-import).", "dim"))
   606→
   607→
   608→__all__ = [
   609→    "ImportFlagValidationError",
   610→    "ReviewImportConfig",
   611→    "do_import",
   612→    "do_validate_import",
   613→    "subjective_at_target_dimensions",
   614→]
   615→
```

```
1→"""Payload parsing and validation helpers for review imports."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
```
