```
    34→
    35→class ImportRootPayload(TypedDict, total=False):
    36→    """Top-level import payload shape prior to strict normalization."""
    37→
    38→    issues: list[object]
    39→    findings: list[object]
    40→    assessments: dict[str, Any]
    41→    reviewed_files: list[object]
    42→    review_scope: dict[str, Any]
    43→    provenance: dict[str, Any]
    44→    dimension_notes: dict[str, Any]
    45→    _assessment_policy: dict[str, Any]
    46→
    47→
    48→class ImportPayloadLoadError(ValueError):
    49→    """Raised when review import payload parsing/validation fails."""
    50→
    51→    def __init__(self, errors: list[str]) -> None:
    52→        cleaned = [str(error).strip() for error in errors if str(error).strip()]
    53→        self.errors = cleaned
    54→        message = "; ".join(cleaned) if cleaned else "import payload validation failed"
    55→        super().__init__(message)
    56→
    57→
    58→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    59→class ImportParseOptions:
    60→    """Import parse policy/options bundle."""
    61→
    62→    lang_name: str | None = None
    63→    allow_partial: bool = False
    64→    trusted_assessment_source: bool = False
    65→    trusted_assessment_label: str | None = None
    66→    attested_external: bool = False
    67→    manual_override: bool = False
    68→    manual_attest: str | None = None
    69→
    70→
    71→def _coerce_import_parse_options(
    72→    options: ImportParseOptions | None = None,
    73→) -> ImportParseOptions:
    74→    """Resolve import-parse options from the typed dataclass contract."""
    75→    base = options or ImportParseOptions()
    76→    return ImportParseOptions(
    77→        lang_name=coerce_optional_str(base.lang_name),
    78→        allow_partial=bool(base.allow_partial),
    79→        trusted_assessment_source=bool(base.trusted_assessment_source),
    80→        trusted_assessment_label=coerce_optional_str(base.trusted_assessment_label),
    81→        attested_external=bool(base.attested_external),
    82→        manual_override=bool(base.manual_override),
    83→        manual_attest=coerce_optional_str(base.manual_attest),
    84→    )
    85→
    86→
    87→def _normalize_import_payload_shape(
    88→    payload: ImportRootPayload,
    89→) -> tuple[ReviewImportPayload | None, list[str]]:
    90→    """Normalize payload into required-key contract with strict type checks."""
    91→    errors: list[str] = []
    92→    issues = payload.get("issues")
    93→    if not isinstance(issues, list):
    94→        errors.append("issues must be a JSON array")
    95→        issues = []
    96→
    97→    assessments = _coerce_optional_object(payload, key="assessments", errors=errors)
    98→    normalized_reviewed_files = _coerce_reviewed_files(payload, errors=errors)
    99→    review_scope = _coerce_optional_object(payload, key="review_scope", errors=errors)
   100→    provenance = _coerce_optional_object(payload, key="provenance", errors=errors)
   101→    dimension_notes = _coerce_optional_object(payload, key="dimension_notes", errors=errors)
   102→
   103→    policy = payload.get(ASSESSMENT_POLICY_KEY)
   104→    normalized_policy = (
   105→        policy if isinstance(policy, dict) else AssessmentImportPolicyModel().to_dict()
   106→    )
   107→    if errors:
   108→        return None, errors
   109→    return (
   110→        {
   111→            "issues": issues,
   112→            "assessments": assessments,
   113→            "reviewed_files": normalized_reviewed_files,
   114→            "review_scope": review_scope,
   115→            "provenance": provenance,
   116→            "dimension_notes": dimension_notes,
   117→            [REDACTED],
   118→        },
   119→        [],
   120→    )
   121→
   122→
   123→def _coerce_optional_object(
   124→    payload: ImportRootPayload,
   125→    *,
   126→    key: str,
   127→    errors: list[str],
   128→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   129→    """Normalize optional object payload fields to dictionaries."""
   130→    value = payload.get(key)
   131→    if value is None:
   132→        return {}
   133→    if isinstance(value, dict):
   134→        return value
   135→    errors.append(f"{key} must be an object when provided")
   136→    return {}
   137→
   138→
   139→def _coerce_reviewed_files(
   140→    payload: ImportRootPayload, *, errors: list[str]
   141→) -> list[str]:
   142→    """Normalize reviewed_files to trimmed string list."""
   143→    reviewed_files = payload.get("reviewed_files")
   144→    if reviewed_files is None:
   145→        return []
   146→    if isinstance(reviewed_files, list):
   147→        return [
   148→            str(item).strip()
   149→            for item in reviewed_files
   150→            if isinstance(item, str) and str(item).strip()
   151→        ]
   152→    errors.append("reviewed_files must be an array when provided")
   153→    return []
   154→
   155→
   156→def resolve_override_context(
   157→    *,
   158→    manual_override: bool,
   159→    manual_attest: str | None,
   160→) -> tuple[bool, str | None]:
   161→    """Normalize manual override settings into one explicit decision."""
   162→    override = bool(manual_override)
   163→    attest = manual_attest
   164→    if isinstance(attest, str):
   165→        attest = attest.strip()
   166→    return override, attest
   167→
   168→
   169→def _has_non_empty_strings(items: object) -> bool:
   170→    """Return True when ``items`` is a list with at least one non-empty string."""
   171→    return isinstance(items, list) and any(
   172→        isinstance(item, str) and item.strip() for item in items
   173→    )
   174→
   175→
   176→def _validate_holistic_issues_schema(
   177→    issues_data: ReviewImportPayload,
   178→    *,
   179→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   180→) -> list[str]:
   181→    """Validate strict holistic issue schema expected by issue import."""
   182→    issues = issues_data["issues"]
   183→
   184→    allowed_dimensions: set[str] = set()
   185→    if isinstance(lang_name, str) and lang_name.strip():
   186→        _, dimension_prompts, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name)
   187→        allowed_dimensions = set(dimension_prompts)
   188→
   189→    errors: list[str] = []
   190→    for idx, entry in enumerate(issues):
   191→        _normalized: ReviewIssuePayload | None
   192→        _normalized, entry_errors = validate_review_issue_payload(
   193→            entry,
   194→            label=f"issues[{idx}]",
   195→            allowed_dimensions=allowed_dimensions or None,
   196→            allow_dismissed=True,
   197→        )
   198→        for message in entry_errors:
   199→            if (
   200→                "is not allowed" in message
   201→                and lang_name
   202→                and "dimension '" in message
   203→            ):
   204→                message = message.replace(
   205→                    "is not allowed",
   206→                    f"is not valid for language '{lang_name}'",
   207→                )
   208→            errors.append(message)
   209→    return errors
   210→
   211→
   212→def _feedback_dimensions_from_issues(issues: object) -> set[str]:
   213→    """Return dimensions with explicit improvement guidance in issues payload."""
   214→    if not isinstance(issues, list):
   215→        return set()
   216→    dims: set[str] = set()
   217→    for entry in issues:
   218→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   219→            continue
   220→        dim = entry.get("dimension")
   221→        if not isinstance(dim, str) or not dim.strip():
   222→            continue
   223→        suggestion = entry.get("suggestion")
   224→        if isinstance(suggestion, str) and suggestion.strip():
   225→            dims.add(dim.strip())
   226→    return dims
   227→
   228→
   229→def _feedback_dimensions_from_dimension_notes(dimension_notes: object) -> set[str]:
   230→    """Return dimensions with concrete review evidence in dimension_notes payload."""
   231→    if not isinstance(dimension_notes, dict):
   232→        return set()
   233→    dims: set[str] = set()
   234→    for dim, note in dimension_notes.items():
   235→        if not isinstance(dim, str) or not dim.strip():
   236→            continue
   237→        if not isinstance(note, dict):
   238→            continue
   239→        if not _has_non_empty_strings(note.get("evidence")):
   240→            continue
   241→        dims.add(dim.strip())
   242→    return dims
   243→
   244→
   245→def _validate_assessment_feedback(
   246→    issues_data: ReviewImportPayload,
   247→) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
   248→    """Return dimensions missing required feedback and required low-score issues."""
   249→    assessments = issues_data["assessments"]
   250→    if not assessments:
   251→        return [], []
   252→
   253→    issue_dims = _feedback_dimensions_from_issues(issues_data["issues"])
   254→    feedback_dims = set(issue_dims)
   255→    feedback_dims.update(
   256→        _feedback_dimensions_from_dimension_notes(issues_data["dimension_notes"])
   257→    )
   258→    missing_feedback: list[str] = []
   259→    missing_low_score_issues: list[str] = []
   260→    for dim_name, payload in assessments.items():
   261→        if not isinstance(dim_name, str) or not dim_name.strip():
   262→            continue
   263→        score = coerce_assessment_score(payload)
   264→        if score is None:
   265→            continue
   266→        if score_requires_dimension_issue(score) and dim_name not in issue_dims:
   267→            missing_low_score_issues.append(f"{dim_name} ({score:.1f})")
   268→        if score_requires_explicit_feedback(score) and dim_name not in feedback_dims:
   269→            missing_feedback.append(f"{dim_name} ({score:.1f})")
   270→    return sorted(missing_feedback), sorted(missing_low_score_issues)
   271→
   272→
   273→def _load_import_json(import_file: str) -> tuple[object | None, list[str]]:
   274→    """Read import file and parse JSON payload."""
   275→    issues_path = Path(import_file)
   276→    if not issues_path.exists():
   277→        return None, [f"file not found: {import_file}"]
   278→    try:
   279→        return json.loads(issues_path.read_text()), []
   280→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError) as exc:
   281→        return None, [f"error reading issues: {exc}"]
   282→
   283→
   284→def _normalize_import_root_payload(
   285→    raw_payload: object,
   286→) -> tuple[ImportRootPayload | None, list[str]]:
   287→    """Normalize top-level payload shape before strict field validation."""
   288→    payload = {"issues": raw_payload} if isinstance(raw_payload, list) else raw_payload
   289→    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   290→        return None, ["issues file must contain a JSON array or object"]
   291→
   292→    key_error = normalize_legacy_findings_alias(
   293→        payload,
   294→        missing_issues_error="issues object must contain an 'issues' key",
   295→    )
   296→    if key_error is not None:
   297→        return None, [key_error]
   298→    return payload, []
   299→
   300→
   301→def _validate_override_option_conflicts(
   302→    options: ImportParseOptions,
   303→    *,
   304→    override_enabled: bool,
   305→) -> list[str]:
   306→    """Validate mutually exclusive override/attestation option combinations."""
   307→    if options.attested_external and override_enabled:
   308→        return ["--attested-external cannot be combined with --manual-override"]
   309→    if options.attested_external and options.allow_partial:
   310→        return [
   311→            "--attested-external cannot be combined with --allow-partial; "
   312→            "attested score imports require fully valid issues payloads"
   313→        ]
   314→    if override_enabled and options.allow_partial:
   315→        return [
   316→            "--manual-override cannot be combined with --allow-partial; "
   317→            "manual score imports require fully valid issues payloads"
   318→        ]
   319→    return []
   320→
   321→
   322→def _validate_feedback_requirements(
   323→    issues_data: ReviewImportPayload,
   324→    *,
   325→    override_enabled: bool,
   326→    override_attest: str | None,
   327→) -> list[str]:
   328→    """Validate feedback and low-score issue requirements."""
   329→    missing_feedback, missing_low_score_issues = _validate_assessment_feedback(issues_data)
   330→    if missing_low_score_issues:
   331→        if override_enabled:
   332→            if not isinstance(override_attest, str) or not override_attest.strip():
   333→                return ["--manual-override requires --attest"]
   334→            return []
   335→        return [
   336→            f"assessments below {LOW_SCORE_ISSUE_THRESHOLD:.1f} must include at "
   337→            "least one issue for that same dimension with a concrete suggestion. "
   338→            f"Missing: {', '.join(missing_low_score_issues)}"
   339→        ]
   340→    if not missing_feedback:
   341→        return []
   342→    if override_enabled:
   343→        if not isinstance(override_attest, str) or not override_attest.strip():
   344→            return ["--manual-override requires --attest"]
   345→        return []
   346→    return [
   347→        f"assessments below {ASSESSMENT_FEEDBACK_THRESHOLD:.1f} must include explicit feedback "
   348→        "(issue with same dimension and non-empty suggestion, or "
   349→        "dimension_notes evidence for that dimension). "
   350→        f"Missing: {', '.join(missing_feedback)}"
   351→    ]
   352→
   353→
   354→def _validate_schema_requirements(
   355→    issues_data: ReviewImportPayload,
   356→    *,
   357→    lang_name: str | None,
   358→    allow_partial: bool,
   359→) -> list[str]:
   360→    """Validate holistic issue schema unless partial imports are enabled."""
   361→    schema_errors = _validate_holistic_issues_schema(issues_data, lang_name=lang_name)
   362→    if not schema_errors or allow_partial:
   363→        return []
   364→    visible_errors = schema_errors[:10]
   365→    remaining = len(schema_errors) - len(visible_errors)
   366→    errors = [
   367→        "issues schema validation failed for holistic import. "
   368→        "Fix payload or rerun with --allow-partial to continue."
   369→    ]
   370→    errors.extend(visible_errors)
   371→    if remaining > 0:
   372→        errors.append(f"... {remaining} additional schema error(s) omitted")
   373→    return errors
   374→
   375→
   376→def _parse_and_validate_import(
   377→    import_file: str,
   378→    *,
   379→    options: ImportParseOptions | None = None,
   380→) -> tuple[ReviewImportPayload | None, list[str]]:
   381→    """Parse and validate a review import file (pure function)."""
   382→    resolved_options = _coerce_import_parse_options(options)
   383→
   384→    raw_payload, load_errors = _load_import_json(import_file)
   385→    if load_errors:
   386→        return None, load_errors
   387→    normalized_root, root_errors = _normalize_import_root_payload(raw_payload)
   388→    if root_errors:
   389→        return None, root_errors
   390→    if normalized_root is None:
   391→        return None, ["issues payload root normalization returned no data"]
   392→
   393→    normalized_issues_data, shape_errors = _normalize_import_payload_shape(normalized_root)
   394→    if shape_errors:
   395→        return None, shape_errors
   396→    if normalized_issues_data is None:
   397→        return None, ["issues payload normalization returned no data"]
   398→
   399→    override_enabled, override_attest = resolve_override_context(
   400→        manual_override=resolved_options.manual_override,
   401→        manual_attest=resolved_options.manual_attest,
   402→    )
   403→    conflict_errors = _validate_override_option_conflicts(
   404→        resolved_options,
   405→        override_enabled=override_enabled,
   406→    )
   407→    if conflict_errors:
   408→        return None, conflict_errors
   409→
   410→    issues_data, policy_errors = apply_assessment_import_policy(
   411→        normalized_issues_data,
   412→        import_file=import_file,
   413→        attested_external=resolved_options.attested_external,
   414→        attested_attest=override_attest,
   415→        manual_override=override_enabled,
   416→        manual_attest=override_attest,
   417→        trusted_assessment_source=resolved_options.trusted_assessment_source,
   418→        trusted_assessment_label=resolved_options.trusted_assessment_label,
   419→    )
   420→    if policy_errors:
   421→        return None, policy_errors
   422→    if issues_data is None:
   423→        return None, ["assessment import policy returned no payload"]
   424→
   425→    feedback_errors = _validate_feedback_requirements(
   426→        issues_data,
   427→        override_enabled=override_enabled,
   428→        override_attest=override_attest,
   429→    )
   430→    if feedback_errors:
   431→        return None, feedback_errors
   432→
   433→    schema_errors = _validate_schema_requirements(
   434→        issues_data,
   435→        lang_name=resolved_options.lang_name,
   436→        allow_partial=resolved_options.allow_partial,
   437→    )
   438→    if schema_errors:
   439→        return None, schema_errors
   440→
   441→    return issues_data, []
   442→
   443→
   444→def load_import_issues_data(
   445→    import_file: str,
   446→    *,
   447→    colorize_fn=None,
   448→    options: ImportParseOptions | None = None,
   449→) -> ReviewImportPayload:
   450→    """Load and normalize review import payload to object format.
   451→
   452→    Raises ``ImportPayloadLoadError`` when validation fails.
   453→    """
   454→    resolved_options = _coerce_import_parse_options(options)
   455→    data, errors = _parse_and_validate_import(
   456→        import_file,
   457→        options=resolved_options,
   458→    )
   459→    if errors:
   460→        raise ImportPayloadLoadError(errors)
   461→    if data is None:
   462→        raise ImportPayloadLoadError(["import payload is empty after validation"])
   463→    return data
   464→
```

```
    46→def import_holistic_issues(

   110→                "dismissed_at": now,
   111→                "reasoning": dismissal.get("reasoning", ""),
   112→                "concern_type": dismissal.get("concern_type", ""),
   113→                "concern_file": dismissal.get("concern_file", ""),
   114→                "source_issue_ids": concern_sources.get(fingerprint, []),
   115→            }
   116→
   117→    potentials = _lang_potentials(state, lang_name)
   118→    existing_review = potentials.get("review", 0)
   119→    potentials["review"] = max(existing_review, HOLISTIC_POTENTIAL)
   120→
   121→    concern_count = sum(1 for issue in review_issues if issue.get("detector") == "concerns")
   122→    if concern_count:
   123→        potentials["concerns"] = max(potentials.get("concerns", 0), concern_count)
   124→
   125→    merge_potentials_dict: dict[str, int] = {"review": potentials.get("review", 0)}
   126→    if potentials.get("concerns", 0) > 0:
   127→        merge_potentials_dict["concerns"] = potentials["concerns"]
   128→
   129→    diff = merge_scan(
   130→        state,
   131→        review_issues,
   132→        options=MergeScanOptions(
   133→            lang=lang_name,
   134→            potentials=merge_potentials_dict,
   135→            merge_potentials=True,
   136→        ),
   137→    )
   138→
   139→    new_ids = {issue["id"] for issue in review_issues}
   140→    _auto_resolve_stale_holistic(
   141→        state,
   142→        new_ids,
   143→        diff,
   144→        utc_now_fn,
   145→        imported_dimensions=imported_dimensions,
   146→        full_sweep_included=scope_full_sweep,
   147→    )
   148→
   149→    if skipped:
   150→        diff["skipped"] = len(skipped)
```

```
1→"""Parallel execution and progress-callback helpers for review batches."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import threading
     8→from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→
    11→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import safe_write_text
    12→
    13→from ._runner_parallel_execution import (
    14→    _drain_parallel_completions,
    15→    _execute_serial,
    16→    _queue_parallel_tasks,
    17→    _resolve_parallel_runtime,
    18→)
    19→from ._runner_parallel_progress import (
    20→    _coerce_batch_execution_options,
    21→)
    22→from ._runner_parallel_types import (
    23→    BatchExecutionOptions,
    24→    BatchProgressEvent,
    25→    BatchResult,
    26→    BatchTask,
    27→)
    28→from .runner_process import _extract_payload_from_log
    29→
    30→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    31→
    32→
    33→def execute_batches(
    34→    *,
    35→    tasks: dict[int, BatchTask],
    36→    options: BatchExecutionOptions | None = None,
    37→    progress_fn=None,
    38→    error_log_fn=None,
    39→) -> list[int]:
    40→    """Run indexed tasks and return failed index list.
    41→
    42→    Each value in *tasks* is a zero-arg callable returning an int exit code.
    43→    All domain knowledge (files, prompts, etc.) is pre-bound by the caller.
    44→    """
    45→    resolved_options = _coerce_batch_execution_options(options)
    46→    contract_cache: dict[int, str] = {}
    47→    indexes = sorted(tasks)
    48→    if resolved_options.run_parallel:
    49→        max_workers, heartbeat = _resolve_parallel_runtime(
    50→            indexes=indexes,
    51→            max_parallel_workers=resolved_options.max_parallel_workers,
    52→            heartbeat_seconds=resolved_options.heartbeat_seconds,
    53→        )
    54→        failures: set[int] = set()
    55→        progress_failures: set[int] = set()
    56→        started_at: dict[int, float] = {}
    57→        lock = threading.Lock()
    58→        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=max_workers) as executor:
    59→            futures = _queue_parallel_tasks(
    60→                executor=executor,
    61→                indexes=indexes,
    62→                tasks=tasks,
    63→                progress_fn=progress_fn,
    64→                error_log_fn=error_log_fn,
    65→                contract_cache=contract_cache,
    66→                max_workers=max_workers,
    67→                failures=failures,
    68→                progress_failures=progress_failures,
    69→                started_at=started_at,
    70→                lock=lock,
    71→                clock_fn=resolved_options.clock_fn,
    72→            )
    73→            pending = set(futures.keys())
    74→            _drain_parallel_completions(
    75→                pending=pending,
    76→                futures=futures,
    77→                heartbeat=heartbeat,
    78→                indexes=indexes,
    79→                progress_fn=progress_fn,
    80→                error_log_fn=error_log_fn,
    81→                contract_cache=contract_cache,
    82→                failures=failures,
    83→                progress_failures=progress_failures,
    84→                started_at=started_at,
    85→                lock=lock,
    86→                clock_fn=resolved_options.clock_fn,
    87→            )
    88→        return sorted(failures)
    89→    return _execute_serial(
    90→        tasks=tasks,
    91→        indexes=indexes,
    92→        progress_fn=progress_fn,
    93→        error_log_fn=error_log_fn,
    94→        clock_fn=resolved_options.clock_fn,
    95→        contract_cache=contract_cache,
    96→    )
    97→
    98→
    99→def collect_batch_results(
   100→    *,
   101→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   102→    failures: list[int],
   103→    output_files: dict[int, Path],
   104→    allowed_dims: set[str],
   105→    extract_payload_fn,
   106→    normalize_result_fn,
   107→) -> tuple[list[BatchResult], list[int]]:
   108→    """Parse and normalize batch outputs, preserving prior failures."""
   109→    batch_results: list[BatchResult] = []
   110→    failure_set = set(failures)
   111→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   112→        had_execution_failure = idx in failure_set
   113→        raw_path = output_files[idx]
   114→        payload = None
   115→        parsed_from_log = False
   116→        if raw_path.exists():
   117→            try:
   118→                payload = extract_payload_fn(raw_path.read_text())
   119→            except OSError as exc:
   120→                logger.debug("Failed reading batch payload %s: %s", raw_path, exc)
   121→                payload = None
   122→        if payload is None:
   123→            payload = _extract_payload_from_log(idx, raw_path, extract_payload_fn)
   124→            parsed_from_log = payload is not None
   125→        if payload is None:
   126→            failure_set.add(idx)
   127→            continue
   128→        if parsed_from_log:
   129→            try:
   130→                safe_write_text(raw_path, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   131→            except OSError as exc:
   132→                logger.error("Failed writing normalized batch payload %s: %s", raw_path, exc)
   133→                raise
   134→        try:
   135→            assessments, issues, dimension_notes, quality = normalize_result_fn(
   136→                payload,
   137→                allowed_dims,
   138→            )
   139→        except ValueError as exc:
   140→            logger.debug("Invalid batch payload at index %s (%s): %s", idx, raw_path, exc)
   141→            failure_set.add(idx)
   142→            continue
   143→        if had_execution_failure:
   144→            failure_set.discard(idx)
   145→        batch_results.append(
   146→            BatchResult(
   147→                batch_index=idx + 1,
   148→                assessments=assessments,
   149→                dimension_notes=dimension_notes,
   150→                issues=issues,
   151→                quality=quality,
   152→            )
   153→        )
   154→    return batch_results, sorted(failure_set)
   155→
   156→
   157→__all__ = [
   158→    "BatchResult",
   159→    "BatchExecutionOptions",
   160→    "BatchProgressEvent",
   161→    "collect_batch_results",
   162→    "execute_batches",
   163→]
   164→
```

```
   286→def _validate_run_dir(run_dir: Path) -> tuple[dict, Path, str]:
   287→    """Validate run directory, load summary, and return (summary, blind_packet_path, immutable_packet_path).
   288→
   289→    Raises CommandError on any validation failure.
   290→    """
   291→    if not run_dir.is_dir():
   292→        raise CommandError(f"run directory not found: {run_dir}", exit_code=1)
   293→
   294→    summary_path = run_dir / "run_summary.json"
   295→    if not summary_path.exists():
   296→        raise CommandError(f"no run_summary.json in {run_dir}", exit_code=1)
   297→    try:
   298→        summary = json.loads(summary_path.read_text())
   299→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   300→        raise CommandError(f"Error reading run summary: {exc}", exit_code=1) from exc
   301→
   302→    successful = summary.get("successful_batches", [])
   303→    blind_packet_path = Path(str(summary.get("blind_packet", "")))
   304→    immutable_packet_path = str(summary.get("immutable_packet", ""))
   305→
   306→    if not successful:
   307→        raise CommandError("no successful batches in run summary.", exit_code=1)
   308→    if not blind_packet_path.exists():
   309→        raise PacketValidationError(f"blind packet not found: {blind_packet_path}", exit_code=1)
   310→
   311→    try:
   312→        packet = json.loads(Path(immutable_packet_path).read_text())
   313→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   314→        raise PacketValidationError(f"Error reading immutable packet: {exc}", exit_code=1) from exc
   315→
   316→    summary["_packet"] = packet
   317→    return summary, blind_packet_path, immutable_packet_path
   318→
   319→
   320→def do_import_run(
   321→    run_dir_path: str,
   322→    state: dict,
   323→    lang,

   331→    """Re-import results from a completed run directory.
   332→
   333→    Replays the merge+provenance+import step that normally runs at the end of

   335→    broken pipe from background execution) but all batch results completed.
   336→    """
   337→    run_dir = Path(run_dir_path)
   338→    summary, blind_packet_path, _immutable_path = _validate_run_dir(run_dir)
   339→
   340→    runner = str(summary.get("runner", "codex"))
   341→    stamp = str(summary.get("run_stamp", ""))
   342→    successful = summary.get("successful_batches", [])
   343→    packet = summary.pop("_packet", {})
   344→    allowed_dims = {str(d) for d in packet.get("dimensions", []) if isinstance(d, str)}
   345→
   346→    # -- locate and parse raw batch results --
   347→    results_dir = run_dir / "results"
```

## Summary: Review Sub-Agent JSON Schema Requirements

Based on my tracing through the code, there are **two distinct JSON schema paths** for review sub-agent output:

---

## PATH 1: BATCH REVIEW (Holistic Batches Run by Subagents)

### Entry Point & Parsing
- **File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/batch/core.py`
- **Functions**: `extract_json_payload()` → `normalize_batch_result()`

### Top-Level Keys Expected

The JSON must contain:
```
{
  "assessments": dict[str, float],  # REQUIRED - dimension scores 0-100
  "issues": list[object],             # REQUIRED (or legacy "findings")
  "dimension_notes": dict[str, object] # OPTIONAL
}
```

**Key Points:**
- `assessments` key is **required** (line 149: checks `isinstance(obj.get("assessments"), dict)`)
- `issues` key is **required** (line 150: checks `isinstance(obj.get("issues"), list)`)
- Legacy key `findings` is **aliased to `issues`** (function `normalize_legacy_findings_alias` at core.py:400-405)
- Key `dimension_notes` is **optional** (core.py:411, defaults to `{}`)

### Issue Object Schema (`issues` Array)

Each issue in the `issues` array must pass `validate_review_issue_payload()` from `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/review/importing/contracts_validation.py`:

**Required Fields** (see `REVIEW_ISSUE_REQUIRED_FIELDS` at line 7-15):
- `dimension` (str, non-empty, must be in `allowed_dims`)
- `identifier` (str, non-empty)
- `summary` (str, non-empty)
- `confidence` (str, one of: "high", "medium", "low")
- `suggestion` (str, non-empty)
- `related_files` (list[str], at least one file path)
- `evidence` (list[str], at least one concrete evidence string)

**Optional Fields**:
- `reasoning` (str)
- `evidence_lines` (list[int])
- `concern_verdict` (str, e.g., "dismissed")
- `concern_fingerprint` (str, required if `concern_verdict="dismissed"`)
- `concern_type` (str)
- `concern_file` (str)
- `file` (str)

**Batch-Specific Additional Fields** (from `BatchIssuePayload` at core.py:38-42):
- `impact_scope` (str, non-empty) - either in issue or inherited from `dimension_notes`
- `fix_scope` (str, non-empty) - either in issue or inherited from `dimension_notes`

### Dimension Notes Schema (`dimension_notes` Object)

Each dimension in `dimension_notes` must follow `BatchDimensionNotePayload` (core.py:45-53):

```
{
  "evidence": list[str],                    # REQUIRED - non-empty array
  "impact_scope": str,                      # REQUIRED - non-empty string
  "fix_scope": str,                         # REQUIRED - non-empty string
  "confidence": str,                        # OPTIONAL - defaults to "medium"
  "issues_preventing_higher_score": str,    # OPTIONAL (or legacy "unreported_risk")
  "sub_axes": dict[str, float]              # OPTIONAL (abstraction_fitness only)
}
```

**Validation** (core.py:160-196):
- `evidence` must be non-empty list (line 176)
- `impact_scope` must be non-empty string (line 180)
- `fix_scope` must be non-empty string (line 184)
- `confidence` defaults to "medium" if missing/invalid (line 189-192)
- `issues_preventing_higher_score` key (line 193, with legacy fallback `unreported_risk` at line 195)

**Sub-Axes** (for `abstraction_fitness` dimension only, core.py:199-228):
- `sub_axes.abstraction_leverage`: float 0-100
- `sub_axes.indirection_cost`: float 0-100
- `sub_axes.interface_honesty`: float 0-100
- `sub_axes.delegation_density`: float 0-100
- `sub_axes.definition_directness`: float 0-100
- `sub_axes.type_discipline`: float 0-100

### Quality Object Schema (Optional)

From `BatchQualityPayload` (core.py:56-62):
```
{
  "dimension_coverage": float,           # ratio dimensions assessed / total
  "evidence_density": float,             # evidence count / issues count
  "high_score_missing_issue_note": float # count of high-score dims missing issue notes
  "high_score_without_risk": float       # legacy name (aliased)
}
```

### Validation Rules

- **Low-score enforcement** (core.py:341-360): If any dimension scores below 85.0 (LOW_SCORE_ISSUE_THRESHOLD), that dimension **must** have at least one explicit issue (feedback_contract.py:5)
- **High-score note requirement** (core.py:437-438): If score > 85.0 (HIGH_SCORE_ISSUES_NOTE_THRESHOLD), dimension must include `issues_preventing_higher_score` note
- **Assessment feedback** (feedback_contract.py:28-30): Scores below 100.0 must have explicit feedback (issue with suggestion or dimension_notes evidence)
- **Issue limit**: Max issues per batch capped at `max(10, dimension_count)` (feedback_contract.py:17-20)

---

## PATH 2: EXTERNAL REVIEW (External Claude Sessions)

### Entry Point & Parsing
- **File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/importing/helpers.py`
- **Function**: `load_import_issues_data()` which calls `_parse_and_validate_import()`

### Top-Level Keys Expected

The JSON must contain:
```
{
  "issues": list[object],                 # REQUIRED (or legacy "findings")
  "assessments": dict[str, Any],          # OPTIONAL - assessment scores
  "reviewed_files": list[str],            # OPTIONAL - files reviewed
  "review_scope": dict[str, Any],         # OPTIONAL - scope metadata
  "provenance": dict[str, Any],           # OPTIONAL - provenance metadata
  "dimension_notes": dict[str, Any]       # OPTIONAL - dimension evidence notes
}
```

**Key Points**:
- `issues` is **required** (helpers.py:70-73, parse.py:82-90)
- Legacy key `findings` aliased to `issues` (payload.py:26-47)
- All other keys are **optional** and default to empty dict/array
- Flat JSON array is also accepted and auto-wrapped as `{"issues": [...]}`(parse.py:288)

### Issue Object Schema (Same as Batch Path)

**Required fields** (contracts_validation.py:7-15):
- `dimension` (str)
- `identifier` (str)
- `summary` (str)
- `confidence` (str: "high", "medium", "low")
- `suggestion` (str)
- `related_files` (list[str])
- `evidence` (list[str])

**Optional fields** (same as batch):
- `reasoning`, `evidence_lines`, `concern_verdict`, `concern_fingerprint`, `concern_type`, `concern_file`, `file`

**Dismissed Concerns** (contracts_validation.py:37-63):
When `concern_verdict: "dismissed"`:
- `concern_fingerprint` becomes **required**
- Other required fields are **optional**
- Returns minimal payload with just: `concern_verdict`, `concern_fingerprint`, plus optional `concern_type`, `concern_file`, `reasoning`

### Validation Rules (External Path Additional)

**Assessment Feedback Requirements** (parse.py:245-270):
- Dimensions with score < 85.0 **must** have at least one issue with same dimension + non-empty suggestion
- Dimensions with score < 100.0 **must** have explicit feedback via:
  - Issue with same dimension and non-empty suggestion, **OR**
  - `dimension_notes` entry with concrete `evidence` array

**Schema Validation** (parse.py:176-209):
- Unless `--allow-partial` flag: all issues must pass strict schema validation
- With `--allow-partial`: schema errors are collected but import continues

**Override Flags**:
- `--manual-override` requires `--attest` flag (parse.py:343-344)
- `--attested-external` cannot combine with `--allow-partial` or `--manual-override` (parse.py:307-318)

---

## Key Differences Between Paths

| Aspect | Batch Path | External Path |
|--------|-----------|----------------|
| **Entry Function** | `normalize_batch_result()` | `load_import_issues_data()` |
| **Dimension Notes** | **Required** (with evidence/impact_scope/fix_scope) | **Optional** (only for feedback evidence) |
| **Sub-axes** | **Supported** in `dimension_notes.sub_axes` | **Not supported** |
| **Quality Object** | **Included** in output | **Not supported** |
| **Impact/Fix Scope** | **Required** on issue or dimension_notes | **Not required** |
| **Assessment Feedback** | Validated at normalize time | Validated at load time with policy options |
| **Dismissed Issues** | **Not allowed** (`allow_dismissed=False`, core.py:251) | **Allowed** with concern_verdict/fingerprint |

---

- **Batch Schema**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/batch/core.py` (lines 65-72, 38-42, 45-53)
- **Issue Validation**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/review/importing/contracts_validation.py`

- **Feedback Contract**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/review/feedback_contract.py` (constants & validation rules)

```
    37→def _record_execution_issue(append_run_log_fn, batch_index: int, exc: Exception) -> None:
    38→    """Record one execute_batches callback/task failure in run.log."""
    39→    if batch_index < 0:
    40→        append_run_log_fn(f"execution-error heartbeat error={exc}")
    41→        return
    42→    append_run_log_fn(f"execution-error batch={batch_index + 1} error={exc}")
    43→
    44→
    45→def _runtime_expectation(
    46→    *,
    47→    total_batches: int,
    48→    run_parallel: bool,
    49→    max_parallel_batches: int,
    50→    batch_timeout_seconds: int,
    51→) -> tuple[int, int]:
    52→    effective_workers = min(total_batches, max_parallel_batches) if run_parallel else 1
    53→    waves = max(1, math.ceil(total_batches / max(1, effective_workers)))
    54→    worst_case_minutes = max(1, math.ceil((waves * batch_timeout_seconds) / 60))
    55→    return effective_workers, worst_case_minutes
    56→

    83→    batch_timeout_seconds: int,
    84→    heartbeat_seconds: float,
    85→    stall_warning_seconds: int,
    86→    stall_kill_seconds: int,
    87→    batch_max_retries: int,
    88→    batch_retry_backoff_seconds: float,
    89→    worst_case_minutes: int,
    90→    selected_indexes: list[int],
    91→    run_dir: Path,
    92→    immutable_packet_path: Path,
    93→    prompt_packet_path: Path,
    94→) -> None:
    95→    append_run_log(
    96→        "run-start "
    97→        f"runner={runner} parallel={run_parallel} max_parallel={max_parallel_batches} "
    98→        f"timeout={batch_timeout_seconds}s heartbeat={heartbeat_seconds:.1f}s "
    99→        f"stall_warning={stall_warning_seconds}s stall_kill={stall_kill_seconds}s "
   100→        f"retries={batch_max_retries} "
   101→        f"retry_backoff={batch_retry_backoff_seconds:.1f}s upper_bound={worst_case_minutes}m "
   102→        f"selected={[idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes]}"
   103→    )
   104→    append_run_log(f"run-path {run_dir}")
   105→    append_run_log(f"packet {immutable_packet_path}")
   106→    append_run_log(f"blind-packet {prompt_packet_path}")
   107→
   108→
   109→def _handle_dry_run(
   110→    *,
   111→    args,
   112→    colorize_fn,
   113→    immutable_packet_path: Path,
   114→    prompt_packet_path: Path,
   115→    run_dir: Path,
   116→    append_run_log,
   117→) -> bool:
   118→    if not getattr(args, "dry_run", False):
   119→        return False
   120→    print(
   121→        colorize_fn(
   122→            "  Dry run only: prompts generated, runner execution skipped.",
   123→            "yellow",
   124→        )
   125→    )
   126→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Immutable packet: {immutable_packet_path}", "dim"))
   127→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Blind packet: {prompt_packet_path}", "dim"))
   128→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Prompts: {run_dir / 'prompts'}", "dim"))
   129→    append_run_log("run-finished dry-run")
   130→    return True
   131→
   132→
   133→def _build_batch_tracking(
   134→    *,
   135→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   136→    prompt_files: dict[int, Path],
   137→    output_files: dict[int, Path],
   138→    log_files: dict[int, Path],
   139→) -> tuple[str, dict[int, int], set[int], dict[str, dict[str, object]]]:
   140→    summary_created_at = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   141→    batch_positions = {batch_idx: pos + 1 for pos, batch_idx in enumerate(selected_indexes)}
   142→    stall_warned_batches: set[int] = set()
   143→    batch_status: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {
   144→        str(idx + 1): {
   145→            "position": batch_positions.get(idx, 0),
   146→            "status": "pending",
   147→            "prompt_path": str(prompt_files[idx]),
   148→            "result_path": str(output_files[idx]),
   149→            "log_path": str(log_files[idx]),
   150→        }
   151→        for idx in selected_indexes
   152→    }
   153→    return summary_created_at, batch_positions, stall_warned_batches, batch_status
   154→
   155→
   156→def _handle_heartbeat_progress(
   157→    *,
   158→    details: dict[str, object],
   159→    total_batches: int,
   160→    stall_warning_seconds: int,
   161→    stall_warned_batches: set[int],
   162→    append_run_log,
   163→    colorize_fn,
   164→) -> None:
   165→    active = details.get("active_batches")
   166→    queued = details.get("queued_batches", [])
   167→    elapsed = details.get("elapsed_seconds", {})
   168→    if not isinstance(active, list):
   169→        active = []
   170→    if not isinstance(queued, list):
   171→        queued = []
   172→    if not active and not queued:
   173→        return
   174→
   175→    segments: list[str] = []
   176→    for idx in active[:6]:
   177→        secs = 0
   178→        if isinstance(elapsed, dict):
   179→            raw_secs = elapsed.get(idx, 0)
   180→            secs = int(raw_secs) if isinstance(raw_secs, int | float) else 0
   181→        segments.append(f"#{idx + 1}:{secs}s")
   182→    if len(active) > 6:
   183→        segments.append(f"+{len(active) - 6} more")
   184→
   185→    queued_segment = f", queued {len(queued)}" if queued else ""
   186→    print(
   187→        colorize_fn(
   188→            "  Batch heartbeat: "
   189→            f"{len(active)}/{total_batches} active{queued_segment} "
   190→            f"({', '.join(segments) if segments else 'running batches pending'})",
   191→            "dim",
   192→        )
   193→    )
   194→    elapsed_text = ""
   195→    if isinstance(elapsed, dict):
   196→        elapsed_text = ", ".join(f"{idx + 1}:{elapsed.get(idx, 0)}" for idx in active)
   197→    append_run_log(
   198→        "heartbeat "
   199→        f"active={[idx + 1 for idx in active]} queued={[idx + 1 for idx in queued]} "
   200→        f"elapsed={{{elapsed_text}}}"
   201→    )
   202→
   203→    if stall_warning_seconds <= 0 or not isinstance(elapsed, dict):
   204→        return
   205→    slow_active = [
   206→        idx
   207→        for idx in active
   208→        if isinstance(elapsed.get(idx), int | float)
   209→        and int(elapsed.get(idx) or 0) >= stall_warning_seconds
   210→    ]
   211→    newly_warned = [idx for idx in slow_active if idx not in stall_warned_batches]
   212→    if not newly_warned:
   213→        return
   214→
   215→    stall_warned_batches.update(newly_warned)
   216→    warning_message = (
   217→        "  Stall warning: batches "
   218→        f"{[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(newly_warned)]} exceeded "
   219→        f"{stall_warning_seconds}s elapsed. "
   220→        "This may be normal for long runs; review run.log and batch logs."
   221→    )
   222→    print(colorize_fn(warning_message, "yellow"))
   223→    append_run_log(
   224→        "stall-warning "
   225→        f"threshold={stall_warning_seconds}s batches={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(newly_warned)]}"
   226→    )
   227→
   228→
   229→def _handle_batch_progress(
   230→    *,
   231→    progress_event: BatchProgressEvent,
   232→    batch_positions: dict[int, int],
   233→    total_batches: int,
   234→    prompt_files: dict[int, Path],
   235→    output_files: dict[int, Path],
   236→    log_files: dict[int, Path],
   237→    batch_status: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
   238→    stall_warned_batches: set[int],
   239→    append_run_log,
   240→    colorize_fn,
   241→) -> None:
   242→    batch_index = progress_event.batch_index
   243→    event = progress_event.event
   244→    code = progress_event.code
   245→    details = progress_event.details
   246→
   247→    position = batch_positions.get(batch_index, 0)
   248→    key = str(batch_index + 1)
   249→    state = batch_status.setdefault(
   250→        key,
   251→        {
   252→            "position": position,
   253→            "status": "pending",
   254→            "prompt_path": str(prompt_files.get(batch_index, "")),
   255→            "result_path": str(output_files.get(batch_index, "")),
   256→            "log_path": str(log_files.get(batch_index, "")),
   257→        },
   258→    )
   259→
   260→    if event == "queued":
   261→        state["status"] = "queued"
   262→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Batch {position}/{total_batches} queued (#{batch_index + 1})", "dim"))
   263→        append_run_log(f"batch-queued batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{total_batches}")
   264→        return
   265→
   266→    if event == "start":
   267→        state["status"] = "running"
   268→        state["started_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   269→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Batch {position}/{total_batches} started (#{batch_index + 1})", "dim"))
   270→        append_run_log(f"batch-start batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{total_batches}")
   271→        return
   272→
   273→    if event != "done":
   274→        return
   275→    status = "done" if code == 0 else f"failed ({code})"
   276→    tone = "dim" if code == 0 else "yellow"
   277→    elapsed_seconds = details.get("elapsed_seconds")
   278→    elapsed_suffix = ""
   279→    if isinstance(elapsed_seconds, int | float):
   280→        elapsed_suffix = f" in {int(max(0, elapsed_seconds))}s"
   281→        state["elapsed_seconds"] = int(max(0, elapsed_seconds))
   282→    state["status"] = "succeeded" if code == 0 else "failed"
   283→    state["exit_code"] = int(code) if isinstance(code, int) else code
   284→    state["completed_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   285→    stall_warned_batches.discard(batch_index)
   286→    print(
   287→        colorize_fn(
   288→            f"  Batch {position}/{total_batches} {status}{elapsed_suffix} (#{batch_index + 1})",
   289→            tone,
   290→        )
   291→    )
   292→    append_run_log(
   293→        f"batch-done batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{total_batches} "
   294→        f"code={code} elapsed={state.get('elapsed_seconds', 0)}"
   295→    )
   296→
   297→
   298→def _build_progress_reporter(
   299→    *,
   300→    batch_positions: dict[int, int],
   301→    total_batches: int,
   302→    prompt_files: dict[int, Path],
   303→    output_files: dict[int, Path],
   304→    log_files: dict[int, Path],
   305→    batch_status: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
   306→    stall_warned_batches: set[int],
   307→    stall_warning_seconds: int,
   308→    append_run_log,
   309→    colorize_fn,
   310→) -> Callable[[BatchProgressEvent], None]:
   311→    def _report_progress(progress_event: BatchProgressEvent) -> None:
   312→        if progress_event.event == "heartbeat":
   313→            _handle_heartbeat_progress(
   314→                details=progress_event.details,
   315→                total_batches=total_batches,
   316→                stall_warning_seconds=stall_warning_seconds,
   317→                stall_warned_batches=stall_warned_batches,
   318→                append_run_log=append_run_log,
   319→                colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   320→            )
   321→            return
   322→        _handle_batch_progress(
   323→            progress_event=progress_event,
   324→            batch_positions=batch_positions,
   325→            total_batches=total_batches,
   326→            prompt_files=prompt_files,
   327→            output_files=output_files,
   328→            log_files=log_files,
   329→            batch_status=batch_status,
   330→            stall_warned_batches=stall_warned_batches,
   331→            append_run_log=append_run_log,
   332→            colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   333→        )
   334→
   335→    return _report_progress
   336→
   337→
   338→def _build_summary_writer(
   339→    *,
   340→    run_summary_path: Path,
   341→    summary_config: BatchRunSummaryConfig,
   342→    batch_status: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
   343→    safe_write_text_fn,
   344→    colorize_fn,
   345→    append_run_log,
   346→):
   347→    def write_run_summary(
   348→        *,
   349→        successful_batches: list[int],
   350→        failed_batches: list[int],
   351→        interrupted: bool = False,
   352→        interruption_reason: str | None = None,
   353→    ) -> None:
   354→        _write_run_summary_impl(
   355→            summary_path=run_summary_path,
   356→            summary_config=summary_config,
   357→            batch_status=batch_status,
   358→            successful_batches=successful_batches,
   359→            failed_batches=failed_batches,
   360→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text_fn,
   361→            colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   362→            append_run_log_fn=append_run_log,
   363→            interrupted=interrupted,
   364→            interruption_reason=interruption_reason,
   365→        )
   366→
   367→    return write_run_summary
   368→
   369→
   370→def _mark_interrupted_batches(
   371→    *,
   372→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   373→    batch_positions: dict[int, int],
   374→    batch_status: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
   375→) -> None:
   376→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   377→        key = str(idx + 1)
   378→        state = batch_status.setdefault(
   379→            key,
   380→            {"position": batch_positions.get(idx, 0), "status": "pending"},
   381→        )
   382→        if state.get("status") in {"pending", "queued", "running"}:
   383→            state["status"] = "interrupted"
   384→
   385→
   386→def _update_batch_statuses(
   387→    *,
   388→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   389→    execution_failures: list[int],
   390→    failures: list[int],
   391→    batch_positions: dict[int, int],
   392→    batch_status: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
   393→    output_files: dict[int, Path],
   394→) -> list[int]:
   395→    execution_failure_set = set(execution_failures)
   396→    failure_set = set(failures)
   397→    successful_indexes = sorted(idx for idx in selected_indexes if idx not in failure_set)
   398→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   399→        key = str(idx + 1)
   400→        state = batch_status.setdefault(
   401→            key,
   402→            {"position": batch_positions.get(idx, 0), "status": "pending"},
   403→        )
   404→        if idx not in failure_set:
   405→            state["status"] = "succeeded"
   406→            continue
   407→        if idx in execution_failure_set:
   408→            state["status"] = "failed"
   409→            continue
   410→        state["status"] = "parse_failed" if output_files[idx].exists() else "missing_output"
   411→    return successful_indexes
   412→
   413→
   414→def _execute_and_collect_results(
   415→    *,
   416→    execute_batches_fn,
   417→    tasks: dict[int, Callable[[], int]],
   418→    run_parallel: bool,
   419→    max_parallel_batches: int,
   420→    heartbeat_seconds: float,
   421→    progress_fn,
   422→    error_log_fn,
   423→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   424→    batch_positions: dict[int, int],
   425→    batch_status: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
   426→    write_run_summary,
   427→    append_run_log,
   428→    collect_batch_results_fn,
   429→    packet: dict[str, object],
   430→    output_files: dict[int, Path],
   431→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, object]], list[int], list[int]]:
   432→    try:
   433→        execution_failures = execute_batches_fn(
   434→            tasks=tasks,
   435→            options=BatchExecutionOptions(
   436→                run_parallel=run_parallel,
   437→                max_parallel_workers=max_parallel_batches,
   438→                heartbeat_seconds=heartbeat_seconds,
   439→            ),
   440→            progress_fn=progress_fn,
   441→            error_log_fn=error_log_fn,
   442→        )
   443→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   444→        _mark_interrupted_batches(
   445→            selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   446→            batch_positions=batch_positions,
   447→            batch_status=batch_status,
   448→        )
   449→        write_run_summary(
   450→            successful_batches=[],
   451→            failed_batches=[],
   452→            interrupted=True,
   453→            interruption_reason="keyboard_interrupt",
   454→        )
   455→        append_run_log("run-interrupted reason=keyboard_interrupt")
   456→        raise SystemExit(130) from None
   457→
   458→    allowed_dims = {str(dim) for dim in packet.get("dimensions", []) if isinstance(dim, str)}
   459→    batch_results, failures = collect_batch_results_fn(
   460→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   461→        failures=execution_failures,
   462→        output_files=output_files,
   463→        allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   464→    )
   465→    return batch_results, failures, execution_failures
   466→
   467→
   468→def _handle_failures_before_import(
   469→    *,
   470→    failures: list[int],
   471→    allow_partial: bool,
   472→    batch_results: list[dict[str, object]],
   473→    immutable_packet_path: Path,
   474→    logs_dir: Path,
   475→    print_failures_fn,
   476→    print_failures_and_raise_fn,
   477→    append_run_log,
   478→    colorize_fn,
   479→    successful_indexes: list[int],
   480→) -> None:
   481→    failure_set = sorted(set(failures))
   482→    if failures and (not allow_partial or not batch_results):
   483→        append_run_log(f"run-finished failures={[idx + 1 for idx in failure_set]} mode=exit")
   484→        print_failures_and_raise_fn(
   485→            failures=failures,
   486→            packet_path=immutable_packet_path,
   487→            logs_dir=logs_dir,
   488→            colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   489→        )
   490→    if not failures:
   491→        return
   492→    print(
   493→        colorize_fn(
   494→            "  Partial completion enabled: importing successful batches and keeping failed batches open.",
   495→            "yellow",
   496→        )
   497→    )
   498→    print_failures_fn(
   499→        failures=failures,
   500→        packet_path=immutable_packet_path,
   501→        logs_dir=logs_dir,
   502→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   503→    )
   504→    append_run_log(
   505→        "run-partial "
   506→        f"successful={[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes]} "
   507→        f"failed={[idx + 1 for idx in failure_set]}"
   508→    )
   509→
   510→
   511→def _merge_and_write_payload(
   512→    *,
   513→    merge_batch_results_fn,
   514→    batch_results: list[dict[str, object]],
   515→    batches: list[dict[str, object]],
   516→    successful_indexes: list[int],
   517→    packet: dict[str, object],
   518→    runner: str,
   519→    prompt_packet_path: Path,
   520→    stamp: str,
   521→    build_import_provenance_fn,
   522→    scored_dimensions: list[str],
   523→    packet_dimensions: list[str],
   524→    scan_path: str,
   525→    run_dir: Path,
   526→    safe_write_text_fn,
   527→    colorize_fn,
   528→) -> Path:
   529→    merged = merge_batch_results_fn(batch_results)
   530→    reviewed_files = collect_reviewed_files_from_batches(
   531→        batches=batches,
   532→        selected_indexes=successful_indexes,
   533→    )
   534→    full_sweep_included = any(
   535→        str(batch.get("name", "")).strip().lower() == "full codebase sweep"
   536→        for idx in successful_indexes
   537→        if 0 <= idx < len(batches)
   538→        for batch in [batches[idx]]
   539→        if isinstance(batch, dict)
   540→    )
   541→
   542→    review_scope: dict[str, object] = {
   543→        "reviewed_files_count": len(reviewed_files),
   544→        "successful_batch_count": len(successful_indexes),
   545→        "full_sweep_included": full_sweep_included,
   546→    }
   547→    total_files = packet.get("total_files")
   548→    if isinstance(total_files, int) and not isinstance(total_files, bool) and total_files > 0:
   549→        review_scope["total_files"] = total_files
   550→    merged["review_scope"] = review_scope
   551→
   552→    if reviewed_files:
   553→        merged["reviewed_files"] = reviewed_files
   554→        print(
   555→            colorize_fn(
   556→                f"  Reviewed files captured for cache refresh: {len(reviewed_files)}",
   557→                "dim",
   558→            )
   559→        )
   560→
   561→    merged["provenance"] = build_import_provenance_fn(
   562→        runner=runner,
   563→        blind_packet_path=prompt_packet_path,
   564→        run_stamp=stamp,
   565→        batch_indexes=successful_indexes,
   566→    )
   567→    merged_assessment_dims = normalize_dimension_list(
   568→        list((merged.get("assessments") or {}).keys())
   569→    )
   570→    merged_issue_dims = normalize_dimension_list(
   571→        [
   572→            issue.get("dimension")
   573→            for issue in (merged.get("issues") or [])
   574→            if isinstance(issue, dict)
   575→        ]
   576→    )
   577→    review_scope["imported_dimensions"] = normalize_dimension_list(
   578→        merged_assessment_dims + merged_issue_dims
   579→    )
   580→
   581→    missing_after_import = print_import_dimension_coverage_notice(
   582→        assessed_dims=merged_assessment_dims,
   583→        scored_dims=scored_dimensions,
   584→        scan_path=scan_path,
   585→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   586→    )
   587→    merged["assessment_coverage"] = {
   588→        "scored_dimensions": scored_dimensions,
   589→        "selected_dimensions": packet_dimensions,
   590→        "imported_dimensions": merged_assessment_dims,
   591→        "missing_dimensions": missing_after_import,
   592→    }
   593→
   594→    merged_path = run_dir / "holistic_issues_merged.json"
   595→    safe_write_text_fn(merged_path, json.dumps(merged, indent=2) + "\n")
   596→    print(colorize_fn(f"\n  Merged outputs: {merged_path}", "bold"))
   597→    print_review_quality(merged.get("review_quality", {}), colorize_fn=colorize_fn)
   598→    return merged_path
   599→
   600→
   601→def _import_merged_payload(
   602→    *,
   603→    merged_path: Path,
   604→    do_import_fn,
   605→    state,
   606→    lang,
   607→    state_file,
   608→    config: dict[str, Any],
   609→    allow_partial: bool,
   610→    append_run_log,
   611→    successful_indexes: list[int],
   612→    failures: list[int],
   613→) -> None:
   614→    try:
   615→        do_import_fn(
   616→            str(merged_path),
   617→            state,
   618→            lang,
   619→            state_file,
   620→            config=config,
   621→            allow_partial=allow_partial,
   622→            trusted_assessment_source=True,
   623→            trusted_assessment_label="trusted internal run-batches import",
   624→        )
   625→    except SystemExit as exc:
   626→        append_run_log(f"run-finished import-failed code={exc.code}")
   627→        raise
   628→    except Exception as exc:
   629→        append_run_log(f"run-finished import-error error={exc}")
   630→        raise
   631→
   632→    append_run_log(
   633→        "run-finished "
   634→        f"successful={[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes]} "
   635→        f"failed={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(set(failures))]} imported={str(merged_path)}"
   636→    )
   637→
   638→
   639→def _run_followup_scan_if_requested(
   640→    *,
   641→    args,
   642→    lang,
   643→    run_followup_scan_fn,
   644→) -> None:
   645→    if not getattr(args, "scan_after_import", False):
   646→        return
   647→    followup_code = run_followup_scan_fn(

   654→            exit_code=followup_code,

   658→def do_run_batches(
   659→    args,
   660→    state,
   661→    lang,
   662→    state_file,
   663→    *,
   664→    config: dict[str, Any] | None,
   665→    run_stamp_fn,

   683→    """Run holistic investigation batches with a local subagent runner."""
   684→    config = config or {}

   695→    batch_retry_backoff_seconds = policy.batch_retry_backoff_seconds
   696→    stall_warning_seconds = policy.stall_warning_seconds

   698→
   699→    stamp = run_stamp_fn()

   701→        args,

   718→    batches = require_batches(

   720→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,

   722→    )
   723→
   724→    selected_indexes = selected_batch_indexes_fn(args, batch_count=len(batches))
   725→    total_batches = len(selected_indexes)
   726→    effective_workers, worst_case_minutes = _runtime_expectation(

   739→
   740→    run_dir, logs_dir, prompt_files, output_files, log_files = prepare_run_artifacts_fn(
   741→        stamp=stamp,

   748→    run_log_path = resolve_run_log_path(

   814→        prompt_files=prompt_files,

   818→        stall_warned_batches=stall_warned_batches,
   819→        stall_warning_seconds=stall_warning_seconds,
   820→        append_run_log=append_run_log,

   825→    summary_config = BatchRunSummaryConfig(

   886→    _handle_failures_before_import(
```

```
    77→def _merge_batch_results(batch_results: list[object]) -> dict[str, object]:
    78→    """Deterministically merge assessments/issues across batch outputs."""
    79→    normalized_results: list[batch_core_mod.BatchResultPayload] = []
    80→    for result in batch_results:
    81→        if hasattr(result, "to_dict") and callable(result.to_dict):
    82→            payload = result.to_dict()
    83→            if isinstance(payload, dict):
    84→                normalized_results.append(cast(batch_core_mod.BatchResultPayload, payload))
    85→                continue
    86→        if isinstance(result, dict):
    87→            normalized_results.append(cast(batch_core_mod.BatchResultPayload, result))
    88→    return batch_core_mod.merge_batch_results(
    89→        normalized_results,
    90→        abstraction_sub_axes=ABSTRACTION_SUB_AXES,
    91→        abstraction_component_names=ABSTRACTION_COMPONENT_NAMES,
    92→    )

   242→        prepare_run_artifacts_fn=_prepare_run_artifacts,
   243→        run_codex_batch_fn=lambda *, prompt, repo_root, output_file, log_file: run_codex_batch(
   244→            prompt=prompt,
   245→            repo_root=repo_root,
   246→            output_file=output_file,

   250→                subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
   251→                timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
   252→                safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   253→                use_popen_runner=(getattr(subprocess.run, "__module__", "") == "subprocess"),
   254→                subprocess_popen=subprocess.Popen,
   255→                live_log_interval_seconds=batch_live_log_interval_seconds,
   256→                stall_after_output_seconds=batch_stall_kill_seconds,
   257→                max_retries=batch_max_retries,
   258→                retry_backoff_seconds=batch_retry_backoff_seconds,
   259→            ),
   260→        ),
   261→        execute_batches_fn=execute_batches,
   262→        collect_batch_results_fn=_collect_batch_results,
   263→        print_failures_fn=print_failures,
   264→        print_failures_and_raise_fn=print_failures_and_raise,
   265→        merge_batch_results_fn=_merge_batch_results,
   266→        build_import_provenance_fn=build_batch_import_provenance,
   267→        do_import_fn=_do_import,
   268→        run_followup_scan_fn=lambda *, lang_name, scan_path: run_followup_scan(
   269→            lang_name=lang_name,
   270→            scan_path=scan_path,
   271→            deps=FollowupScanDeps(

   281→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   282→        project_root=runtime_project_root,
   283→        subagent_runs_dir=_subagent_runs_dir(),
   284→    )
   285→
   286→def _validate_run_dir(run_dir: Path) -> tuple[dict, Path, str]:
   287→    """Validate run directory, load summary, and return (summary, blind_packet_path, immutable_packet_path).
   288→
   289→    Raises CommandError on any validation failure.
   290→    """
   291→    if not run_dir.is_dir():
   292→        raise CommandError(f"run directory not found: {run_dir}", exit_code=1)
   293→
   294→    summary_path = run_dir / "run_summary.json"
   295→    if not summary_path.exists():
   296→        raise CommandError(f"no run_summary.json in {run_dir}", exit_code=1)
   297→    try:
   298→        summary = json.loads(summary_path.read_text())
   299→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   300→        raise CommandError(f"Error reading run summary: {exc}", exit_code=1) from exc
   301→
   302→    successful = summary.get("successful_batches", [])
   303→    blind_packet_path = Path(str(summary.get("blind_packet", "")))
   304→    immutable_packet_path = str(summary.get("immutable_packet", ""))
   305→
   306→    if not successful:
   307→        raise CommandError("no successful batches in run summary.", exit_code=1)
   308→    if not blind_packet_path.exists():
   309→        raise PacketValidationError(f"blind packet not found: {blind_packet_path}", exit_code=1)
   310→
   311→    try:
   312→        packet = json.loads(Path(immutable_packet_path).read_text())
   313→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   314→        raise PacketValidationError(f"Error reading immutable packet: {exc}", exit_code=1) from exc
   315→
   316→    summary["_packet"] = packet
   317→    return summary, blind_packet_path, immutable_packet_path
   318→
   319→
   320→def do_import_run(
   321→    run_dir_path: str,
   322→    state: dict,
   323→    lang,
   324→    state_file: str,
   325→    *,
   326→    config: dict | None = None,
   327→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   328→    scan_after_import: bool = False,
   329→    scan_path: str = ".",
   330→) -> None:
   331→    """Re-import results from a completed run directory.
   332→
   333→    Replays the merge+provenance+import step that normally runs at the end of
   334→    ``--run-batches``.  Useful when the original pipeline was interrupted (e.g.
   335→    broken pipe from background execution) but all batch results completed.
   336→    """
   337→    run_dir = Path(run_dir_path)
   338→    summary, blind_packet_path, _immutable_path = _validate_run_dir(run_dir)
   339→
   340→    runner = str(summary.get("runner", "codex"))
   341→    stamp = str(summary.get("run_stamp", ""))
   342→    successful = summary.get("successful_batches", [])
   343→    packet = summary.pop("_packet", {})
   344→    allowed_dims = {str(d) for d in packet.get("dimensions", []) if isinstance(d, str)}
   345→
   346→    # -- locate and parse raw batch results --
   347→    results_dir = run_dir / "results"
   348→    selected_indexes = [idx - 1 for idx in successful]  # convert 1-based to 0-based
   349→    output_files = {
   350→        idx: results_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.raw.txt"
   351→        for idx in selected_indexes
   352→    }
   353→
   354→    missing = [idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes if not output_files[idx].exists()]
   355→    if missing:
   356→        raise CommandError(f"missing result files for batches: {missing}", exit_code=1)
   357→
   358→    batch_results, failures = collect_batch_results(
   359→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   360→        failures=[],
   361→        output_files=output_files,
   362→        allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   363→        extract_payload_fn=lambda raw: batch_core_mod.extract_json_payload(raw, log_fn=log),
   364→        normalize_result_fn=lambda payload, dims: batch_core_mod.normalize_batch_result(
   365→            payload,
   366→            dims,
   367→            max_batch_issues=max_batch_issues_for_dimension_count(len(dims)),
   368→            abstraction_sub_axes=ABSTRACTION_SUB_AXES,
   369→        ),
   370→    )
   371→
   372→    if not batch_results:
   373→        raise CommandError("no valid batch results could be parsed.", exit_code=1)
   374→
   375→    print(colorize(f"  Parsed {len(batch_results)} batch results from {run_dir}", "bold"))
   376→    if failures:
   377→        print(colorize(f"  Warning: {len(failures)} batches failed to parse: {[f + 1 for f in failures]}", "yellow"))
   378→
   379→    # -- merge --
   380→    merged = _merge_batch_results(batch_results)
   381→
   382→    # -- build provenance --
   383→    successful_indexes = [idx for idx in selected_indexes if idx not in set(failures)]
   384→    merged["provenance"] = build_batch_import_provenance(
   385→        runner=runner,
   386→        blind_packet_path=blind_packet_path,
   387→        run_stamp=stamp,
   388→        batch_indexes=successful_indexes,
   389→    )
   390→
   391→    # -- write merged output --
   392→    merged_path = run_dir / "holistic_issues_merged.json"
   393→    safe_write_text(merged_path, json.dumps(merged, indent=2) + "\n")
   394→    print(colorize(f"  Merged output: {merged_path}", "bold"))
   395→
   396→    # -- import with trusted source --
   397→    _do_import(
   398→        str(merged_path),
   399→        state,
   400→        lang,
   401→        state_file,
   402→        config=config,
   403→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   404→        trusted_assessment_source=True,
   405→        trusted_assessment_label=f"trusted import-run replay from {run_dir.name}",
   406→    )
   407→
   408→    # -- optional follow-up scan --
   409→    if scan_after_import:
   410→        lang_name = getattr(lang, "name", None) or str(getattr(lang, "lang", ""))
   411→        if lang_name:
   412→            run_followup_scan(
   413→                lang_name=lang_name,
   414→                scan_path=scan_path,
   415→                deps=FollowupScanDeps(
   416→                    project_root=_runtime_project_root(),
   417→                    timeout_seconds=FOLLOWUP_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
   418→                    python_executable=sys.executable,
   419→                    subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
   420→                    timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
   421→                    colorize_fn=colorize,
   422→                ),
   423→            )
   424→
```

```
    48→def _utc_now() -> datetime:
    49→    return datetime.now(UTC)
    50→
    51→
    52→def _iso_seconds(dt: datetime) -> str:
    53→    return dt.isoformat(timespec="seconds")
    54→
    55→
    56→def _parse_iso(raw: object) -> datetime | None:
    57→    if not isinstance(raw, str) or not raw.strip():
    58→        return None
    59→    try:
    60→        dt = datetime.fromisoformat(raw)
    61→    except ValueError:
    62→        return None
    63→    if dt.tzinfo is None:
    64→        return dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
    65→    return dt.astimezone(UTC)
    66→

    80→def _validate_session_id(session_id: str) -> None:
    81→    if not session_id.strip():
    82→        raise CommandError("Error: --session-id is required.", exit_code=2)
    83→    invalid_chars = {"/", "\\", ".."}
    84→    if any(part in session_id for part in invalid_chars):
    85→        raise CommandError("Error: invalid --session-id value.", exit_code=2)
    86→

    88→def _load_json_object(path: Path, *, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    89→    if not path.exists():
    90→        raise CommandError(f"Error: {label} not found: {path}")
    91→    try:
    92→        payload = json.loads(path.read_text())
    93→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
    94→        raise CommandError(f"Error: failed reading {label}: {exc}") from exc
    95→    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    96→        raise CommandError(f"Error: {label} must contain a JSON object.")
    97→    return payload
    98→

   100→def _session_payload(session_id: str) -> tuple[Path, dict[str, Any]]:
   101→    _validate_session_id(session_id)
   102→    path = _session_file(session_id)

   104→    payload_id = str(payload.get("session_id", "")).strip()
   105→    if payload_id != session_id:
   106→        raise CommandError(
   107→            f"Error: session id mismatch in {path} (expected {session_id}, found {payload_id or '<missing>'}).",

   109→    return path, payload

   112→def _prepare_packet_snapshot(
   113→    args,
   114→    state: dict,
   115→    lang,
   116→    *,
   117→    config: dict[str, Any],
   118→) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], Path, Path]:
   119→    """Prepare holistic review packet and persist immutable+blind snapshots."""
   120→    path = Path(getattr(args, "path", ".") or ".")
   121→    dims = parse_dimensions(args)
   122→    dimensions = list(dims) if dims else None
   123→    retrospective = bool(getattr(args, "retrospective", False))
   124→    retrospective_max_issues = coerce_positive_int(
   125→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_issues", None),
   126→        default=30,
   127→    )
   128→    retrospective_max_batch_items = coerce_positive_int(
   129→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_batch_items", None),
   130→        default=20,
   131→    )
   132→    lang_run, found_files = setup_lang_concrete(lang, path, config)
   133→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   134→        state,
   135→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_run.name, command="review"),
   136→    )
   137→    packet = review_mod.prepare_holistic_review(
   138→        path,
   139→        lang_run,
   140→        state,
   141→        options=review_mod.HolisticReviewPrepareOptions(
   142→            dimensions=dimensions,
   143→            files=found_files or None,
   144→            include_issue_history=retrospective,
   145→            issue_history_max_issues=retrospective_max_issues,
   146→            issue_history_max_batch_items=retrospective_max_batch_items,
   147→        ),
   148→    )
   149→    packet["narrative"] = narrative
   150→    next_command = "desloppify review --external-submit --session-id <id> --import <file>"
   151→    if retrospective:
   152→        next_command += (
   153→            " --retrospective"
   154→            f" --retrospective-max-issues {retrospective_max_issues}"
   155→            f" --retrospective-max-batch-items {retrospective_max_batch_items}"
   156→        )
   157→    packet["next_command"] = next_command
   158→    write_query(packet)
   159→
   160→    stamp = run_stamp()
   161→    blind_packet_path = _blind_packet_path()
   162→    packet_path, blind_path = write_packet_snapshot(
   163→        packet,
   164→        stamp=stamp,
   165→        review_packet_dir=_review_packet_dir(),
   166→        blind_path=blind_packet_path,
   167→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   168→    )
   169→    return packet, packet_path, blind_path

   172→def _build_template_payload(packet: dict[str, Any], *, session_id: str, token: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
   173→    dimensions = [
   174→        dim
   175→        for dim in packet.get("dimensions", [])
   176→        if isinstance(dim, str) and dim.strip()

   178→    return {
   179→        "session": {

   181→            "token": token,
```
