   387→    append_run_log,
   388→):
   389→    def write_run_summary(
   390→        *,
   391→        successful_batches: list[int],
   392→        failed_batches: list[int],
   393→        interrupted: bool = False,
   394→        interruption_reason: str | None = None,
   395→    ) -> None:
   396→        _write_run_summary_impl(
   397→            summary_path=run_summary_path,
   398→            summary_config=summary_config,
   399→            batch_status=batch_status,
   400→            successful_batches=successful_batches,
   401→            failed_batches=failed_batches,
   402→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text_fn,
   403→            colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   404→            append_run_log_fn=append_run_log,
   405→            interrupted=interrupted,
   406→            interruption_reason=interruption_reason,
   407→        )
   408→
   409→    return write_run_summary
   410→
   411→
   412→def _mark_interrupted_batches(
   413→    *,
   414→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   415→    batch_positions: dict[int, int],
   416→    batch_status: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
   417→) -> None:
   418→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   419→        key = str(idx + 1)
   420→        state = batch_status.setdefault(
   421→            key,
   422→            {"position": batch_positions.get(idx, 0), "status": "pending"},
   423→        )
   424→        if state.get("status") in {"pending", "queued", "running"}:
   425→            state["status"] = "interrupted"
   426→
   427→
   428→def _update_batch_statuses(
   429→    *,
   430→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   431→    execution_failures: list[int],
   432→    failures: list[int],
   433→    batch_positions: dict[int, int],
   434→    batch_status: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
   435→    output_files: dict[int, Path],
   436→) -> list[int]:
   437→    execution_failure_set = set(execution_failures)
   438→    failure_set = set(failures)
   439→    successful_indexes = sorted(idx for idx in selected_indexes if idx not in failure_set)
   440→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   441→        key = str(idx + 1)
   442→        state = batch_status.setdefault(
   443→            key,
   444→            {"position": batch_positions.get(idx, 0), "status": "pending"},
   445→        )
   446→        if idx not in failure_set:
   447→            state["status"] = "succeeded"
   448→            continue
   449→        if idx in execution_failure_set:
   450→            state["status"] = "failed"
   451→            continue
   452→        state["status"] = "parse_failed" if output_files[idx].exists() else "missing_output"
   453→    return successful_indexes
   454→
   455→
   456→def _execute_and_collect_results(
   457→    *,
   458→    execute_batches_fn,
   459→    tasks: dict[int, Callable[[], int]],
   460→    run_parallel: bool,
   461→    max_parallel_batches: int,
   462→    heartbeat_seconds: float,
   463→    progress_fn,
   464→    error_log_fn,
   465→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   466→    batch_positions: dict[int, int],
   467→    batch_status: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
   468→    write_run_summary,
   469→    append_run_log,
   470→    collect_batch_results_fn,
   471→    packet: dict[str, object],
   472→    output_files: dict[int, Path],
   473→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, object]], list[int], list[int]]:
   474→    try:
   475→        execution_failures = execute_batches_fn(
   476→            tasks=tasks,
   477→            options=BatchExecutionOptions(
   478→                run_parallel=run_parallel,
   479→                max_parallel_workers=max_parallel_batches,
   480→                heartbeat_seconds=heartbeat_seconds,
   481→            ),
   482→            progress_fn=progress_fn,
   483→            error_log_fn=error_log_fn,
   484→        )
   485→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   486→        _mark_interrupted_batches(
   487→            selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   488→            batch_positions=batch_positions,
   489→            batch_status=batch_status,
   490→        )
   491→        write_run_summary(
   492→            successful_batches=[],
   493→            failed_batches=[],
   494→            interrupted=True,
   495→            interruption_reason="keyboard_interrupt",
   496→        )
   497→        append_run_log("run-interrupted reason=keyboard_interrupt")
   498→        raise SystemExit(130) from None
   499→
   500→    allowed_dims = {str(dim) for dim in packet.get("dimensions", []) if isinstance(dim, str)}
   501→    batch_results, failures = collect_batch_results_fn(
   502→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   503→        failures=execution_failures,
   504→        output_files=output_files,
   505→        allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   506→    )
   507→    return batch_results, failures, execution_failures
   508→
   509→
   510→def _handle_failures_before_import(
   511→    *,
   512→    failures: list[int],
   513→    allow_partial: bool,
   514→    batch_results: list[dict[str, object]],
   515→    immutable_packet_path: Path,
   516→    logs_dir: Path,
   517→    print_failures_fn,
   518→    print_failures_and_raise_fn,
   519→    append_run_log,
   520→    colorize_fn,
   521→    successful_indexes: list[int],
   522→) -> None:
   523→    failure_set = sorted(set(failures))
   524→    if failures and (not allow_partial or not batch_results):
   525→        append_run_log(f"run-finished failures={[idx + 1 for idx in failure_set]} mode=exit")
   526→        print_failures_and_raise_fn(
   527→            failures=failures,
   528→            packet_path=immutable_packet_path,
   529→            logs_dir=logs_dir,
   530→            colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   531→        )
   532→    if not failures:
   533→        return
   534→    print(
   535→        colorize_fn(
   536→            "  Partial completion enabled: importing successful batches and keeping failed batches open.",
   537→            "yellow",
   538→        )
   539→    )
   540→    print_failures_fn(
   541→        failures=failures,
   542→        packet_path=immutable_packet_path,
   543→        logs_dir=logs_dir,
   544→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   545→    )
   546→    append_run_log(
   547→        "run-partial "
   548→        f"successful={[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes]} "
   549→        f"failed={[idx + 1 for idx in failure_set]}"
   550→    )
   551→
   552→
   553→def _merge_and_write_payload(
   554→    *,
   555→    merge_batch_results_fn,
   556→    batch_results: list[dict[str, object]],
   557→    batches: list[dict[str, object]],
   558→    successful_indexes: list[int],
   559→    packet: dict[str, object],
   560→    runner: str,
   561→    prompt_packet_path: Path,
   562→    stamp: str,
   563→    build_import_provenance_fn,
   564→    scored_dimensions: list[str],
   565→    packet_dimensions: list[str],
   566→    scan_path: str,
   567→    run_dir: Path,
   568→    safe_write_text_fn,
   569→    colorize_fn,
   570→) -> Path:
   571→    merged = merge_batch_results_fn(batch_results)
   572→    reviewed_files = collect_reviewed_files_from_batches(
   573→        batches=batches,
   574→        selected_indexes=successful_indexes,
   575→    )
   576→    full_sweep_included = any(
   577→        str(batch.get("name", "")).strip().lower() == "full codebase sweep"
   578→        for idx in successful_indexes
   579→        if 0 <= idx < len(batches)
   580→        for batch in [batches[idx]]
   581→        if isinstance(batch, dict)
   582→    )
   583→
   584→    review_scope: dict[str, object] = {
   585→        "reviewed_files_count": len(reviewed_files),
   586→        "successful_batch_count": len(successful_indexes),
   587→        "full_sweep_included": full_sweep_included,
   588→    }
   589→    total_files = packet.get("total_files")
   590→    if isinstance(total_files, int) and not isinstance(total_files, bool) and total_files > 0:
   591→        review_scope["total_files"] = total_files
   592→    merged["review_scope"] = review_scope
   593→
   594→    if reviewed_files:
   595→        merged["reviewed_files"] = reviewed_files
   596→        print(
   597→            colorize_fn(
   598→                f"  Reviewed files captured for cache refresh: {len(reviewed_files)}",
   599→                "dim",
   600→            )
   601→        )
   602→
   603→    merged["provenance"] = build_import_provenance_fn(
   604→        runner=runner,
   605→        blind_packet_path=prompt_packet_path,
   606→        run_stamp=stamp,
   607→        batch_indexes=successful_indexes,
   608→    )
   609→    merged_assessment_dims = normalize_dimension_list(
   610→        list((merged.get("assessments") or {}).keys())
   611→    )
   612→    merged_issue_dims = normalize_dimension_list(
   613→        [
   614→            issue.get("dimension")
   615→            for issue in (merged.get("issues") or [])
   616→            if isinstance(issue, dict)
   617→        ]
   618→    )
   619→    review_scope["imported_dimensions"] = normalize_dimension_list(
   620→        merged_assessment_dims + merged_issue_dims
   621→    )
   622→
   623→    missing_after_import = print_import_dimension_coverage_notice(
   624→        assessed_dims=merged_assessment_dims,
   625→        scored_dims=scored_dimensions,
   626→        scan_path=scan_path,
   627→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   628→    )
   629→    merged["assessment_coverage"] = {
   630→        "scored_dimensions": scored_dimensions,
   631→        "selected_dimensions": packet_dimensions,
   632→        "imported_dimensions": merged_assessment_dims,
   633→        "missing_dimensions": missing_after_import,
   634→    }
   635→
   636→    merged_path = run_dir / "holistic_issues_merged.json"
   637→    safe_write_text_fn(merged_path, json.dumps(merged, indent=2) + "\n")
   638→    print(colorize_fn(f"\n  Merged outputs: {merged_path}", "bold"))
   639→    print_review_quality(merged.get("review_quality", {}), colorize_fn=colorize_fn)
   640→    return merged_path
   641→
   642→
   643→def _import_merged_payload(
   644→    *,
   645→    merged_path: Path,
   646→    do_import_fn,
   647→    state,
   648→    lang,
   649→    state_file,
   650→    config: dict[str, Any],
   651→    allow_partial: bool,
   652→    append_run_log,
   653→    successful_indexes: list[int],
   654→    failures: list[int],
   655→) -> None:
   656→    try:
   657→        do_import_fn(
   658→            str(merged_path),
   659→            state,
   660→            lang,
   661→            state_file,
   662→            config=config,
   663→            allow_partial=allow_partial,
   664→            trusted_assessment_source=True,
   665→            trusted_assessment_label="trusted internal run-batches import",
   666→        )
   667→    except SystemExit as exc:
   668→        append_run_log(f"run-finished import-failed code={exc.code}")
   669→        raise
   670→    except Exception as exc:
   671→        append_run_log(f"run-finished import-error error={exc}")
   672→        raise
   673→
   674→    append_run_log(
   675→        "run-finished "
   676→        f"successful={[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes]} "
   677→        f"failed={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(set(failures))]} imported={str(merged_path)}"
   678→    )
   679→
   680→
   681→def _run_followup_scan_if_requested(
   682→    *,
   683→    args,
   684→    lang,
   685→    run_followup_scan_fn,
   686→) -> None:
   687→    if not getattr(args, "scan_after_import", False):
   688→        return
   689→    followup_code = run_followup_scan_fn(
   690→        lang_name=lang.name,
   691→        scan_path=str(args.path),
   692→    )
   693→    if followup_code != 0:
   694→        raise CommandError(
   695→            f"Error: follow-up scan failed with exit code {followup_code}.",
   696→            exit_code=followup_code,
   697→        )
   698→
   699→
   700→def _prepare_run_setup(
   701→    *,
   702→    args,
   703→    state,
   704→    lang,
   705→    config: dict[str, Any] | None,
   706→    run_stamp_fn,
   707→    load_or_prepare_packet_fn,
   708→    selected_batch_indexes_fn,
   709→    colorize_fn,
   710→) -> _RunSetup:
   711→    resolved_config = config or {}
   712→    runner = getattr(args, "runner", "codex")
   713→    validate_runner(runner, colorize_fn=colorize_fn)
   714→    allow_partial = bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False))
   715→
   716→    policy = resolve_batch_run_policy(args)
   717→    stamp = run_stamp_fn()
   718→    packet, immutable_packet_path, prompt_packet_path = load_or_prepare_packet_fn(
   719→        args,
   720→        state=state,
   721→        lang=lang,
   722→        config=resolved_config,
   723→        stamp=stamp,
   724→    )
   725→
   726→    scan_path = str(getattr(args, "path", ".") or ".")
   727→    packet_dimensions = normalize_dimension_list(packet.get("dimensions", []))
   728→    scored_dimensions = scored_dimensions_for_lang(lang.name)
   729→    print_preflight_dimension_scope_notice(
   730→        selected_dims=packet_dimensions,
   731→        scored_dims=scored_dimensions,
   732→        explicit_selection=bool(getattr(args, "dimensions", None)),
   733→        scan_path=scan_path,
   734→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   735→    )
   736→    batches = require_batches(
   737→        packet,
   738→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   739→        suggested_prepare_cmd=f"desloppify review --prepare --path {scan_path}",
   740→    )
   741→    selected_indexes = selected_batch_indexes_fn(args, batch_count=len(batches))
   742→    total_batches = len(selected_indexes)
   743→    return _RunSetup(
   744→        config=resolved_config,
   745→        runner=runner,
   746→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   747→        run_parallel=policy.run_parallel,
   748→        max_parallel_batches=policy.max_parallel_batches,
   749→        heartbeat_seconds=policy.heartbeat_seconds,
   750→        batch_timeout_seconds=policy.batch_timeout_seconds,
   751→        batch_max_retries=policy.batch_max_retries,
   752→        batch_retry_backoff_seconds=policy.batch_retry_backoff_seconds,
   753→        stall_warning_seconds=policy.stall_warning_seconds,
   754→        stall_kill_seconds=policy.stall_kill_seconds,
   755→        stamp=stamp,
   756→        packet=packet,
   757→        immutable_packet_path=immutable_packet_path,
   758→        prompt_packet_path=prompt_packet_path,
   759→        scan_path=scan_path,
   760→        packet_dimensions=packet_dimensions,
   761→        scored_dimensions=scored_dimensions,
   762→        batches=batches,
   763→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   764→        total_batches=total_batches,
   765→    )
   766→
   767→
   768→def _prepare_execution_state(
   769→    *,
   770→    args,
   771→    setup: _RunSetup,
   772→    prepare_run_artifacts_fn,
   773→    run_codex_batch_fn,
   774→    project_root: Path,
   775→    subagent_runs_dir: Path,
   776→    safe_write_text_fn,
   777→    colorize_fn,
   778→) -> _ExecutionState | None:
   779→    effective_workers, worst_case_minutes = _runtime_expectation(
   780→        total_batches=setup.total_batches,
   781→        run_parallel=setup.run_parallel,
   782→        max_parallel_batches=setup.max_parallel_batches,
   783→        batch_timeout_seconds=setup.batch_timeout_seconds,
   784→    )
   785→    _print_runtime_expectation(
   786→        total_batches=setup.total_batches,
   787→        effective_workers=effective_workers,
   788→        batch_timeout_seconds=setup.batch_timeout_seconds,
   789→        worst_case_minutes=worst_case_minutes,
   790→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   791→    )
   792→    run_dir, logs_dir, prompt_files, output_files, log_files = prepare_run_artifacts_fn(
   793→        stamp=setup.stamp,
   794→        selected_indexes=setup.selected_indexes,
   795→        batches=setup.batches,
   796→        packet_path=setup.prompt_packet_path,
   797→        run_root=subagent_runs_dir,
   798→        repo_root=project_root,
   799→    )
   800→    run_log_path = resolve_run_log_path(
   801→        getattr(args, "run_log_file", None),
   802→        project_root=project_root,
   803→        run_dir=run_dir,
   804→    )
   805→    append_run_log = make_run_log_writer(run_log_path)
   806→    _log_run_start(
   807→        append_run_log=append_run_log,
   808→        runner=setup.runner,
   809→        run_parallel=setup.run_parallel,
   810→        max_parallel_batches=setup.max_parallel_batches,
   811→        batch_timeout_seconds=setup.batch_timeout_seconds,
   812→        heartbeat_seconds=setup.heartbeat_seconds,
   813→        stall_warning_seconds=setup.stall_warning_seconds,
   814→        stall_kill_seconds=setup.stall_kill_seconds,
   815→        batch_max_retries=setup.batch_max_retries,
   816→        batch_retry_backoff_seconds=setup.batch_retry_backoff_seconds,
   817→        worst_case_minutes=worst_case_minutes,
   818→        selected_indexes=setup.selected_indexes,
   819→        run_dir=run_dir,
   820→        immutable_packet_path=setup.immutable_packet_path,
   821→        prompt_packet_path=setup.prompt_packet_path,
   822→    )
   823→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Live run log: {run_log_path}", "dim"))
   824→    if _handle_dry_run(
   825→        args=args,
   826→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   827→        immutable_packet_path=setup.immutable_packet_path,
   828→        prompt_packet_path=setup.prompt_packet_path,
   829→        run_dir=run_dir,
   830→        append_run_log=append_run_log,
   831→    ):
   832→        return None
   833→
   834→    tasks = build_batch_tasks(
   835→        selected_indexes=setup.selected_indexes,
   836→        prompt_files=prompt_files,
   837→        output_files=output_files,
   838→        log_files=log_files,
   839→        project_root=project_root,
   840→        run_codex_batch_fn=run_codex_batch_fn,
   841→    )
   842→    summary_created_at, batch_positions, stall_warned_batches, batch_status = _build_batch_tracking(
   843→        selected_indexes=setup.selected_indexes,
   844→        prompt_files=prompt_files,
   845→        output_files=output_files,
   846→        log_files=log_files,
   847→    )
   848→    if setup.run_parallel:
   849→        print(
   850→            colorize_fn(
   851→                "  Parallel runner config: "
   852→                f"max-workers={min(setup.total_batches, setup.max_parallel_batches)}, "
   853→                f"heartbeat={setup.heartbeat_seconds:.1f}s",
   854→                "dim",
   855→            )
   856→        )
   857→    progress_fn = _build_progress_reporter(
   858→        batch_positions=batch_positions,
   859→        total_batches=setup.total_batches,
   860→        prompt_files=prompt_files,
   861→        output_files=output_files,
   862→        log_files=log_files,
   863→        batch_status=batch_status,
   864→        stall_warned_batches=stall_warned_batches,
   865→        stall_warning_seconds=setup.stall_warning_seconds,
   866→        append_run_log=append_run_log,
   867→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   868→    )
   869→    run_summary_path = run_dir / "run_summary.json"
   870→    summary_config = BatchRunSummaryConfig(
   871→        created_at=summary_created_at,
   872→        run_stamp=setup.stamp,
   873→        runner=setup.runner,
   874→        run_parallel=setup.run_parallel,
   875→        selected_indexes=setup.selected_indexes,
   876→        allow_partial=setup.allow_partial,
   877→        max_parallel_batches=setup.max_parallel_batches,
   878→        batch_timeout_seconds=setup.batch_timeout_seconds,
   879→        batch_max_retries=setup.batch_max_retries,
   880→        batch_retry_backoff_seconds=setup.batch_retry_backoff_seconds,
   881→        heartbeat_seconds=setup.heartbeat_seconds,
   882→        stall_warning_seconds=setup.stall_warning_seconds,
   883→        stall_kill_seconds=setup.stall_kill_seconds,
   884→        immutable_packet_path=setup.immutable_packet_path,
   885→        prompt_packet_path=setup.prompt_packet_path,
   886→        run_dir=run_dir,
   887→        logs_dir=logs_dir,
   888→        run_log_path=run_log_path,
   889→    )
   890→    write_run_summary = _build_summary_writer(
   891→        run_summary_path=run_summary_path,
   892→        summary_config=summary_config,
   893→        batch_status=batch_status,
   894→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text_fn,
   895→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   896→        append_run_log=append_run_log,
   897→    )
   898→    record_execution_issue = partial(_record_execution_issue, append_run_log)
   899→    return _ExecutionState(
   900→        run_dir=run_dir,
   901→        logs_dir=logs_dir,
   902→        prompt_files=prompt_files,
   903→        output_files=output_files,
   904→        log_files=log_files,
   905→        append_run_log=append_run_log,
   906→        write_run_summary=write_run_summary,
   907→        tasks=tasks,
   908→        batch_positions=batch_positions,
   909→        batch_status=batch_status,
   910→        progress_fn=progress_fn,
   911→        record_execution_issue=record_execution_issue,
   912→    )
   913→
   914→
   915→def do_run_batches(
   916→    args,
   917→    state,
   918→    lang,
   919→    state_file,
   920→    *,
   921→    config: dict[str, Any] | None,
   922→    run_stamp_fn,
   923→    load_or_prepare_packet_fn,
   924→    selected_batch_indexes_fn,
   925→    prepare_run_artifacts_fn,
   926→    run_codex_batch_fn,
   927→    execute_batches_fn,
   928→    collect_batch_results_fn,
   929→    print_failures_fn,
   930→    print_failures_and_raise_fn,
   931→    merge_batch_results_fn,
   932→    build_import_provenance_fn,
   933→    do_import_fn,
   934→    run_followup_scan_fn,
   935→    safe_write_text_fn,
   936→    colorize_fn,
   937→    project_root: Path,
   938→    subagent_runs_dir: Path,
   939→) -> None:
   940→    """Run holistic investigation batches with a local subagent runner."""
   941→    setup = _prepare_run_setup(
   942→        args=args,
   943→        state=state,
   944→        lang=lang,
   945→        config=config,
   946→        run_stamp_fn=run_stamp_fn,
   947→        load_or_prepare_packet_fn=load_or_prepare_packet_fn,
   948→        selected_batch_indexes_fn=selected_batch_indexes_fn,
   949→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   950→    )
   951→    execution = _prepare_execution_state(
   952→        args=args,
   953→        setup=setup,
   954→        prepare_run_artifacts_fn=prepare_run_artifacts_fn,
   955→        run_codex_batch_fn=run_codex_batch_fn,
   956→        project_root=project_root,
   957→        subagent_runs_dir=subagent_runs_dir,
   958→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text_fn,
   959→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   960→    )
   961→    if execution is None:
   962→        return
   963→
   964→    batch_results, failures, execution_failures = _execute_and_collect_results(
   965→        execute_batches_fn=execute_batches_fn,
   966→        tasks=execution.tasks,
   967→        run_parallel=setup.run_parallel,
   968→        max_parallel_batches=setup.max_parallel_batches,
   969→        heartbeat_seconds=setup.heartbeat_seconds,
   970→        progress_fn=execution.progress_fn,
   971→        error_log_fn=execution.record_execution_issue,
   972→        selected_indexes=setup.selected_indexes,
   973→        batch_positions=execution.batch_positions,
   974→        batch_status=execution.batch_status,
   975→        write_run_summary=execution.write_run_summary,
   976→        append_run_log=execution.append_run_log,
   977→        collect_batch_results_fn=collect_batch_results_fn,
   978→        packet=setup.packet,
   979→        output_files=execution.output_files,
   980→    )
   981→    successful_indexes = _update_batch_statuses(
   982→        selected_indexes=setup.selected_indexes,
   983→        execution_failures=execution_failures,
   984→        failures=failures,
   985→        batch_positions=execution.batch_positions,
   986→        batch_status=execution.batch_status,
   987→        output_files=execution.output_files,
   988→    )
   989→    execution.write_run_summary(
   990→        successful_batches=[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes],
   991→        failed_batches=[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(set(failures))],
   992→    )
   993→    _handle_failures_before_import(
   994→        failures=failures,
   995→        allow_partial=setup.allow_partial,
   996→        batch_results=batch_results,
   997→        immutable_packet_path=setup.immutable_packet_path,
   998→        logs_dir=execution.logs_dir,

1→"""Packet sanitization, hashing, and artifact layout helpers for review batches."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from copy import deepcopy
     7→from datetime import UTC, datetime
     8→from hashlib import sha256
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→from typing import Any
    11→
    12→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PacketValidationError
    13→
    14→_BLIND_PACKET_DROP_KEYS = {
    15→    "narrative",
    16→    "next_command",
    17→    "score_snapshot",
    18→    "strict_target",
    19→    "strict_target_progress",
    20→    "subjective_at_target",
    21→}
    22→
    23→_BLIND_CONFIG_SCORE_HINT_KEYS = {
    24→    "target_strict_score",
    25→    "strict_target_score",
    26→    "target_score",
    27→    "strict_score",
    28→    "objective_score",
    29→    "overall_score",
    30→    "verified_strict_score",
    31→}
    32→
    33→
    34→def write_packet_snapshot(
    35→    packet: dict[str, Any],
    36→    *,
    37→    stamp: str,
    38→    review_packet_dir: Path,
    39→    blind_path: Path,
    40→    safe_write_text_fn,
    41→) -> tuple[Path, Path]:
    42→    """Persist immutable and blind packet snapshots for runner workflows."""
    43→    review_packet_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    44→    packet_path = review_packet_dir / f"holistic_packet_{stamp}.json"
    45→    safe_write_text_fn(packet_path, json.dumps(packet, indent=2) + "\n")
    46→    blind_packet = _build_blind_packet(packet)
    47→    safe_write_text_fn(blind_path, json.dumps(blind_packet, indent=2) + "\n")
    48→    return packet_path, blind_path
    49→
    50→
    51→def _build_blind_packet(packet: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    52→    """Return a blind-review packet with score anchoring metadata removed."""
    53→    blind = deepcopy(packet)
    54→    for key in _BLIND_PACKET_DROP_KEYS:
    55→        blind.pop(key, None)
    56→
    57→    config = blind.get("config")
    58→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    59→        sanitized = _sanitize_blind_config(config)
    60→        if sanitized:
    61→            blind["config"] = sanitized
    62→        else:
    63→            blind.pop("config", None)
    64→    return blind
    65→
    66→
    67→def build_blind_packet(packet: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    68→    """Public wrapper for blind packet sanitization."""
    69→    return _build_blind_packet(packet)
    70→
    71→
    72→def run_stamp() -> str:
    73→    """Stable UTC run stamp for artifact paths."""
    74→    return datetime.now(UTC).strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    75→
    76→
    77→def _sanitize_blind_config(config: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    78→    """Drop score/target hints from config while preserving unrelated options."""
    79→    sanitized: dict[str, Any] = {}
    80→    for key, value in config.items():
    81→        lowered = key.strip().lower()
    82→        if not lowered:
    83→            continue
    84→        if lowered in _BLIND_CONFIG_SCORE_HINT_KEYS:
    85→            continue
    86→        if "target" in lowered:
    87→            continue
    88→        if lowered.endswith("_score"):
    89→            continue
    90→        sanitized[key] = value
    91→    return sanitized
    92→
    93→
    94→def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str | None:
    95→    """Compute sha256 hex digest for path contents (or None on read failure)."""
    96→    try:
    97→        data = path.read_bytes()
    98→    except OSError:
    99→        return None
   100→    return sha256(data).hexdigest()
   101→
   102→
   103→def build_batch_import_provenance(
   104→    *,
   105→    runner: str,
   106→    blind_packet_path: Path,
   107→    run_stamp: str,
   108→    batch_indexes: list[int],
   109→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   110→    """Build provenance payload used to trust assessment-bearing imports."""
   111→    packet_hash = sha256_file(blind_packet_path)
   112→    batch_indexes_1 = sorted({int(index) + 1 for index in batch_indexes})
   113→    return {
   114→        "kind": "blind_review_batch_import",
   115→        "blind": True,
   116→        "runner": runner,
   117→        "run_stamp": run_stamp,
   118→        "created_at": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
   119→        "batch_count": len(batch_indexes_1),
   120→        "batch_indexes": batch_indexes_1,
   121→        "packet_path": str(blind_packet_path),
   122→        "packet_sha256": packet_hash,
   123→    }
   124→
   125→
   126→def selected_batch_indexes(
   127→    *,
   128→    raw_selection: str | None,
   129→    batch_count: int,
   130→    parse_fn,
   131→    colorize_fn,
   132→) -> list[int]:
   133→    """Validate selected batch indexes or raise CommandError."""
   134→    try:
   135→        selected = parse_fn(raw_selection, batch_count)
   136→    except ValueError as exc:
   137→        raise PacketValidationError(str(exc), exit_code=2) from exc
   138→    if selected:
   139→        return selected
   140→    raise PacketValidationError("no batches selected", exit_code=2)
   141→
   142→
   143→def prepare_run_artifacts(
   144→    *,
   145→    stamp: str,
   146→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   147→    batches: list[dict[str, Any]],
   148→    packet_path: Path,
   149→    run_root: Path,
   150→    repo_root: Path,
   151→    build_prompt_fn,
   152→    safe_write_text_fn,
   153→    colorize_fn,
   154→) -> tuple[Path, Path, dict[int, Path], dict[int, Path], dict[int, Path]]:
   155→    """Build prompt/output/log paths and persist prompts for selected batches."""
   156→    run_dir = run_root / stamp
   157→    prompts_dir = run_dir / "prompts"
   158→    results_dir = run_dir / "results"
   159→    logs_dir = run_dir / "logs"
   160→    prompts_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   161→    results_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   162→    logs_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   163→
   164→    selected_1_based = [idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes]
   165→    print(colorize_fn(f"\n  Running holistic batches: {selected_1_based}", "bold"))
   166→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Run artifacts: {run_dir}", "dim"))
   167→
   168→    prompt_files: dict[int, Path] = {}
   169→    output_files: dict[int, Path] = {}
   170→    log_files: dict[int, Path] = {}
   171→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   172→        batch = batches[idx] if isinstance(batches[idx], dict) else {}
   173→        prompt_text = build_prompt_fn(
   174→            repo_root=repo_root,
   175→            packet_path=packet_path,
   176→            batch_index=idx,
   177→            batch=batch,
   178→        )
   179→        prompt_file = prompts_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.md"
   180→        output_file = results_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.raw.txt"
   181→        log_file = logs_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.log"
   182→        safe_write_text_fn(prompt_file, prompt_text)
   183→        prompt_files[idx] = prompt_file
   184→        output_files[idx] = output_file
   185→        log_files[idx] = log_file
   186→    return run_dir, logs_dir, prompt_files, output_files, log_files
   187→
   188→
   189→__all__ = [
   190→    "build_batch_import_provenance",
   191→    "build_blind_packet",
   192→    "prepare_run_artifacts",
   193→    "run_stamp",
   194→    "selected_batch_indexes",
   195→    "sha256_file",
   196→    "write_packet_snapshot",
   197→]
   198→

1→"""Subprocess-oriented runner helpers for review batch execution."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→import sys
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from ._runner_process_attempts import (
    10→    _handle_early_attempt_return,
    11→    _handle_failed_attempt,
    12→    _handle_successful_attempt,
    13→    _handle_timeout_or_stall,
    14→    _resolve_retry_config,
    15→    _run_batch_attempt,
    16→)
    17→from ._runner_process_io import _extract_payload_from_log  # noqa: F401 (runner_parallel import)
    18→from ._runner_process_types import (
    19→    CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
    20→    FollowupScanDeps,
    21→)
    22→
    23→
    24→def codex_batch_command(*, prompt: str, repo_root: Path, output_file: Path) -> list[str]:
    25→    """Build one codex exec command line for a batch prompt."""
    26→    effort = os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_CODEX_REASONING_EFFORT", "low").strip().lower()
    27→    if effort not in {"low", "medium", "high", "xhigh"}:
    28→        effort = "low"
    29→    return [
    30→        "codex",
    31→        "exec",
    32→        "--ephemeral",
    33→        "-C",
    34→        str(repo_root),
    35→        "-s",
    36→        "workspace-write",
    37→        "-c",
    38→        'approval_policy="never"',
    39→        "-c",
    40→        f'model_reasoning_effort="{effort}"',
    41→        "-o",
    42→        str(output_file),
    43→        prompt,
    44→    ]
    45→
    46→
    47→def run_codex_batch(
    48→    *,
    49→    prompt: str,
    50→    repo_root: Path,
    51→    output_file: Path,
    52→    log_file: Path,
    53→    deps: CodexBatchRunnerDeps,
    54→    codex_batch_command_fn=None,
    55→) -> int:
    56→    """Execute one codex batch and return a stable CLI-style status code."""
    57→    if codex_batch_command_fn is None:
    58→        codex_batch_command_fn = codex_batch_command
    59→    cmd = codex_batch_command_fn(
    60→        prompt=prompt,
    61→        repo_root=repo_root,
    62→        output_file=output_file,
    63→    )
    64→    config = _resolve_retry_config(deps)
    65→    log_sections: list[str] = []
    66→
    67→    for attempt in range(1, config.max_attempts + 1):
    68→        header, result = _run_batch_attempt(
    69→            cmd=cmd,
    70→            deps=deps,
    71→            output_file=output_file,
    72→            log_file=log_file,
    73→            log_sections=log_sections,
    74→            attempt=attempt,
    75→            max_attempts=config.max_attempts,
    76→            use_popen=config.use_popen,
    77→            live_log_interval=config.live_log_interval,
    78→            stall_seconds=config.stall_seconds,
    79→        )
    80→        early_return = _handle_early_attempt_return(result)
    81→        if early_return is not None:
    82→            return early_return
    83→        timeout_or_stall = _handle_timeout_or_stall(
    84→            header=header,
    85→            result=result,
    86→            deps=deps,
    87→            output_file=output_file,
    88→            log_file=log_file,
    89→            log_sections=log_sections,
    90→            stall_seconds=config.stall_seconds,
    91→        )
    92→        if timeout_or_stall is not None:
    93→            return timeout_or_stall
    94→
    95→        log_sections.append(
    96→            f"{header}\n\nSTDOUT:\n{result.stdout_text}\n\nSTDERR:\n{result.stderr_text}\n"
    97→        )
    98→
    99→        success_code = _handle_successful_attempt(
   100→            result=result,
   101→            output_file=output_file,
   102→            log_file=log_file,
   103→            deps=deps,
   104→            log_sections=log_sections,
   105→        )
   106→        if success_code is not None:
   107→            return success_code
   108→        failure_code = _handle_failed_attempt(
   109→            result=result,
   110→            deps=deps,
   111→            attempt=attempt,
   112→            max_attempts=config.max_attempts,
   113→            retry_backoff_seconds=config.retry_backoff_seconds,
   114→            log_file=log_file,
   115→            log_sections=log_sections,
   116→        )
   117→        if failure_code is not None:
   118→            return failure_code
   119→
   120→    deps.safe_write_text_fn(log_file, "\n\n".join(log_sections))
   121→    return 1
   122→
   123→
   124→def run_followup_scan(
   125→    *,
   126→    lang_name: str,
   127→    scan_path: str,
   128→    deps: FollowupScanDeps,
   129→    force_queue_bypass: bool = False,
   130→) -> int:
   131→    """Run a follow-up scan and return a non-zero status when it fails."""
   132→    scan_cmd = [
   133→        deps.python_executable,
   134→        "-m",
   135→        "desloppify",
   136→        "--lang",
   137→        lang_name,
   138→        "scan",
   139→        "--path",
   140→        scan_path,
   141→    ]
   142→    if force_queue_bypass:
   143→        followup_attest = (
   144→            "I understand this is not the intended workflow and "
   145→            "I am intentionally skipping queue completion"
   146→        )
   147→        scan_cmd.extend(["--force-rescan", "--attest", followup_attest])
   148→        print(
   149→            deps.colorize_fn(
   150→                "  Follow-up scan queue bypass enabled (--force-followup-scan).",
   151→                "yellow",
   152→            )
   153→        )
   154→    print(deps.colorize_fn("\n  Running follow-up scan...", "bold"))
   155→    try:
   156→        result = deps.subprocess_run(
   157→            scan_cmd,
   158→            cwd=str(deps.project_root),
   159→            timeout=deps.timeout_seconds,
   160→        )
   161→    except deps.timeout_error:
   162→        print(
   163→            deps.colorize_fn(
   164→                f"  Follow-up scan timed out after {deps.timeout_seconds}s.",
   165→                "yellow",
   166→            ),
   167→            file=sys.stderr,
   168→        )
   169→        return 124
   170→    except OSError as exc:
   171→        print(
   172→            deps.colorize_fn(f"  Follow-up scan failed: {exc}", "red"),
   173→            file=sys.stderr,
   174→        )
   175→        return 1
   176→    return int(getattr(result, "returncode", 0) or 0)
   177→
   178→
   179→__all__ = [
   180→    "CodexBatchRunnerDeps",
   181→    "FollowupScanDeps",
   182→    "_extract_payload_from_log",
   183→    "codex_batch_command",
   184→    "run_codex_batch",
   185→    "run_followup_scan",
   186→]
   187→

    92→            "dimension": self.dimension,

   109→def parse_batch_selection(raw: str | None, batch_count: int) -> list[int]:
   110→    """Parse optional 1-based CSV list of batches."""
   111→    if not raw:
   112→        return list(range(batch_count))
   113→
   114→    selected: list[int] = []
   115→    seen: set[int] = set()
   116→    for token in raw.split(","):
   117→        text = token.strip()
   118→        if not text:
   119→            continue
   120→        idx_1 = int(text)
   121→        if idx_1 < 1 or idx_1 > batch_count:
   122→            raise ValueError(f"batch index {idx_1} out of range 1..{batch_count}")
   123→        idx_0 = idx_1 - 1
   124→        if idx_0 in seen:
   125→            continue
   126→        seen.add(idx_0)
   127→        selected.append(idx_0)
   128→    return selected
   129→
   130→
   131→def extract_json_payload(raw: str, *, log_fn) -> dict[str, object] | None:
   132→    """Best-effort extraction of first JSON object from agent output text."""
   133→    text = raw.strip()
   134→    if not text:
   135→        return None
   136→
   137→    decoder = json.JSONDecoder()
   138→    last_decode_error: json.JSONDecodeError | None = None
   139→    for start, ch in enumerate(text):
   140→        if ch not in "{[":
   141→            continue
   142→        try:
   143→            obj, _ = decoder.raw_decode(text[start:])
   144→        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
   145→            last_decode_error = exc
   146→            continue
   147→        if (
   148→            isinstance(obj, dict)
   149→            and isinstance(obj.get("assessments"), dict)
   150→            and isinstance(obj.get("issues"), list)
   151→        ):
   152→            return obj
   153→    if last_decode_error is not None:
   154→        log_fn(f"  batch output JSON parse failed: {last_decode_error.msg}")
   155→    else:

   165→
   166→    Returns (evidence, impact_scope, fix_scope, confidence, issues_preventing_higher_score).
   167→    Raises ValueError on invalid structure.
   168→    """
   169→    if not isinstance(note_raw, dict):
   170→        raise ValueError(
   171→            f"dimension_notes missing object for assessed dimension: {key}"
   172→        )
   173→    evidence = note_raw.get("evidence")
   174→    impact_scope = note_raw.get("impact_scope")
   175→    fix_scope = note_raw.get("fix_scope")
   176→    if not isinstance(evidence, list) or not evidence:
   177→        raise ValueError(
   178→            f"dimension_notes.{key}.evidence must be a non-empty array"
   179→        )
   180→    if not isinstance(impact_scope, str) or not impact_scope.strip():
   181→        raise ValueError(
   182→            f"dimension_notes.{key}.impact_scope must be a non-empty string"
   183→        )
   184→    if not isinstance(fix_scope, str) or not fix_scope.strip():
   185→        raise ValueError(
   186→            f"dimension_notes.{key}.fix_scope must be a non-empty string"
   187→        )
   188→
   189→    confidence_raw = str(note_raw.get("confidence", "medium")).strip().lower()
   190→    confidence = (
   191→        confidence_raw if confidence_raw in {"high", "medium", "low"} else "medium"
   192→    )
   193→    issues_note = str(note_raw.get(DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY, "")).strip()
   194→    if not issues_note:
   195→        issues_note = str(note_raw.get(LEGACY_DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY, "")).strip()
   196→    return evidence, impact_scope, fix_scope, confidence, issues_note

1→"""Parallel execution and progress-callback helpers for review batches."""
     2→

     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import threading
     8→from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→
    11→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import safe_write_text
    12→
    13→from ._runner_parallel_execution import (
    14→    _drain_parallel_completions,
    15→    _execute_serial,
    16→    _queue_parallel_tasks,
    17→    _resolve_parallel_runtime,
    18→)
    19→from ._runner_parallel_progress import (
    20→    _coerce_batch_execution_options,
    21→)
    22→from ._runner_parallel_types import (
    23→    BatchExecutionOptions,
    24→    BatchProgressEvent,

    33→def execute_batches(
    34→    *,
    35→    tasks: dict[int, BatchTask],
    36→    options: BatchExecutionOptions | None = None,
    37→    progress_fn=None,
    38→    error_log_fn=None,
    39→) -> list[int]:
    40→    """Run indexed tasks and return failed index list.
    41→
    42→    Each value in *tasks* is a zero-arg callable returning an int exit code.
    43→    All domain knowledge (files, prompts, etc.) is pre-bound by the caller.
    44→    """
    45→    resolved_options = _coerce_batch_execution_options(options)
    46→    contract_cache: dict[int, str] = {}
    47→    indexes = sorted(tasks)
    48→    if resolved_options.run_parallel:
    49→        max_workers, heartbeat = _resolve_parallel_runtime(
    50→            indexes=indexes,
    51→            max_parallel_workers=resolved_options.max_parallel_workers,
    52→            heartbeat_seconds=resolved_options.heartbeat_seconds,
    53→        )
    54→        failures: set[int] = set()
    55→        progress_failures: set[int] = set()
    56→        started_at: dict[int, float] = {}
    57→        lock = threading.Lock()
    58→        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=max_workers) as executor:
    59→            futures = _queue_parallel_tasks(
    60→                executor=executor,
    61→                indexes=indexes,
    62→                tasks=tasks,
    63→                progress_fn=progress_fn,
    64→                error_log_fn=error_log_fn,
    65→                contract_cache=contract_cache,
    66→                max_workers=max_workers,
    67→                failures=failures,
    68→                progress_failures=progress_failures,
    69→                started_at=started_at,
    70→                lock=lock,
    71→                clock_fn=resolved_options.clock_fn,
    72→            )
    73→            pending = set(futures.keys())
    74→            _drain_parallel_completions(
    75→                pending=pending,
    76→                futures=futures,
    77→                heartbeat=heartbeat,
    78→                indexes=indexes,
    79→                progress_fn=progress_fn,
    80→                error_log_fn=error_log_fn,
    81→                contract_cache=contract_cache,
    82→                failures=failures,
    83→                progress_failures=progress_failures,
    84→                started_at=started_at,
    85→                lock=lock,
    86→                clock_fn=resolved_options.clock_fn,
    87→            )
    88→        return sorted(failures)
    89→    return _execute_serial(
    90→        tasks=tasks,
    91→        indexes=indexes,
    92→        progress_fn=progress_fn,
    93→        error_log_fn=error_log_fn,
    94→        clock_fn=resolved_options.clock_fn,
    95→        contract_cache=contract_cache,
    96→    )

    99→def collect_batch_results(
   100→    *,
   101→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   102→    failures: list[int],
   103→    output_files: dict[int, Path],
   104→    allowed_dims: set[str],
   105→    extract_payload_fn,
   106→    normalize_result_fn,
   107→) -> tuple[list[BatchResult], list[int]]:
   108→    """Parse and normalize batch outputs, preserving prior failures."""
   109→    batch_results: list[BatchResult] = []
   110→    failure_set = set(failures)
   111→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   112→        had_execution_failure = idx in failure_set
   113→        raw_path = output_files[idx]
   114→        payload = None
   115→        parsed_from_log = False
   116→        if raw_path.exists():
   117→            try:
   118→                payload = extract_payload_fn(raw_path.read_text())
   119→            except OSError as exc:
   120→                logger.debug("Failed reading batch payload %s: %s", raw_path, exc)
   121→                payload = None
   122→        if payload is None:
   123→            payload = _extract_payload_from_log(idx, raw_path, extract_payload_fn)
   124→            parsed_from_log = payload is not None
   125→        if payload is None:
   126→            failure_set.add(idx)
   127→            continue
   128→        if parsed_from_log:
   129→            try:
   130→                safe_write_text(raw_path, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   131→            except OSError as exc:
   132→                logger.error("Failed writing normalized batch payload %s: %s", raw_path, exc)
   133→                raise
   134→        try:
   135→            assessments, issues, dimension_notes, quality = normalize_result_fn(
   136→                payload,
   137→                allowed_dims,
   138→            )
   139→        except ValueError as exc:
   140→            logger.debug("Invalid batch payload at index %s (%s): %s", idx, raw_path, exc)
   141→            failure_set.add(idx)
   142→            continue
   143→        if had_execution_failure:
   144→            failure_set.discard(idx)
   145→        batch_results.append(
   146→            BatchResult(
   147→                batch_index=idx + 1,
   148→                assessments=assessments,
   149→                dimension_notes=dimension_notes,
   150→                issues=issues,

   231→def _normalize_issues(
   232→    raw_issues: object,
   233→    dimension_notes: dict[str, BatchDimensionNotePayload],
   234→    *,
   235→    max_batch_issues: int,

   238→) -> list[NormalizedBatchIssue]:
   239→    """Validate and normalize the issues array from a batch payload."""
   240→    if not isinstance(raw_issues, list):
   241→        raise ValueError("issues must be an array")
   242→
   243→    issues: list[NormalizedBatchIssue] = []
   244→    errors: list[str] = []
   245→    for idx, item in enumerate(raw_issues):
   246→        issue: ReviewIssuePayload | None
   247→        issue, issue_errors = validate_review_issue_payload(
   248→            item,
   249→            label=f"issues[{idx}]",
   250→            allowed_dimensions=allowed_dims,
   251→            allow_dismissed=False,
   252→        )
   253→        if issue_errors:
   254→            errors.extend(issue_errors)
   255→            continue
   256→        if issue is None:
   257→            raise ValueError(

   259→            )
   260→
   261→        dim = issue["dimension"]
   262→        note = dimension_notes.get(dim, {})
   263→        impact_scope = str(
   264→            (item if isinstance(item, dict) else {}).get(
   265→                "impact_scope", note.get("impact_scope", "")
   266→            )
   267→        ).strip()
   268→        fix_scope = str(
   269→            (item if isinstance(item, dict) else {}).get(
   270→                "fix_scope", note.get("fix_scope", "")
   271→            )
   272→        ).strip()
   273→        if not impact_scope or not fix_scope:
   274→            errors.append(
   275→                f"issues[{idx}] requires impact_scope and fix_scope "
   276→                "(or dimension_notes defaults)"
   277→            )
   278→            continue
   279→        issues.append(
   280→            NormalizedBatchIssue(
   281→                dimension=issue["dimension"],
   282→                identifier=issue["identifier"],
   283→                summary=issue["summary"],
   284→                confidence=issue["confidence"],
   285→                suggestion=issue["suggestion"],
   286→                related_files=list(issue.get("related_files", [])),
   287→                evidence=list(issue.get("evidence", [])),
   288→                impact_scope=impact_scope,
   289→                fix_scope=fix_scope,
   290→                reasoning=str(issue.get("reasoning", "")),
   291→                evidence_lines=list(issue.get("evidence_lines", []))
   292→                if isinstance(issue.get("evidence_lines"), list)
   293→                else None,
   294→            )
   295→        )
   296→    if errors:
   297→        visible = errors[:10]
   298→        remaining = len(errors) - len(visible)
   299→        if remaining > 0:
   300→            visible.append(f"... {remaining} additional issue schema error(s) omitted")
   301→        raise ValueError("; ".join(visible))
   302→    if len(issues) <= max_batch_issues:
   303→        return issues
   304→
   305→    required_dims = set(low_score_dimensions or set())
   306→    if not required_dims:
   307→        return issues[:max_batch_issues]
   308→
   309→    # Preserve at least one issue per low-score dimension before trimming.
   310→    selected: list[NormalizedBatchIssue] = []
   311→    selected_indexes: set[int] = set()
   312→    covered: set[str] = set()
   313→    for idx, issue in enumerate(issues):
   314→        if len(selected) >= max_batch_issues:
   315→            break
   316→        dim = issue.dimension.strip()
   317→        if dim not in required_dims or dim in covered:
   318→            continue
   319→        selected.append(issue)
   320→        selected_indexes.add(idx)
   321→        covered.add(dim)
   322→
   323→    for idx, issue in enumerate(issues):
   324→        if len(selected) >= max_batch_issues:
   325→            break
   326→        if idx in selected_indexes:
   327→            continue
   328→        selected.append(issue)
   329→    return selected

   331→
   332→def _low_score_dimensions(assessments: dict[str, float]) -> set[str]:
   333→    """Return assessed dimensions requiring explicit defect issues."""
   334→    return {
   335→        dim
   336→        for dim, score in assessments.items()
   337→        if score < LOW_SCORE_ISSUE_THRESHOLD
   338→    }
   339→
   340→
   341→def _enforce_low_score_issues(
   342→    *,
   343→    assessments: dict[str, float],
   344→    issues: list[NormalizedBatchIssue],
   345→) -> None:
   346→    """Fail closed when low scores do not report explicit issues."""
   347→    required_dims = _low_score_dimensions(assessments)
   348→    if not required_dims:
   349→        return
   350→    issue_dims = {
   351→        issue.dimension.strip() for issue in issues
   352→    }
   353→    missing = sorted(dim for dim in required_dims if dim not in issue_dims)
   354→    if not missing:
   355→        return
   356→    joined = ", ".join(missing)
   357→    raise ValueError(
   358→        "low-score dimensions must include at least one explicit issue: "
   359→        f"{joined} (threshold {LOW_SCORE_ISSUE_THRESHOLD:.1f})"
   360→    )
   361→
   362→
   363→def _compute_batch_quality(
   364→    assessments: dict[str, float],
   365→    issues: list[NormalizedBatchIssue],
   366→    dimension_notes: dict[str, BatchDimensionNotePayload],
   367→    allowed_dims: set[str],
   368→    high_score_missing_issue_note: float,
   369→) -> BatchQualityPayload:
   370→    """Compute quality metrics for a single batch result."""
   371→    return {
   372→        "dimension_coverage": round(
   373→            len(assessments) / max(len(allowed_dims), 1),
   374→            3,
   375→        ),
   376→        "evidence_density": round(
   377→            sum(len(note.get("evidence", [])) for note in dimension_notes.values())
   378→            / max(len(issues), 1),
   379→            3,
   380→        ),
   381→        "high_score_missing_issue_note": high_score_missing_issue_note,
   382→    }
   383→
   384→
   385→def normalize_batch_result(
   386→    payload: dict[str, object],
   387→    allowed_dims: set[str],
   388→    *,
   389→    max_batch_issues: int,
   390→    abstraction_sub_axes: tuple[str, ...],
   391→) -> tuple[
   392→    dict[str, float],
   393→    list[BatchIssuePayload],
   394→    dict[str, BatchDimensionNotePayload],
   395→    BatchQualityPayload,
   396→]:
   397→    """Validate and normalize one batch payload."""
   398→    if "assessments" not in payload:
   399→        raise ValueError("payload missing required key: assessments")
   400→    key_error = normalize_legacy_findings_alias(
   401→        payload,
   402→        missing_issues_error="payload missing required key: issues",
   403→    )
   404→    if key_error is not None:
   405→        raise ValueError(key_error)
   406→
   407→    raw_assessments = payload.get("assessments")
   408→    if not isinstance(raw_assessments, dict):
   409→        raise ValueError("assessments must be an object")
   410→
   411→    raw_dimension_notes = payload.get("dimension_notes", {})
   412→    if not isinstance(raw_dimension_notes, dict):
   413→        raise ValueError("dimension_notes must be an object")
   414→
   415→    assessments: dict[str, float] = {}
   416→    dimension_notes: dict[str, BatchDimensionNotePayload] = {}
   417→    high_score_missing_issue_note = 0.0
   418→    for key, value in raw_assessments.items():
   419→        if not isinstance(key, str) or not key:
   420→            continue
   421→        if key not in allowed_dims:
   422→            continue
   423→        if isinstance(value, bool):
   424→            continue
   425→        if not isinstance(value, int | float):
   426→            continue
   427→        score = round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))), 1)
   428→
   429→        note_raw = raw_dimension_notes.get(key)
   430→        evidence, impact_scope, fix_scope, confidence, issues_note = (
   431→            _validate_dimension_note(key, note_raw)
   432→        )
   433→        if not isinstance(note_raw, dict):
   434→            raise ValueError(
   435→                f"dimension_notes missing object for assessed dimension: {key}"
   436→            )
   437→        if score > HIGH_SCORE_ISSUES_NOTE_THRESHOLD and not issues_note:
   438→            high_score_missing_issue_note += 1
   439→
   440→        normalized_sub_axes: dict[str, float] = {}
   441→        if key == "abstraction_fitness":
   442→            normalized_sub_axes = _normalize_abstraction_sub_axes(
   443→                note_raw, abstraction_sub_axes
   444→            )
   445→
   446→        assessments[key] = score
   447→        dimension_notes[key] = {
   448→            "evidence": [str(item).strip() for item in evidence if str(item).strip()],
   449→            "impact_scope": impact_scope.strip(),
   450→            "fix_scope": fix_scope.strip(),
   451→            "confidence": confidence,
   452→            "issues_preventing_higher_score": issues_note,
   453→        }
   454→        if normalized_sub_axes:
   455→            dimension_notes[key]["sub_axes"] = normalized_sub_axes
   456→
   457→    issues = _normalize_issues(
   458→        payload.get("issues"),
   459→        dimension_notes,
   460→        max_batch_issues=max_batch_issues,
   461→        allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   462→        low_score_dimensions=_low_score_dimensions(assessments),
   463→    )
   464→    _enforce_low_score_issues(assessments=assessments, issues=issues)
   465→
   466→    quality = _compute_batch_quality(
   467→        assessments,
   468→        issues,
   469→        dimension_notes,
   470→        allowed_dims,
   471→        high_score_missing_issue_note,
   472→    )
   473→    return (
   474→        assessments,
   475→        [issue.to_payload() for issue in issues],
   476→        dimension_notes,
   477→        quality,
   478→    )
   479→
   480→
   481→def assessment_weight(
   482→    *,
   483→    dimension: str,
   484→    issues: list[BatchIssuePayload],
   485→    dimension_notes: dict[str, BatchDimensionNotePayload],
   486→) -> float:
   487→    """Evidence-weighted assessment score weight with a neutral floor.
   488→
   489→    Weighting is evidence-based and score-independent: the raw score does not
   490→    influence how much weight a batch contributes during merge.
   491→    """
   492→    note = dimension_notes.get(dimension, {})
   493→    note_evidence = len(note.get("evidence", [])) if isinstance(note, dict) else 0
   494→    issue_count = sum(
   495→        1
   496→        for issue in issues
   497→        if str(issue.get("dimension", "")).strip() == dimension
   498→    )
   499→    return float(1 + note_evidence + issue_count)

    48→def _utc_now() -> datetime:
    49→    return datetime.now(UTC)
    50→
    51→
    52→def _iso_seconds(dt: datetime) -> str:
    53→    return dt.isoformat(timespec="seconds")
    54→
    55→
    56→def _parse_iso(raw: object) -> datetime | None:
    57→    if not isinstance(raw, str) or not raw.strip():
    58→        return None
    59→    try:
    60→        dt = datetime.fromisoformat(raw)
    61→    except ValueError:
    62→        return None
    63→    if dt.tzinfo is None:
    64→        return dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
    65→    return dt.astimezone(UTC)
    66→
    67→
    68→def _session_id() -> str:
    69→    return f"ext_{run_stamp()}_{secrets.token_hex(4)}"
    70→
    71→
    72→def _session_dir(session_id: str) -> Path:
    73→    return _external_session_root() / session_id
    74→
    75→
    76→def _session_file(session_id: str) -> Path:
    77→    return _session_dir(session_id) / "session.json"
    78→
    79→
    80→def _validate_session_id(session_id: str) -> None:
    81→    if not session_id.strip():
    82→        raise CommandError("Error: --session-id is required.", exit_code=2)
    83→    invalid_chars = {"/", "\\", ".."}
    84→    if any(part in session_id for part in invalid_chars):
    85→        raise CommandError("Error: invalid --session-id value.", exit_code=2)
    86→
    87→
    88→def _load_json_object(path: Path, *, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    89→    if not path.exists():
    90→        raise CommandError(f"Error: {label} not found: {path}")
    91→    try:
    92→        payload = json.loads(path.read_text())
    93→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
    94→        raise CommandError(f"Error: failed reading {label}: {exc}") from exc
    95→    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    96→        raise CommandError(f"Error: {label} must contain a JSON object.")
    97→    return payload
    98→
    99→
   100→def _session_payload(session_id: str) -> tuple[Path, dict[str, Any]]:
   101→    _validate_session_id(session_id)
   102→    path = _session_file(session_id)
   103→    payload = _load_json_object(path, label="session")
   104→    payload_id = str(payload.get("session_id", "")).strip()
   105→    if payload_id != session_id:
   106→        raise CommandError(
   107→            f"Error: session id mismatch in {path} (expected {session_id}, found {payload_id or '<missing>'}).",
   108→        )
   109→    return path, payload

   111→
   112→def _prepare_packet_snapshot(
   113→    args,
   114→    state: dict,
   115→    lang,
   116→    *,
   117→    config: dict[str, Any],
   118→) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], Path, Path]:
   119→    """Prepare holistic review packet and persist immutable+blind snapshots."""
   120→    path = Path(getattr(args, "path", ".") or ".")
   121→    dims = parse_dimensions(args)
   122→    dimensions = list(dims) if dims else None
   123→    retrospective = bool(getattr(args, "retrospective", False))
   124→    retrospective_max_issues = coerce_positive_int(
   125→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_issues", None),
   126→        default=30,
   127→    )
   128→    retrospective_max_batch_items = coerce_positive_int(
   129→        getattr(args, "retrospective_max_batch_items", None),
   130→        default=20,
   131→    )
   132→    lang_run, found_files = setup_lang_concrete(lang, path, config)
   133→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   134→        state,
   135→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_run.name, command="review"),
   136→    )
   137→    packet = review_mod.prepare_holistic_review(
   138→        path,
   139→        lang_run,
   140→        state,
   141→        options=review_mod.HolisticReviewPrepareOptions(
   142→            dimensions=dimensions,
   143→            files=found_files or None,
   144→            include_issue_history=retrospective,
   145→            issue_history_max_issues=retrospective_max_issues,
   146→            issue_history_max_batch_items=retrospective_max_batch_items,
   147→        ),
   148→    )
   149→    packet["narrative"] = narrative
   150→    next_command = "desloppify review --external-submit --session-id <id> --import <file>"
   151→    if retrospective:
   152→        next_command += (
   153→            " --retrospective"
   154→            f" --retrospective-max-issues {retrospective_max_issues}"
   155→            f" --retrospective-max-batch-items {retrospective_max_batch_items}"
   156→        )
   157→    packet["next_command"] = next_command
   158→    write_query(packet)
   159→
   160→    stamp = run_stamp()
   161→    blind_packet_path = _blind_packet_path()
   162→    packet_path, blind_path = write_packet_snapshot(
   163→        packet,
   164→        stamp=stamp,
   165→        review_packet_dir=_review_packet_dir(),
   166→        blind_path=blind_packet_path,
   167→        safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   168→    )
   169→    return packet, packet_path, blind_path
   170→
   171→
   172→def _build_template_payload(packet: dict[str, Any], *, session_id: str, token: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
   173→    dimensions = [
   174→        dim
   175→        for dim in packet.get("dimensions", [])
   176→        if isinstance(dim, str) and dim.strip()
   177→    ]
   178→    return {
   179→        "session": {
   180→            "id": session_id,
   181→            "token": token,
   182→        },
   183→        "assessments": {dim: 0 for dim in dimensions},
   184→        "issues": [],
   185→    }
   186→

   188→def _build_claude_launch_prompt(
   189→    *,
   190→    session_id: str,
   191→    token: str,
   192→    blind_path: Path,
   193→    template_path: Path,
   194→    output_path: Path,
   195→) -> str:
   196→    """Build a copy/paste-ready prompt for a Claude blind reviewer subagent."""
   197→    return "\n".join(
   198→        [
   199→            "# Claude Blind Reviewer Launch Prompt",
   200→            "",
   201→            "You are an isolated blind reviewer. Do not use prior chat context, prior score history, or target-score anchoring.",
   202→            "",
   203→            f"Blind packet: {blind_path}",
   204→            f"Template JSON: {template_path}",
   205→            f"Output JSON path: {output_path}",

   209→            "2. Start from the template JSON so `session.id` and `session.token` are preserved.",
   210→            f"3. Keep `session.id` exactly `{session_id}`.",
   211→            f"4. Keep `session.token` exactly `{token}`.",
   212→            "5. Output must be valid JSON with top-level keys: session, assessments, issues.",
   213→            "6. Every issue must include: dimension, identifier, summary, related_files, evidence, suggestion, confidence.",
   214→            "7. Do not include provenance metadata (CLI injects canonical provenance).",
   215→            "8. Return JSON only (no markdown fences).",
   216→        ]
   217→    )
   218→

   220→def do_external_start(args, state, lang, *, config: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> None:
   221→    """Start an external review session with CLI-issued provenance context."""
   222→    config = config or {}
   223→    runner = str(getattr(args, "external_runner", "claude")).strip().lower()
   224→    if runner not in _EXTERNAL_SUPPORTED_RUNNERS:
   225→        raise CommandError(
   226→            f"Error: unsupported external runner '{runner}'. Supported: claude.",
   227→            exit_code=2,
   228→        )
   229→    ttl_hours = int(getattr(args, "session_ttl_hours", 24) or 0)
   230→    if ttl_hours <= 0:
   231→        raise CommandError("Error: --session-ttl-hours must be > 0.", exit_code=2)
   232→
   233→    packet, packet_path, blind_path = _prepare_packet_snapshot(
   234→        args,
   235→        state,
   236→        lang,
   237→        config=config,
   238→    )
   239→    packet_hash = sha256_file(blind_path)
   240→    if not isinstance(packet_hash, str):
   241→        raise CommandError(f"Error: failed to hash blind packet: {blind_path}")
   242→
   243→    now = _utc_now()
   244→    expires = now + timedelta(hours=ttl_hours)
   245→    session_id = _session_id()
   246→    [REDACTED](16)
   247→    session_dir = _session_dir(session_id)
   248→    session_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   249→
   250→    template_payload = _build_template_payload(packet, session_id=session_id, token=token)
   251→    template_path = session_dir / "review_result.template.json"
   252→    instructions_path = session_dir / "reviewer_instructions.md"
   253→    launch_prompt_path = session_dir / "claude_launch_prompt.md"
   254→    output_path = session_dir / "review_result.json"
   255→    session_path = _session_file(session_id)
   256→
   257→    session_payload = {
   258→        "session_id": session_id,
   259→        "status": "open",
   260→        "runner": runner,
   261→        "created_at": _iso_seconds(now),
   262→        "expires_at": _iso_seconds(expires),
   263→        "ttl_hours": ttl_hours,
   264→        "token": token,
   265→        "attest": EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT,
   266→        "packet_path": str(packet_path),
   267→        "blind_packet_path": str(blind_path),
   268→        "packet_sha256": packet_hash,
   269→        "template_path": str(template_path),
   270→        "launch_prompt_path": str(launch_prompt_path),
   271→        "instructions_path": str(instructions_path),
   272→        "expected_output_path": str(output_path),
   273→    }
   274→    safe_write_text(session_path, json.dumps(session_payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   275→    safe_write_text(template_path, json.dumps(template_payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   276→    safe_write_text(
   277→        launch_prompt_path,
   278→        _build_claude_launch_prompt(
   279→            session_id=session_id,
   280→            token=token,
   281→            blind_path=blind_path,
   282→            template_path=template_path,
   283→            output_path=output_path,
   284→        )
   285→        + "\n",
   286→    )
   287→
   288→    instructions = "\n".join(
   289→        [
   290→            "# External Blind Review Session",
   291→            "",
   292→            f"Session id: {session_id}",
   293→            f"Session token: {token}",
   294→            f"Blind packet: {blind_path}",
   295→            f"Template output: {template_path}",
   296→            f"Claude launch prompt: {launch_prompt_path}",
   297→            f"Expected reviewer output: {output_path}",
   298→            "",
   299→            "Happy path:",
   300→            "1. Open the Claude launch prompt file and paste it into a context-isolated subagent task.",

   302→            "3. Submit with the printed --external-submit command.",

   309→            "5. Use the blind packet only (no score targets or prior context).",

   313→

   333→            "dim",

   338→    print(colorize(f"  4. Optional auto-rescan: `{submit_with_scan_cmd}`", "dim"))

   340→
   341→def _canonical_external_payload(
   342→    raw_payload: dict[str, Any],
   343→    *,
   344→    session: dict[str, Any],
   345→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   346→    """Return import payload with canonical provenance and required session token."""
   347→    session_meta = raw_payload.get("session")
   348→    if not isinstance(session_meta, dict):
   349→        raise CommandError(
   350→            "Error: external reviewer payload must include top-level `session` object."
   351→            ' Expected: {"session":{"id":"...","token":"..."},"assessments":{...},"issues":[...]}',
   352→        )
   353→
   354→    payload_id = str(session_meta.get("id", "")).strip()
   355→    payload_token = str(session_meta.get("token", "")).strip()
   356→    expected_id = str(session.get("session_id", "")).strip()
   357→    expected_token = str(session.get("token", "")).strip()
   358→    if payload_id != expected_id or payload_token != expected_token:
   359→        raise CommandError(
   360→            "Error: session id/token mismatch in external reviewer payload."
   361→            " Regenerate output using the session template/instructions.",
   362→        )
   363→
   364→    payload = {
   365→        key: value
   366→        for key, value in raw_payload.items()
   367→        if key not in {"session", "provenance"}
   368→    }
   369→    payload["provenance"] = {
   370→        "kind": "blind_review_batch_import",
   371→        "blind": True,
   372→        "runner": str(session.get("runner", "claude")),
   373→        "session_id": str(session.get("session_id", "")),
   374→        "created_at": _iso_seconds(_utc_now()),
   375→        "packet_path": str(session.get("blind_packet_path", "")),
   376→        "packet_sha256": str(session.get("packet_sha256", "")),
   377→    }
   378→    return payload
   379→
   380→
   381→def _ensure_session_open(session: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   382→    status = str(session.get("status", "open")).strip().lower()
   383→    if status == "open":
   384→        return
   385→    raise CommandError(
   386→        f"Error: session is not open (status={status or 'unknown'}). Start a new session with --external-start.",
   387→    )
   388→
   389→
   390→def _ensure_session_not_expired(session: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   391→    expires_at = _parse_iso(session.get("expires_at"))
   392→    if expires_at is None:
   393→        raise CommandError(
   394→            "Error: session metadata is missing/invalid expires_at.",
   395→        )
   396→    now = _utc_now()
   397→    if now <= expires_at:
   398→        return
   399→    raise CommandError(
   400→        f"Error: session expired at {session.get('expires_at')}. Start a new session with --external-start.",
   401→    )
   402→
   403→
   404→def do_external_submit(
   405→    *,
   406→    import_file: str,
   407→    session_id: str,
   408→    state: dict,
   409→    lang,
   410→    state_file,
   411→    config: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   412→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   413→    scan_after_import: bool = False,
   414→    scan_path: str = ".",
   415→    dry_run: bool = False,
   416→) -> None:
   417→    """Submit external reviewer output via session, adding canonical provenance."""
   418→    config = config or {}
   419→    session_path, session = _session_payload(session_id)
   420→    _ensure_session_open(session)
   421→    _ensure_session_not_expired(session)
   422→
   423→    if str(session.get("runner", "")).strip().lower() not in _EXTERNAL_SUPPORTED_RUNNERS:
   424→        raise CommandError(
   425→            "Error: only Claude external sessions currently support durable score submit.",
   426→        )
   427→
   428→    issues_path = Path(import_file)
   429→    raw_payload = _load_json_object(issues_path, label="external issues")
   430→    canonical_payload = _canonical_external_payload(raw_payload, session=session)
   431→
   432→    stamp = run_stamp()
   433→    session_dir = session_path.parent
   434→    canonical_path = session_dir / f"canonical_import_{stamp}.json"
   435→    safe_write_text(canonical_path, json.dumps(canonical_payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   436→
   437→    if dry_run:
   438→        do_validate_import(
   439→            str(canonical_path),
   440→            lang,
   441→            allow_partial=allow_partial,
   442→            attested_external=True,
   443→            manual_attest=str(session.get("attest", EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT)),
   444→        )
   445→        return
   446→
   447→    do_import(
   448→        str(canonical_path),
   449→        state,
   450→        lang,
   451→        state_file,
   452→        config=config,
   453→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   454→        attested_external=True,
   455→        manual_attest=str(session.get("attest", EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT)),
   456→    )
   457→
   458→    submitted_at = _iso_seconds(_utc_now())
   459→    session["status"] = "submitted"
   460→    session["submitted_at"] = submitted_at
   461→    session["submitted_input_file"] = str(issues_path)
   462→    session["submitted_canonical_file"] = str(canonical_path)
   463→    safe_write_text(session_path, json.dumps(session, indent=2) + "\n")
   464→
   465→    if scan_after_import:
   466→        code = run_followup_scan(
   467→            lang_name=lang.name,
   468→            scan_path=scan_path,
   469→            deps=FollowupScanDeps(
   470→                project_root=_runtime_project_root(),
   471→                timeout_seconds=FOLLOWUP_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
   472→                python_executable=sys.executable,
   473→                subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
   474→                timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
   475→                colorize_fn=colorize,
   476→            ),
   477→        )
   478→        if code != 0:
   479→            raise SystemExit(code)
   480→
   481→
   482→__all__ = ["do_external_start", "do_external_submit", "EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT"]
   483→
