1→"""Payload parsing and validation helpers for review imports."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→from typing import Any, TypedDict
     9→
    10→from desloppify.base.coercions import coerce_optional_str
    11→from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.data import load_dimensions_for_lang
    12→from desloppify.intelligence.review.feedback_contract import (
    13→    ASSESSMENT_FEEDBACK_THRESHOLD,
    14→    LOW_SCORE_ISSUE_THRESHOLD,
    15→    score_requires_dimension_issue,
    16→    score_requires_explicit_feedback,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.intelligence.review.importing.contracts import (
    19→    AssessmentImportPolicyModel,
    20→    ReviewImportPayload,
    21→    ReviewIssuePayload,
    22→    validate_review_issue_payload,
    23→)
    24→from desloppify.intelligence.review.importing.payload import (
    25→    normalize_legacy_findings_alias,
    26→)
    27→from desloppify.state import coerce_assessment_score
    28→
    29→from .policy import (
    30→    ASSESSMENT_POLICY_KEY,
    31→    apply_assessment_import_policy,
    32→)
    33→
    34→
    35→class ImportRootPayload(TypedDict, total=False):
    36→    """Top-level import payload shape prior to strict normalization."""
    37→
    38→    issues: list[object]
    39→    findings: list[object]
    40→    assessments: dict[str, Any]
    41→    reviewed_files: list[object]
    42→    review_scope: dict[str, Any]
    43→    provenance: dict[str, Any]
    44→    dimension_notes: dict[str, Any]
    45→    _assessment_policy: dict[str, Any]
    46→
    47→
    48→class ImportPayloadLoadError(ValueError):
    49→    """Raised when review import payload parsing/validation fails."""
    50→
    51→    def __init__(self, errors: list[str]) -> None:
    52→        cleaned = [str(error).strip() for error in errors if str(error).strip()]
    53→        self.errors = cleaned
    54→        message = "; ".join(cleaned) if cleaned else "import payload validation failed"
    55→        super().__init__(message)
    56→
    57→
    58→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    59→class ImportParseOptions:
    60→    """Import parse policy/options bundle."""
    61→
    62→    lang_name: str | None = None
    63→    allow_partial: bool = False
    64→    trusted_assessment_source: bool = False
    65→    trusted_assessment_label: str | None = None
    66→    attested_external: bool = False
    67→    manual_override: bool = False
    68→    manual_attest: str | None = None
    69→
    70→
    71→def _coerce_import_parse_options(
    72→    options: ImportParseOptions | None = None,
    73→) -> ImportParseOptions:
    74→    """Resolve import-parse options from the typed dataclass contract."""
    75→    base = options or ImportParseOptions()
    76→    return ImportParseOptions(
    77→        lang_name=coerce_optional_str(base.lang_name),
    78→        allow_partial=bool(base.allow_partial),
    79→        trusted_assessment_source=bool(base.trusted_assessment_source),
    80→        trusted_assessment_label=coerce_optional_str(base.trusted_assessment_label),
    81→        attested_external=bool(base.attested_external),
    82→        manual_override=bool(base.manual_override),
    83→        manual_attest=coerce_optional_str(base.manual_attest),
    84→    )
    85→
    86→
    87→def _normalize_import_payload_shape(
    88→    payload: ImportRootPayload,
    89→) -> tuple[ReviewImportPayload | None, list[str]]:
    90→    """Normalize payload into required-key contract with strict type checks."""
    91→    errors: list[str] = []
    92→    issues = payload.get("issues")
    93→    if not isinstance(issues, list):
    94→        errors.append("issues must be a JSON array")
    95→        issues = []
    96→
    97→    assessments = _coerce_optional_object(payload, key="assessments", errors=errors)
    98→    normalized_reviewed_files = _coerce_reviewed_files(payload, errors=errors)
    99→    review_scope = _coerce_optional_object(payload, key="review_scope", errors=errors)
   100→    provenance = _coerce_optional_object(payload, key="provenance", errors=errors)
   101→    dimension_notes = _coerce_optional_object(payload, key="dimension_notes", errors=errors)
   102→
   103→    policy = payload.get(ASSESSMENT_POLICY_KEY)
   104→    normalized_policy = (
   105→        policy if isinstance(policy, dict) else AssessmentImportPolicyModel().to_dict()
   106→    )
   107→    if errors:
   108→        return None, errors
   109→    return (
   110→        {
   111→            "issues": issues,
   112→            "assessments": assessments,
   113→            "reviewed_files": normalized_reviewed_files,
   114→            "review_scope": review_scope,
   115→            "provenance": provenance,
   116→            "dimension_notes": dimension_notes,
   117→            [REDACTED],
   118→        },
   119→        [],
   120→    )
   121→
   122→
   123→def _coerce_optional_object(
   124→    payload: ImportRootPayload,
   125→    *,
   126→    key: str,
   127→    errors: list[str],
   128→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   129→    """Normalize optional object payload fields to dictionaries."""
   130→    value = payload.get(key)
   131→    if value is None:
   132→        return {}
   133→    if isinstance(value, dict):
   134→        return value
   135→    errors.append(f"{key} must be an object when provided")
   136→    return {}
   137→
   138→
   139→def _coerce_reviewed_files(
   140→    payload: ImportRootPayload, *, errors: list[str]
   141→) -> list[str]:
   142→    """Normalize reviewed_files to trimmed string list."""
   143→    reviewed_files = payload.get("reviewed_files")
   144→    if reviewed_files is None:
   145→        return []
   146→    if isinstance(reviewed_files, list):
   147→        return [
   148→            str(item).strip()
   149→            for item in reviewed_files
   150→            if isinstance(item, str) and str(item).strip()
   151→        ]
   152→    errors.append("reviewed_files must be an array when provided")
   153→    return []
   154→
   155→
   156→def resolve_override_context(
   157→    *,
   158→    manual_override: bool,
   159→    manual_attest: str | None,
   160→) -> tuple[bool, str | None]:
   161→    """Normalize manual override settings into one explicit decision."""
   162→    override = bool(manual_override)
   163→    attest = manual_attest
   164→    if isinstance(attest, str):
   165→        attest = attest.strip()
   166→    return override, attest
   167→
   168→
   169→def _has_non_empty_strings(items: object) -> bool:
   170→    """Return True when ``items`` is a list with at least one non-empty string."""
   171→    return isinstance(items, list) and any(
   172→        isinstance(item, str) and item.strip() for item in items
   173→    )
   174→
   175→
   176→def _validate_holistic_issues_schema(
   177→    issues_data: ReviewImportPayload,
   178→    *,
   179→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   180→) -> list[str]:
   181→    """Validate strict holistic issue schema expected by issue import."""
   182→    issues = issues_data["issues"]
   183→
   184→    allowed_dimensions: set[str] = set()
   185→    if isinstance(lang_name, str) and lang_name.strip():
   186→        _, dimension_prompts, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name)
   187→        allowed_dimensions = set(dimension_prompts)
   188→
   189→    errors: list[str] = []
   190→    for idx, entry in enumerate(issues):
   191→        _normalized: ReviewIssuePayload | None
   192→        _normalized, entry_errors = validate_review_issue_payload(
   193→            entry,
   194→            label=f"issues[{idx}]",
   195→            allowed_dimensions=allowed_dimensions or None,
   196→            allow_dismissed=True,
   197→        )
   198→        for message in entry_errors:
   199→            if (
   200→                "is not allowed" in message
   201→                and lang_name
   202→                and "dimension '" in message
   203→            ):
   204→                message = message.replace(
   205→                    "is not allowed",
   206→                    f"is not valid for language '{lang_name}'",
   207→                )
   208→            errors.append(message)
   209→    return errors
   210→
   211→
   212→def _feedback_dimensions_from_issues(issues: object) -> set[str]:
   213→    """Return dimensions with explicit improvement guidance in issues payload."""
   214→    if not isinstance(issues, list):
   215→        return set()
   216→    dims: set[str] = set()
   217→    for entry in issues:
   218→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   219→            continue
   220→        dim = entry.get("dimension")
   221→        if not isinstance(dim, str) or not dim.strip():
   222→            continue
   223→        suggestion = entry.get("suggestion")
   224→        if isinstance(suggestion, str) and suggestion.strip():
   225→            dims.add(dim.strip())
   226→    return dims
   227→
   228→
   229→def _feedback_dimensions_from_dimension_notes(dimension_notes: object) -> set[str]:
   230→    """Return dimensions with concrete review evidence in dimension_notes payload."""
   231→    if not isinstance(dimension_notes, dict):
   232→        return set()
   233→    dims: set[str] = set()
   234→    for dim, note in dimension_notes.items():
   235→        if not isinstance(dim, str) or not dim.strip():
   236→            continue
   237→        if not isinstance(note, dict):
   238→            continue
   239→        if not _has_non_empty_strings(note.get("evidence")):
   240→            continue
   241→        dims.add(dim.strip())
   242→    return dims
   243→
   244→
   245→def _validate_assessment_feedback(
   246→    issues_data: ReviewImportPayload,
   247→) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
   248→    """Return dimensions missing required feedback and required low-score issues."""
   249→    assessments = issues_data["assessments"]
   250→    if not assessments:
   251→        return [], []
   252→
   253→    issue_dims = _feedback_dimensions_from_issues(issues_data["issues"])
   254→    feedback_dims = set(issue_dims)
   255→    feedback_dims.update(
   256→        _feedback_dimensions_from_dimension_notes(issues_data["dimension_notes"])
   257→    )
   258→    missing_feedback: list[str] = []
   259→    missing_low_score_issues: list[str] = []
   260→    for dim_name, payload in assessments.items():
   261→        if not isinstance(dim_name, str) or not dim_name.strip():
   262→            continue
   263→        score = coerce_assessment_score(payload)
   264→        if score is None:
   265→            continue
   266→        if score_requires_dimension_issue(score) and dim_name not in issue_dims:
   267→            missing_low_score_issues.append(f"{dim_name} ({score:.1f})")
   268→        if score_requires_explicit_feedback(score) and dim_name not in feedback_dims:
   269→            missing_feedback.append(f"{dim_name} ({score:.1f})")
   270→    return sorted(missing_feedback), sorted(missing_low_score_issues)
   271→
   272→
   273→def _load_import_json(import_file: str) -> tuple[object | None, list[str]]:
   274→    """Read import file and parse JSON payload."""
   275→    issues_path = Path(import_file)
   276→    if not issues_path.exists():
   277→        return None, [f"file not found: {import_file}"]
   278→    try:
   279→        return json.loads(issues_path.read_text()), []
   280→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError) as exc:
   281→        return None, [f"error reading issues: {exc}"]
   282→
   283→
   284→def _normalize_import_root_payload(
   285→    raw_payload: object,
   286→) -> tuple[ImportRootPayload | None, list[str]]:
   287→    """Normalize top-level payload shape before strict field validation."""
   288→    payload = {"issues": raw_payload} if isinstance(raw_payload, list) else raw_payload
   289→    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   290→        return None, ["issues file must contain a JSON array or object"]
   291→
   292→    key_error = normalize_legacy_findings_alias(
   293→        payload,
   294→        missing_issues_error="issues object must contain an 'issues' key",
   295→    )
   296→    if key_error is not None:
   297→        return None, [key_error]
   298→    return payload, []
   299→
   300→
   301→def _validate_override_option_conflicts(
   302→    options: ImportParseOptions,
   303→    *,
   304→    override_enabled: bool,
   305→) -> list[str]:
   306→    """Validate mutually exclusive override/attestation option combinations."""
   307→    if options.attested_external and override_enabled:
   308→        return ["--attested-external cannot be combined with --manual-override"]
   309→    if options.attested_external and options.allow_partial:
   310→        return [
   311→            "--attested-external cannot be combined with --allow-partial; "
   312→            "attested score imports require fully valid issues payloads"
   313→        ]
   314→    if override_enabled and options.allow_partial:
   315→        return [
   316→            "--manual-override cannot be combined with --allow-partial; "
   317→            "manual score imports require fully valid issues payloads"
   318→        ]
   319→    return []
   320→
   321→
   322→def _validate_feedback_requirements(
   323→    issues_data: ReviewImportPayload,
   324→    *,
   325→    override_enabled: bool,
   326→    override_attest: str | None,
   327→) -> list[str]:
   328→    """Validate feedback and low-score issue requirements."""
   329→    missing_feedback, missing_low_score_issues = _validate_assessment_feedback(issues_data)
   330→    if missing_low_score_issues:
   331→        if override_enabled:
   332→            if not isinstance(override_attest, str) or not override_attest.strip():
   333→                return ["--manual-override requires --attest"]
   334→            return []
   335→        return [
   336→            f"assessments below {LOW_SCORE_ISSUE_THRESHOLD:.1f} must include at "
   337→            "least one issue for that same dimension with a concrete suggestion. "
   338→            f"Missing: {', '.join(missing_low_score_issues)}"
   339→        ]
   340→    if not missing_feedback:
   341→        return []
   342→    if override_enabled:
   343→        if not isinstance(override_attest, str) or not override_attest.strip():
   344→            return ["--manual-override requires --attest"]
   345→        return []
   346→    return [
   347→        f"assessments below {ASSESSMENT_FEEDBACK_THRESHOLD:.1f} must include explicit feedback "
   348→        "(issue with same dimension and non-empty suggestion, or "
   349→        "dimension_notes evidence for that dimension). "
   350→        f"Missing: {', '.join(missing_feedback)}"
   351→    ]
   352→
   353→
   354→def _validate_schema_requirements(
   355→    issues_data: ReviewImportPayload,
   356→    *,
   357→    lang_name: str | None,
   358→    allow_partial: bool,
   359→) -> list[str]:
   360→    """Validate holistic issue schema unless partial imports are enabled."""
   361→    schema_errors = _validate_holistic_issues_schema(issues_data, lang_name=lang_name)
   362→    if not schema_errors or allow_partial:
   363→        return []
   364→    visible_errors = schema_errors[:10]
   365→    remaining = len(schema_errors) - len(visible_errors)
   366→    errors = [
   367→        "issues schema validation failed for holistic import. "
   368→        "Fix payload or rerun with --allow-partial to continue."
   369→    ]
   370→    errors.extend(visible_errors)
   371→    if remaining > 0:
   372→        errors.append(f"... {remaining} additional schema error(s) omitted")
   373→    return errors
   374→
   375→
   376→def _parse_and_validate_import(
   377→    import_file: str,
   378→    *,
   379→    options: ImportParseOptions | None = None,
   380→) -> tuple[ReviewImportPayload | None, list[str]]:
   381→    """Parse and validate a review import file (pure function)."""
   382→    resolved_options = _coerce_import_parse_options(options)
   383→
   384→    raw_payload, load_errors = _load_import_json(import_file)
   385→    if load_errors:
   386→        return None, load_errors
   387→    normalized_root, root_errors = _normalize_import_root_payload(raw_payload)
   388→    if root_errors:
   389→        return None, root_errors
   390→    if normalized_root is None:
   391→        return None, ["issues payload root normalization returned no data"]
   392→
   393→    normalized_issues_data, shape_errors = _normalize_import_payload_shape(normalized_root)
   394→    if shape_errors:
   395→        return None, shape_errors
   396→    if normalized_issues_data is None:
   397→        return None, ["issues payload normalization returned no data"]
   398→
   399→    override_enabled, override_attest = resolve_override_context(
   400→        manual_override=resolved_options.manual_override,
   401→        manual_attest=resolved_options.manual_attest,
   402→    )
   403→    conflict_errors = _validate_override_option_conflicts(
   404→        resolved_options,
   405→        override_enabled=override_enabled,
   406→    )
   407→    if conflict_errors:
   408→        return None, conflict_errors
   409→
   410→    issues_data, policy_errors = apply_assessment_import_policy(
   411→        normalized_issues_data,
   412→        import_file=import_file,
   413→        attested_external=resolved_options.attested_external,
   414→        attested_attest=override_attest,
   415→        manual_override=override_enabled,
   416→        manual_attest=override_attest,
   417→        trusted_assessment_source=resolved_options.trusted_assessment_source,
   418→        trusted_assessment_label=resolved_options.trusted_assessment_label,
   419→    )
   420→    if policy_errors:
   421→        return None, policy_errors
   422→    if issues_data is None:
   423→        return None, ["assessment import policy returned no payload"]
   424→
   425→    feedback_errors = _validate_feedback_requirements(
   426→        issues_data,
   427→        override_enabled=override_enabled,
   428→        override_attest=override_attest,
   429→    )
   430→    if feedback_errors:
   431→        return None, feedback_errors
   432→
   433→    schema_errors = _validate_schema_requirements(
   434→        issues_data,
   435→        lang_name=resolved_options.lang_name,
   436→        allow_partial=resolved_options.allow_partial,
   437→    )
   438→    if schema_errors:
   439→        return None, schema_errors
   440→
   441→    return issues_data, []
   442→
   443→
   444→def load_import_issues_data(
   445→    import_file: str,
   446→    *,
   447→    colorize_fn=None,
   448→    options: ImportParseOptions | None = None,
   449→) -> ReviewImportPayload:
   450→    """Load and normalize review import payload to object format.
   451→
   452→    Raises ``ImportPayloadLoadError`` when validation fails.
   453→    """
   454→    resolved_options = _coerce_import_parse_options(options)
   455→    data, errors = _parse_and_validate_import(
   456→        import_file,
   457→        options=resolved_options,
   458→    )
   459→    if errors:
   460→        raise ImportPayloadLoadError(errors)
   461→    if data is None:
   462→        raise ImportPayloadLoadError(["import payload is empty after validation"])
   463→    return data
   464→
```

   110→def _imported_assessment_keys(issues_data: ReviewImportPayload) -> set[str]:
   111→    """Return normalized assessment dimension keys from payload."""
   112→    raw_assessments = issues_data["assessments"]
   113→    keys: set[str] = set()
   114→    for raw_key in raw_assessments:

   116→        if normalized:

   121→def _mark_manual_override_assessments_provisional(
   122→    state: dict,
   123→    *,
   124→    assessment_keys: set[str],
   125→) -> int:
   126→    """Mark imported manual override assessments as provisional until next scan."""
   127→    if not assessment_keys:
   128→        return 0
   129→    store = state.get("subjective_assessments")
   130→    if not isinstance(store, dict):
   131→        return 0
   132→
   133→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   134→    expires_scan = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0) + 1
   135→    marked = 0
   136→    for key in sorted(assessment_keys):
   137→        payload = store.get(key)
   138→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   139→            continue
   140→        payload["source"] = "manual_override"
   141→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   142→        payload["provisional_override"] = True
   143→        payload["provisional_until_scan"] = expires_scan
   144→        payload.pop("placeholder", None)
   145→        marked += 1
   146→    return marked
   147→
   148→
   149→def _clear_provisional_override_flags(
   150→    state: dict,
   151→    *,
   152→    assessment_keys: set[str],
   153→) -> int:
   154→    """Clear provisional override flags when trusted internal assessments replace them."""
   155→    if not assessment_keys:
   156→        return 0
   157→    store = state.get("subjective_assessments")
   158→    if not isinstance(store, dict):
   159→        return 0
   160→
   161→    cleared = 0
   162→    for key in sorted(assessment_keys):
   163→        payload = store.get(key)
   164→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   165→            continue
   166→        if payload.pop("provisional_override", None) is not None:
   167→            cleared += 1
   168→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   169→        if payload.get("source") == "manual_override":
   170→            payload["source"] = "holistic"
   171→    return cleared

   201→def _sync_plan_after_import(state: dict, diff: dict, assessment_mode: str) -> None:
   202→    """All post-import plan syncs. Load once, save once.
   203→
   204→    Phase 1 (issue sync): Only when new/reopened issues arrived — adds them
   205→    to queue, auto-resolves covered subjective items.
   206→    Phase 2 (workflow items): Always — injects score-checkpoint, create-plan,
   207→    import-scores, and communicate-score workflow items as needed.
   208→    """
   209→    try:
   210→        from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   211→            sync_communicate_score_needed,
   212→            sync_import_scores_needed,
   213→        )
   214→        from desloppify.engine.plan import (
   215→            append_log_entry,
   216→            current_unscored_ids,
   217→            has_living_plan,
   218→            load_plan,
   219→            purge_ids,
   220→            save_plan,
   221→            sync_create_plan_needed,
   222→            sync_plan_after_review_import,
   223→            sync_score_checkpoint_needed,
   224→        )
   225→
   226→        if not has_living_plan():
   227→            return
   228→
   229→        plan = load_plan()
   230→        dirty = False
   231→
   232→        # Phase 1: Issue sync (only when new/reopened issues arrived)
   233→        has_new_issues = (
   234→            int(diff.get("new", 0) or 0) > 0
   235→            or int(diff.get("reopened", 0) or 0) > 0
   236→        )
   237→        import_result = None
   238→        covered_ids: list[str] = []
   239→        if has_new_issues:
   240→            import_result = sync_plan_after_review_import(plan, state)
   241→            if import_result is not None:
   242→                dirty = True
   243→
   244→            # Auto-resolve subjective dimension items that are no longer unscored
   245→            still_unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   246→            order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   247→            covered_ids = [
   248→                fid for fid in order
   249→                if fid.startswith("subjective::") and fid not in still_unscored
   250→            ]
   251→            if covered_ids:
   252→                purge_ids(plan, covered_ids)
   253→                dirty = True
   254→
   255→        # Phase 2: Workflow items (always)
   256→        injected_parts: list[str] = []
   257→
   258→        checkpoint_result = sync_score_checkpoint_needed(plan, state)
   259→        if checkpoint_result.changes:
   260→            dirty = True
   261→            injected_parts.append("`workflow::score-checkpoint`")
   262→
   263→        scores_imported = assessment_mode in (
   264→            "trusted_internal", "attested_external", "manual_override",
   265→        )
   266→        import_scores_result = sync_import_scores_needed(
   267→            plan, state, assessment_mode=assessment_mode,
   268→        )
   269→        if import_scores_result.changes:
   270→            dirty = True
   271→            injected_parts.append("`workflow::import-scores`")
   272→
   273→        communicate_result = sync_communicate_score_needed(
   274→            plan, state, scores_just_imported=scores_imported,
   275→        )
   276→        if communicate_result.changes:
   277→            dirty = True
   278→            injected_parts.append("`workflow::communicate-score`")
   279→
   280→        create_plan_result = sync_create_plan_needed(plan, state)
   281→        if create_plan_result.changes:
   282→            dirty = True
   283→            injected_parts.append("`workflow::create-plan`")
   284→
   285→        # Save once
   286→        if dirty:
   287→            if import_result is not None:
   288→                append_log_entry(
   289→                    plan,
   290→                    "review_import_sync",
   291→                    actor="system",
   292→                    detail={
   293→                        "trigger": "review_import",
   294→                        "new_ids": sorted(import_result.new_ids),
   295→                        "added_to_queue": import_result.added_to_queue,
   296→                        "diff_new": diff.get("new", 0),
   297→                        "diff_reopened": diff.get("reopened", 0),
   298→                        "covered_subjective": covered_ids,
   299→                    },
   300→                )
   301→            save_plan(plan)
   302→
   303→        # Print results
   304→        if import_result is not None:
   305→            _print_review_import_sync(state, import_result)
   306→        if injected_parts:
   307→            print(colorize(
   308→                f"  Plan: {' and '.join(injected_parts)} queued. Run `desloppify next`.",
   309→                "cyan",
   310→            ))
   311→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   312→        print(
   313→            colorize(
   314→                f"  Note: skipped plan sync after review import ({exc}).",
   315→                "dim",
   316→            )
   317→        )

   319→
   320→def do_import(
   321→    import_file,
   322→    state,
   323→    lang,
   324→    state_file,
   325→    *,
   326→    config: dict | None = None,
   327→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   328→    trusted_assessment_source: bool = False,
   329→    trusted_assessment_label: str | None = None,
   330→    attested_external: bool = False,
   331→    manual_override: bool = False,
   332→    manual_attest: str | None = None,
   333→) -> None:
   334→    """Import mode: ingest agent-produced issues."""
   335→    import_config = ReviewImportConfig(
   336→        config=config,
   337→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   338→        trusted_assessment_source=trusted_assessment_source,
   339→        trusted_assessment_label=trusted_assessment_label,
   340→        attested_external=attested_external,
   341→        manual_override=manual_override,
   342→        manual_attest=manual_attest,
   343→    )
   344→    override_enabled, override_attest = import_helpers_mod.resolve_override_context(
   345→        manual_override=import_config.manual_override,
   346→        manual_attest=import_config.manual_attest,
   347→    )
   348→    try:
   349→        _validate_import_flag_combos(
   350→            attested_external=import_config.attested_external,
   351→            allow_partial=import_config.allow_partial,
   352→            override_enabled=override_enabled,
   353→            override_attest=override_attest,
   354→        )
   355→    except ImportFlagValidationError as exc:
   356→        raise CommandError(str(exc), exit_code=1) from exc
   357→
   358→    try:
   359→        issues_data = import_helpers_mod.load_import_issues_data(
   360→            import_file,
   361→            config=_build_import_load_config(
   362→                lang_name=lang.name,
   363→                import_config=import_config,
   364→                override_enabled=override_enabled,
   365→                override_attest=override_attest,
   366→            ),
   367→        )
   368→    except import_helpers_mod.ImportPayloadLoadError as exc:
   369→        import_helpers_mod.print_import_load_errors(
   370→            exc.errors,
   371→            import_file=str(import_file),
   372→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   373→        )
   374→        raise PacketValidationError("import payload validation failed", exit_code=1) from exc
   375→    assessment_policy: AssessmentImportPolicyModel = (
   376→        import_helpers_mod.assessment_policy_model_from_payload(issues_data)
   377→    )
   378→    import_helpers_mod.print_assessment_mode_banner(
   379→        assessment_policy.to_dict(),
   380→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   381→    )
   382→    import_helpers_mod.print_assessment_policy_notice(
   383→        assessment_policy.to_dict(),
   384→        import_file=str(import_file),
   385→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   386→    )
   387→
   388→    prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   389→
   390→    # Transactional import: only persist if all post-import guards pass.
   391→    # Rebase on the latest on-disk state when available so long-running review
   392→    # sessions don't clobber newer imports/scans that completed while batches ran.
   393→    state_path = Path(state_file) if state_file is not None else None
   394→    if state_path is not None and state_path.exists():
   395→        working_state = copy.deepcopy(state_mod.load_state(state_path))
   396→    else:
   397→        working_state = copy.deepcopy(state)
   398→    diff = review_mod.import_holistic_issues(issues_data, working_state, lang.name)
   399→    label = "Holistic review"
   400→    imported_assessment_keys = _imported_assessment_keys(issues_data)
   401→    provisional_count = 0
   402→    if assessment_policy.mode == "manual_override":
   403→        provisional_count = _mark_manual_override_assessments_provisional(
   404→            working_state,
   405→            assessment_keys=imported_assessment_keys,
   406→        )
   407→    elif assessment_policy.mode in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}:
   408→        _clear_provisional_override_flags(
   409→            working_state,
   410→            assessment_keys=imported_assessment_keys,
   411→        )
   412→
   413→    if diff.get("skipped", 0) > 0 and not import_config.allow_partial:
   414→        details_lines: list[str] = []
   415→        for detail in diff.get("skipped_details", []):
   416→            reasons = "; ".join(detail.get("missing", []))
   417→            details_lines.append(
   418→                f"  #{detail.get('index', '?')} ({detail.get('identifier', '<none>')}): {reasons}"
   419→            )
   420→        msg = "import produced skipped issue(s); refusing partial import."
   421→        if details_lines:
   422→            msg += "\n" + "\n".join(details_lines)
   423→        msg += "\nFix the payload and retry, or pass --allow-partial to override."
   424→        raise CommandError(msg, exit_code=1)
   425→
   426→    if assessment_policy.assessments_present:
   427→        audit = working_state.setdefault("assessment_import_audit", [])
   428→        audit.append(
   429→            {
   430→                "timestamp": state_mod.utc_now(),
   431→                "mode": assessment_policy.mode,
   432→                "trusted": bool(assessment_policy.trusted),
   433→                "reason": assessment_policy.reason,
   434→                "override_used": bool(assessment_policy.mode == "manual_override"),
   435→                "attested_external": bool(assessment_policy.mode == "attested_external"),
   436→                "provisional": bool(assessment_policy.mode == "manual_override"),
   437→                "provisional_count": int(provisional_count),
   438→                "attest": (override_attest or "").strip(),
   439→                "import_file": str(import_file),
   440→            }
   441→        )
   442→    state.clear()
   443→    state.update(working_state)
   444→    state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
   445→
   446→    # Sync plan: issue sync (if new issues) + workflow items (always).
   447→    _sync_plan_after_import(state, diff, assessment_policy.mode)
   448→
   449→    _print_import_results(
   450→        state=state,
   451→        lang_name=lang.name,
   452→        config=import_config.config,
   453→        diff=diff,
   454→        prev=prev,
   455→        label=label,
   456→        provisional_count=provisional_count,
   457→        assessment_policy=assessment_policy,
   458→    )
   459→
   460→
   461→def _print_import_results(
   462→    *,
   463→    state: dict,
   464→    lang_name: str,
   465→    config: dict | None,
   466→    diff: dict,
   467→    prev: dict,
   468→    label: str,
   469→    provisional_count: int,
   470→    assessment_policy,
   471→) -> None:
   472→    """Print import results, scores, and write query.json."""
   473→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   474→        state, NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="review")
   475→    )
   476→
   477→    print(colorize(f"\n  {label} imported:", "bold"))
   478→    issue_count = int(diff.get("new", 0) or 0)
   479→    print(
   480→        colorize(
   481→            f"  +{issue_count} new issue{'s' if issue_count != 1 else ''} "
   482→            f"(review issues), "
   483→            f"{diff['auto_resolved']} resolved, "
   484→            f"{diff['reopened']} reopened",
   485→            "dim",
   486→        )
   487→    )
   488→    if provisional_count > 0:
   489→        print(
   490→            colorize(
   491→                "  WARNING: manual override assessments are provisional and will "
   492→                "reset on the next scan unless replaced by "
   493→                "`review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import`.",
   494→                "yellow",
   495→            )
   496→        )
   497→    import_helpers_mod.print_skipped_validation_details(diff, colorize_fn=colorize)
   498→    import_helpers_mod.print_assessments_summary(state, colorize_fn=colorize)
   499→    next_command = import_helpers_mod.print_open_review_summary(
   500→        state, colorize_fn=colorize
   501→    )
   502→    show_score_with_plan_context(state, prev)
   503→    at_target = import_helpers_mod.print_review_import_scores_and_integrity(
   504→        state,
   505→        config or {},
   506→        state_mod=state_mod,
   507→        target_strict_score_from_config_fn=target_strict_score_from_config,
   508→        subjective_at_target_fn=_SCORECARD_SUBJECTIVE_AT_TARGET,
   509→        subjective_rerun_command_fn=reporting_dimensions_mod.subjective_rerun_command,
   510→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   511→    )
   512→
   513→    print(
   514→        colorize(
   515→            f"  Next command to improve subjective scores: `{next_command}`", "dim"
   516→        )
   517→    )
   518→    write_query(
   519→        {
   520→            "command": "review",
   521→            "action": "import",
   522→            "mode": "holistic",
   523→            "diff": diff,
   524→            "next_command": next_command,
   525→            "subjective_at_target": [
   526→                {"dimension": entry["name"], "score": entry["score"]}
   527→                for entry in at_target
   528→            ],
   529→            "assessment_import": {
   530→                "mode": assessment_policy.mode,
   531→                "trusted": bool(assessment_policy.trusted),
   532→                "reason": assessment_policy.reason,
   533→            },
   534→            "narrative": narrative,
   535→        }
   536→    )
   537→
   538→
   539→def do_validate_import(
   540→    import_file,
   541→    lang,
   542→    *,
   543→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   544→    attested_external: bool = False,
   545→    manual_override: bool = False,
   546→    manual_attest: str | None = None,
   547→) -> None:
   548→    """Validate import payload/policy and print mode without mutating state."""
   549→    import_config = ReviewImportConfig(
   550→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   551→        attested_external=attested_external,
   552→        manual_override=manual_override,
   553→        manual_attest=manual_attest,
   554→    )
   555→    override_enabled, override_attest = import_helpers_mod.resolve_override_context(
   556→        manual_override=import_config.manual_override,
   557→        manual_attest=import_config.manual_attest,
   558→    )
   559→    try:
   560→        _validate_import_flag_combos(
   561→            attested_external=import_config.attested_external,
   562→            allow_partial=import_config.allow_partial,
   563→            override_enabled=override_enabled,
   564→            override_attest=override_attest,
   565→        )
   566→    except ImportFlagValidationError as exc:
   567→        raise CommandError(str(exc), exit_code=1) from exc
   568→
   569→    try:
   570→        issues_data = import_helpers_mod.load_import_issues_data(
   571→            import_file,
   572→            config=_build_import_load_config(
   573→                lang_name=lang.name,
   574→                import_config=import_config,
   575→                override_enabled=override_enabled,
   576→                override_attest=override_attest,
   577→            ),
   578→        )
   579→    except import_helpers_mod.ImportPayloadLoadError as exc:
   580→        import_helpers_mod.print_import_load_errors(
   581→            exc.errors,
   582→            import_file=str(import_file),
   583→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   584→        )
   585→        raise PacketValidationError("import payload validation failed", exit_code=1) from exc
   586→    assessment_policy = import_helpers_mod.assessment_policy_model_from_payload(
   587→        issues_data
   588→    )
   589→    import_helpers_mod.print_assessment_mode_banner(
   590→        assessment_policy.to_dict(),
   591→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   592→    )
   593→    import_helpers_mod.print_assessment_policy_notice(
   594→        assessment_policy.to_dict(),
   595→        import_file=str(import_file),
   596→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   597→    )
   598→
   599→    issues_count = len(issues_data["issues"])
   600→    print(colorize("\n  Import payload validation passed.", "bold"))
   601→    print(colorize(f"  Issues parsed: {issues_count}", "dim"))
   602→    if assessment_policy.assessments_present:
   603→        count = int(assessment_policy.assessment_count)
   604→        print(colorize(f"  Assessment entries in payload: {count}", "dim"))
   605→    print(colorize("  No state changes were made (--validate-import).", "dim"))
   606→
   607→
   608→__all__ = [
   609→    "ImportFlagValidationError",
   610→    "ReviewImportConfig",
   611→    "do_import",
   612→    "do_validate_import",
   613→    "subjective_at_target_dimensions",
   614→]

1→"""Shared subjective-review contract for prompts, normalization, and import validation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→LOW_SCORE_ISSUE_THRESHOLD = 85.0
     6→ASSESSMENT_FEEDBACK_THRESHOLD = 100.0
     7→HIGH_SCORE_ISSUES_NOTE_THRESHOLD = 85.0
     8→[REDACTED]
     9→LEGACY_DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY = "unreported_risk"
    10→[REDACTED]
    11→[REDACTED]
    12→DEFAULT_MAX_BATCH_ISSUES = 10
    13→_PROMPT_CONTRACT_HEADER = "GLOBAL REVIEW CONTRACT (applies to every dimension):"
    14→TRUSTED_IMPORT_COVERAGE_OVERRIDE_FLAG = "--allow-partial"
    15→
    16→
    17→def max_batch_issues_for_dimension_count(dimension_count: int) -> int:
    18→    """Return the normalized max issues budget for one batch payload."""
    19→    safe_count = max(0, int(dimension_count))
    20→    return max(DEFAULT_MAX_BATCH_ISSUES, safe_count)
    21→
    22→
    23→def score_requires_dimension_issue(score: float) -> bool:
    24→    """Return True when score requires at least one explicit issue."""
    25→    return float(score) < LOW_SCORE_ISSUE_THRESHOLD
    26→
    27→
    28→def score_requires_explicit_feedback(score: float) -> bool:
    29→    """Return True when score requires a issue or dimension-note evidence."""
    30→    return float(score) < ASSESSMENT_FEEDBACK_THRESHOLD
    31→
    32→
    33→def global_prompt_contract() -> str:
    34→    """Render shared review contract text injected into all review prompts."""
    35→    return (
    36→        f"{_PROMPT_CONTRACT_HEADER}\n"
    37→        "- Scope breadth: report any material issues supported by evidence "
    38→        "(structural, architectural, boundary, readability, lifecycle), "
    39→        "not only low-level nits.\n"
    40→        "- Dimension boundaries are guidance, not a gag-order: if an issue "
    41→        "spans dimensions, report it under the most impacted dimension.\n"
    42→        "- Do not default to 100. Reserve 100 for genuinely exemplary code "
    43→        "with clear positive evidence; if there is uncertainty or residual "
    44→        "issues, score below 100.\n"
    45→        "- Do not suppress valid issues to keep scores high.\n"
    46→        f"- Scores below {LOW_SCORE_ISSUE_THRESHOLD:.1f} MUST include at least one "
    47→        "issue for that same dimension.\n"
    48→        f"- Scores below {ASSESSMENT_FEEDBACK_THRESHOLD:.1f} MUST include explicit "
    49→        "feedback for that same dimension (issue with suggestion or "
    50→        "dimension_notes evidence).\n"
    51→        f"- Scores above {HIGH_SCORE_ISSUES_NOTE_THRESHOLD:.1f} MUST include a "
    52→        "non-empty `issues_preventing_higher_score` note for that dimension.\n"
    53→        "- Issues must always describe defects that need change, never positive observations.\n"
    54→        "- Think structurally: when individual issues form a pattern, consider what is\n"
    55→        "  causing them. If several issues stem from a shared root cause (missing abstraction,\n"
    56→        "  repeated pattern, inconsistent convention), say so in the issues — explain the\n"
    57→        "  deeper issue and use root_cause_cluster to connect related symptoms."
    58→    )
    59→
    60→
    61→def ensure_prompt_contract(system_prompt: str) -> str:
    62→    """Append shared contract text once to a system prompt."""
    63→    text = (system_prompt or "").strip()
    64→    if _PROMPT_CONTRACT_HEADER in text:
    65→        return text
    66→    suffix = global_prompt_contract()
    67→    if not text:
    68→        return suffix
    69→    return f"{text}\n\n{suffix}"
    70→
    71→
    72→__all__ = [
    73→    "ASSESSMENT_FEEDBACK_THRESHOLD",
    74→    "DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY",
    75→    "DEFAULT_MAX_BATCH_ISSUES",
    76→    "HIGH_SCORE_ISSUES_NOTE_THRESHOLD",
    77→    "LEGACY_DIMENSION_NOTE_ISSUES_KEY",
    78→    "LEGACY_REVIEW_QUALITY_HIGH_SCORE_MISSING_ISSUES_KEY",
    79→    "REVIEW_QUALITY_HIGH_SCORE_MISSING_ISSUES_KEY",
    80→    "TRUSTED_IMPORT_COVERAGE_OVERRIDE_FLAG",
    81→    "LOW_SCORE_ISSUE_THRESHOLD",
    82→    "ensure_prompt_contract",
    83→    "global_prompt_contract",
    84→    "max_batch_issues_for_dimension_count",
    85→    "score_requires_dimension_issue",
    86→    "score_requires_explicit_feedback",
    87→]

    77→def _merge_batch_results(batch_results: list[object]) -> dict[str, object]:
    78→    """Deterministically merge assessments/issues across batch outputs."""
    79→    normalized_results: list[batch_core_mod.BatchResultPayload] = []
    80→    for result in batch_results:
    81→        if hasattr(result, "to_dict") and callable(result.to_dict):
    82→            payload = result.to_dict()
    83→            if isinstance(payload, dict):
    84→                normalized_results.append(cast(batch_core_mod.BatchResultPayload, payload))
    85→                continue
    86→        if isinstance(result, dict):
    87→            normalized_results.append(cast(batch_core_mod.BatchResultPayload, result))
    88→    return batch_core_mod.merge_batch_results(
    89→        normalized_results,
    90→        abstraction_sub_axes=ABSTRACTION_SUB_AXES,
    91→        abstraction_component_names=ABSTRACTION_COMPONENT_NAMES,
    92→    )

   186→    policy = resolve_batch_run_policy(args)
   187→    batch_timeout_seconds = policy.batch_timeout_seconds
   188→    batch_max_retries = policy.batch_max_retries
   189→    batch_retry_backoff_seconds = policy.batch_retry_backoff_seconds
   190→    batch_heartbeat_seconds = policy.heartbeat_seconds
   191→    batch_live_log_interval_seconds = (
   192→        max(1.0, min(batch_heartbeat_seconds, 10.0))
   193→        if batch_heartbeat_seconds > 0
   194→        else 5.0
   195→    )
   196→    batch_stall_kill_seconds = policy.stall_kill_seconds
   197→
   198→    def _prepare_run_artifacts(*, stamp, selected_indexes, batches, packet_path, run_root, repo_root):
   199→        return prepare_run_artifacts(
   200→            stamp=stamp,
   201→            selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   202→            batches=batches,
   203→            packet_path=packet_path,
   204→            run_root=run_root,
   205→            repo_root=repo_root,
   206→            build_prompt_fn=batch_core_mod.build_batch_prompt,
   207→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   208→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   209→        )
   210→
   211→    def _collect_batch_results(*, selected_indexes, failures, output_files, allowed_dims):
   212→        return collect_batch_results(
   213→            selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   214→            failures=failures,
   215→            output_files=output_files,
   216→            allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   217→            extract_payload_fn=lambda raw: batch_core_mod.extract_json_payload(raw, log_fn=log),
   218→            normalize_result_fn=lambda payload, dims: batch_core_mod.normalize_batch_result(
   219→                payload,
   220→                dims,
   221→                max_batch_issues=max_batch_issues_for_dimension_count(
   222→                    len(dims)
   223→                ),
   224→                abstraction_sub_axes=ABSTRACTION_SUB_AXES,
   225→            ),
   226→        )
   227→
   228→    return review_batches_mod.do_run_batches(
   229→        args,
   230→        state,
   231→        lang,
   232→        state_file,
   233→        config=config,
   234→        run_stamp_fn=run_stamp,
   235→        load_or_prepare_packet_fn=_load_or_prepare_packet,
   236→        selected_batch_indexes_fn=lambda args, *, batch_count: selected_batch_indexes(
   237→            raw_selection=getattr(args, "only_batches", None),
   238→            batch_count=batch_count,
   239→            parse_fn=batch_core_mod.parse_batch_selection,
   240→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   241→        ),
   242→        prepare_run_artifacts_fn=_prepare_run_artifacts,
   243→        run_codex_batch_fn=lambda *, prompt, repo_root, output_file, log_file: run_codex_batch(
   244→            prompt=prompt,
   245→            repo_root=repo_root,
   246→            output_file=output_file,
   247→            log_file=log_file,

   250→                subprocess_run=subprocess.run,

   252→                safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,

   254→                subprocess_popen=subprocess.Popen,

   256→                stall_after_output_seconds=batch_stall_kill_seconds,

   283→        subagent_runs_dir=_subagent_runs_dir(),
   284→    )
   285→
   286→def _validate_run_dir(run_dir: Path) -> tuple[dict, Path, str]:
   287→    """Validate run directory, load summary, and return (summary, blind_packet_path, immutable_packet_path).
   288→
   289→    Raises CommandError on any validation failure.
   290→    """
   291→    if not run_dir.is_dir():
   292→        raise CommandError(f"run directory not found: {run_dir}", exit_code=1)
   293→
   294→    summary_path = run_dir / "run_summary.json"
   295→    if not summary_path.exists():
   296→        raise CommandError(f"no run_summary.json in {run_dir}", exit_code=1)
   297→    try:
   298→        summary = json.loads(summary_path.read_text())
   299→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   300→        raise CommandError(f"Error reading run summary: {exc}", exit_code=1) from exc
   301→
   302→    successful = summary.get("successful_batches", [])
   303→    blind_packet_path = Path(str(summary.get("blind_packet", "")))
   304→    immutable_packet_path = str(summary.get("immutable_packet", ""))
   305→
   306→    if not successful:
   307→        raise CommandError("no successful batches in run summary.", exit_code=1)
   308→    if not blind_packet_path.exists():
   309→        raise PacketValidationError(f"blind packet not found: {blind_packet_path}", exit_code=1)
   310→
   311→    try:
   312→        packet = json.loads(Path(immutable_packet_path).read_text())
   313→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   314→        raise PacketValidationError(f"Error reading immutable packet: {exc}", exit_code=1) from exc
   315→
   316→    summary["_packet"] = packet
   317→    return summary, blind_packet_path, immutable_packet_path
   318→
   319→
   320→def do_import_run(
   321→    run_dir_path: str,
   322→    state: dict,
   323→    lang,
   324→    state_file: str,
   325→    *,
   326→    config: dict | None = None,
   327→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   328→    scan_after_import: bool = False,
   329→    scan_path: str = ".",
   330→) -> None:
   331→    """Re-import results from a completed run directory.
   332→
   333→    Replays the merge+provenance+import step that normally runs at the end of
   334→    ``--run-batches``.  Useful when the original pipeline was interrupted (e.g.
   335→    broken pipe from background execution) but all batch results completed.
   336→    """
   337→    run_dir = Path(run_dir_path)
   338→    summary, blind_packet_path, _immutable_path = _validate_run_dir(run_dir)
   339→
   340→    runner = str(summary.get("runner", "codex"))
   341→    stamp = str(summary.get("run_stamp", ""))
   342→    successful = summary.get("successful_batches", [])
   343→    packet = summary.pop("_packet", {})
   344→    allowed_dims = {str(d) for d in packet.get("dimensions", []) if isinstance(d, str)}
   345→
   346→    # -- locate and parse raw batch results --
   347→    results_dir = run_dir / "results"
   348→    selected_indexes = [idx - 1 for idx in successful]  # convert 1-based to 0-based
   349→    output_files = {
   350→        idx: results_dir / f"batch-{idx + 1}.raw.txt"
   351→        for idx in selected_indexes
   352→    }
   353→
   354→    missing = [idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes if not output_files[idx].exists()]
   355→    if missing:
   356→        raise CommandError(f"missing result files for batches: {missing}", exit_code=1)
   357→
   358→    batch_results, failures = collect_batch_results(
   359→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   360→        failures=[],
   361→        output_files=output_files,
   362→        allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   363→        extract_payload_fn=lambda raw: batch_core_mod.extract_json_payload(raw, log_fn=log),
   364→        normalize_result_fn=lambda payload, dims: batch_core_mod.normalize_batch_result(
   365→            payload,
   366→            dims,
   367→            max_batch_issues=max_batch_issues_for_dimension_count(len(dims)),
   368→            abstraction_sub_axes=ABSTRACTION_SUB_AXES,
   369→        ),
   370→    )
   371→
   372→    if not batch_results:
   373→        raise CommandError("no valid batch results could be parsed.", exit_code=1)
   374→
   375→    print(colorize(f"  Parsed {len(batch_results)} batch results from {run_dir}", "bold"))
   376→    if failures:
   377→        print(colorize(f"  Warning: {len(failures)} batches failed to parse: {[f + 1 for f in failures]}", "yellow"))
   378→
   379→    # -- merge --
   380→    merged = _merge_batch_results(batch_results)
   381→
   382→    # -- build provenance --
   383→    successful_indexes = [idx for idx in selected_indexes if idx not in set(failures)]
   384→    merged["provenance"] = build_batch_import_provenance(
   385→        runner=runner,
   386→        blind_packet_path=blind_packet_path,
   387→        run_stamp=stamp,
   388→        batch_indexes=successful_indexes,
   389→    )
   390→
   391→    # -- write merged output --
   392→    merged_path = run_dir / "holistic_issues_merged.json"
   393→    safe_write_text(merged_path, json.dumps(merged, indent=2) + "\n")
   394→    print(colorize(f"  Merged output: {merged_path}", "bold"))
   395→
   396→    # -- import with trusted source --
   397→    _do_import(
   398→        str(merged_path),
   399→        state,
   400→        lang,
   401→        state_file,
   402→        config=config,
   403→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   404→        trusted_assessment_source=True,
   405→        trusted_assessment_label=f"trusted import-run replay from {run_dir.name}",
   406→    )
   407→
   408→    # -- optional follow-up scan --
   409→    if scan_after_import:
   410→        lang_name = getattr(lang, "name", None) or str(getattr(lang, "lang", ""))
   411→        if lang_name:
   412→            run_followup_scan(
   413→                lang_name=lang_name,
   414→                scan_path=scan_path,
   415→                deps=FollowupScanDeps(
   416→                    project_root=_runtime_project_root(),
   417→                    timeout_seconds=FOLLOWUP_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
   418→                    python_executable=sys.executable,
   419→                    subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
   420→                    timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
   421→                    colorize_fn=colorize,
   422→                ),
   423→            )
   424→
```

    42→@dataclass

    58→    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
    59→        self.batch_positions = {
    60→            batch_idx: pos + 1 for pos, batch_idx in enumerate(self.selected_indexes)
    61→        }
    62→        self.batch_status = {
    63→            str(idx + 1): {
    64→                "position": self.batch_positions.get(idx, 0),
    65→                "status": "pending",
    66→                "prompt_path": str(self.prompt_files[idx]),
    67→                "result_path": str(self.output_files[idx]),
    68→                "log_path": str(self.log_files[idx]),
    69→            }
    70→            for idx in self.selected_indexes
    71→        }
    72→
    73→    def report(self, batch_index: int, event: str, code: int | None = None, **details) -> None:
    74→        if event == "heartbeat":
    75→            self._report_heartbeat(details)
    76→            return
    77→
    78→        position = self.batch_positions.get(batch_index, 0)
    79→        key = str(batch_index + 1)
    80→        state = self.batch_status.setdefault(
    81→            key,
    82→            {
    83→                "position": position,
    84→                "status": "pending",
    85→                "prompt_path": str(self.prompt_files.get(batch_index, "")),
    86→                "result_path": str(self.output_files.get(batch_index, "")),
    87→                "log_path": str(self.log_files.get(batch_index, "")),
    88→            },
    89→        )
    90→        if event == "queued":
    91→            state["status"] = "queued"
    92→            print(
    93→                self.colorize_fn(
    94→                    f"  Batch {position}/{self.total_batches} queued (#{batch_index + 1})",
    95→                    "dim",
    96→                )
    97→            )
    98→            self.append_run_log_fn(
    99→                f"batch-queued batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{self.total_batches}"
   100→            )
   101→            return
   102→        if event == "start":
   103→            state["status"] = "running"
   104→            state["started_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   105→            print(
   106→                self.colorize_fn(
   107→                    f"  Batch {position}/{self.total_batches} started (#{batch_index + 1})",
   108→                    "dim",
   109→                )
   110→            )
   111→            self.append_run_log_fn(
   112→                f"batch-start batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{self.total_batches}"
   113→            )
   114→            return
   115→        if event != "done":
   116→            return
   117→        self._mark_done(batch_index, code=code, details=details)
   118→
   119→    def report_event(self, progress_event: BatchProgressEvent) -> None:
   120→        """Typed event entrypoint shared with runner_parallel callbacks."""
   121→        if not hasattr(progress_event, "batch_index") or not hasattr(
   122→            progress_event,
   123→            "event",
   124→        ):
   125→            return
   126→        details = getattr(progress_event, "details", {})
   127→        payload = details if isinstance(details, dict) else {}
   128→        self.report(
   129→            int(getattr(progress_event, "batch_index", -1)),
   130→            str(getattr(progress_event, "event", "")),
   131→            getattr(progress_event, "code", None),
   132→            **payload,
   133→        )
   134→
   135→    def record_execution_issue(self, batch_index: int, exc: Exception) -> None:
   136→        if batch_index < 0:
   137→            self.append_run_log_fn(f"execution-error heartbeat error={exc}")
   138→            return
   139→        self.append_run_log_fn(f"execution-error batch={batch_index + 1} error={exc}")
   140→
   141→    def mark_interrupted(self) -> None:
   142→        for idx in self.selected_indexes:
   143→            key = str(idx + 1)
   144→            state = self.batch_status.setdefault(
   145→                key,
   146→                {"position": self.batch_positions.get(idx, 0), "status": "pending"},
   147→            )
   148→            if state.get("status") in {"pending", "queued", "running"}:
   149→                state["status"] = "interrupted"
   150→
   151→    def mark_final_statuses(
   152→        self,
   153→        *,
   154→        selected_indexes: list[int],
   155→        failure_set: set[int],
   156→        execution_failure_set: set[int],
   157→    ) -> None:
   158→        for idx in selected_indexes:
   159→            key = str(idx + 1)
   160→            state = self.batch_status.setdefault(
   161→                key,
   162→                {"position": self.batch_positions.get(idx, 0), "status": "pending"},
   163→            )
   164→            if idx not in failure_set:
   165→                state["status"] = "succeeded"
   166→                continue
   167→            if idx in execution_failure_set:
   168→                state["status"] = "failed"
   169→                continue
   170→            if not self.output_files[idx].exists():
   171→                state["status"] = "missing_output"
   172→                continue
   173→            state["status"] = "parse_failed"
   174→
   175→    def _report_heartbeat(self, details: dict[str, object]) -> None:
   176→        active, queued, elapsed = _normalize_heartbeat_payload(details)
   177→        if not active and not queued:
   178→            return
   179→        segments = _heartbeat_segments(active, elapsed)
   180→        queued_segment = f", queued {len(queued)}" if queued else ""
   181→        print(
   182→            self.colorize_fn(
   183→                "  Batch heartbeat: "
   184→                f"{len(active)}/{self.total_batches} active{queued_segment} "
   185→                f"({', '.join(segments) if segments else 'running batches pending'})",
   186→                "dim",
   187→            )
   188→        )
   189→        self.append_run_log_fn(
   190→            "heartbeat "
   191→            f"active={[idx + 1 for idx in active]} queued={[idx + 1 for idx in queued]} "
   192→            f"elapsed={{{_heartbeat_elapsed_log(active, elapsed)}}}"
   193→        )
   194→        if self.stall_warning_seconds <= 0:
   195→            return
   196→        slow_active = _slow_active_batches(
   197→            active,
   198→            elapsed=elapsed,
   199→            threshold=self.stall_warning_seconds,
   200→        )
   201→        newly_warned = [idx for idx in slow_active if idx not in self.stall_warned_batches]
   202→        if not newly_warned:
   203→            return
   204→        self.stall_warned_batches.update(newly_warned)
   205→        warning_message = (
   206→            "  Stall warning: batches "
   207→            f"{[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(newly_warned)]} exceeded "
   208→            f"{self.stall_warning_seconds}s elapsed. "
   209→            "This may be normal for long runs; review run.log and batch logs."
   210→        )
   211→        print(self.colorize_fn(warning_message, "yellow"))
   212→        self.append_run_log_fn(
   213→            "stall-warning "
   214→            f"threshold={self.stall_warning_seconds}s "
   215→            f"batches={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(newly_warned)]}"
   216→        )
   217→
   218→    def _mark_done(self, batch_index: int, *, code: int | None, details: dict[str, object]) -> None:
   219→        position = self.batch_positions.get(batch_index, 0)
   220→        key = str(batch_index + 1)
   221→        state = self.batch_status.setdefault(
   222→            key,
   223→            {
   224→                "position": position,
   225→                "status": "pending",
   226→                "prompt_path": str(self.prompt_files.get(batch_index, "")),
   227→                "result_path": str(self.output_files.get(batch_index, "")),
   228→                "log_path": str(self.log_files.get(batch_index, "")),
   229→            },
   230→        )
   231→        status = "done" if code == 0 else f"failed ({code})"
   232→        tone = "dim" if code == 0 else "yellow"
   233→        elapsed_seconds = details.get("elapsed_seconds")
   234→        elapsed_suffix = ""
   235→        if isinstance(elapsed_seconds, int | float):
   236→            elapsed_suffix = f" in {int(max(0, elapsed_seconds))}s"
   237→            state["elapsed_seconds"] = int(max(0, elapsed_seconds))
   238→        state["status"] = "succeeded" if code == 0 else "failed"
   239→        state["exit_code"] = int(code) if isinstance(code, int) else code
   240→        state["completed_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   241→        self.stall_warned_batches.discard(batch_index)
   242→        print(
   243→            self.colorize_fn(
   244→                f"  Batch {position}/{self.total_batches} {status}{elapsed_suffix} (#{batch_index + 1})",
   245→                tone,
   246→            )
   247→        )
   248→        self.append_run_log_fn(
   249→            f"batch-done batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{self.total_batches} "
   250→            f"code={code} elapsed={state.get('elapsed_seconds', 0)}"
   251→        )
   252→
   253→
   254→def _normalize_heartbeat_payload(
   255→    details: dict[str, object],
   256→) -> tuple[list[int], list[int], dict[int, object]]:
   257→    active = details.get("active_batches")
   258→    queued = details.get("queued_batches", [])
   259→    elapsed = details.get("elapsed_seconds", {})
   260→    active_list = active if isinstance(active, list) else []
   261→    queued_list = queued if isinstance(queued, list) else []
   262→    elapsed_map = elapsed if isinstance(elapsed, dict) else {}
   263→    return active_list, queued_list, elapsed_map
   264→
   265→
   266→def _elapsed_seconds_for(elapsed: dict[int, object], index: int) -> int:
   267→    raw_secs = elapsed.get(index, 0)
   268→    return int(raw_secs) if isinstance(raw_secs, int | float) else 0
   269→
   270→
   271→def _heartbeat_segments(active: list[int], elapsed: dict[int, object]) -> list[str]:
   272→    segments: list[str] = []
   273→    for idx in active[:6]:
   274→        segments.append(f"#{idx + 1}:{_elapsed_seconds_for(elapsed, idx)}s")
   275→    if len(active) > 6:
   276→        segments.append(f"+{len(active) - 6} more")
   277→    return segments
   278→
   279→
   280→def _heartbeat_elapsed_log(active: list[int], elapsed: dict[int, object]) -> str:
   281→    return ", ".join(f"{idx + 1}:{elapsed.get(idx, 0)}" for idx in active)
   282→
   283→
   284→def _slow_active_batches(
   285→    active: list[int],
   286→    *,
   287→    elapsed: dict[int, object],
   288→    threshold: int,
   289→) -> list[int]:
   290→    return [
   291→        idx
   292→        for idx in active
   293→        if _elapsed_seconds_for(elapsed, idx) >= threshold
   294→    ]
   295→
   296→
   297→def resolve_run_log_path(
   298→    raw_run_log_file: object,
   299→    *,
   300→    project_root: Path,
   301→    run_dir: Path,
   302→) -> Path:
   303→    if isinstance(raw_run_log_file, str) and raw_run_log_file.strip():
   304→        candidate = Path(raw_run_log_file.strip()).expanduser()
   305→        run_log_path = candidate if candidate.is_absolute() else project_root / candidate
   306→    else:
   307→        run_log_path = run_dir / "run.log"
   308→    run_log_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   309→    return run_log_path
   310→
   311→
   312→def make_run_log_writer(run_log_path: Path) -> Callable[[str], None]:
   313→    def _append_run_log(message: str) -> None:
   314→        line = f"{datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec='seconds')} {message}\n"
   315→        try:
   316→            with run_log_path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
   317→                handle.write(line)
   318→        except OSError:
   319→            return
   320→
   321→    return _append_run_log
   322→
   323→
   324→def build_batch_tasks(
   325→    *,
   326→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   327→    prompt_files: dict[int, Path],
   328→    output_files: dict[int, Path],
   329→    log_files: dict[int, Path],
   330→    project_root: Path,
   331→    run_codex_batch_fn: Callable[..., int],
   332→) -> dict[int, Callable[[], int]]:
   333→    return {
   334→        idx: partial(
   335→            _run_batch_task,
   336→            batch_index=idx,
   337→            prompt_path=prompt_files[idx],
   338→            output_path=output_files[idx],
   339→            log_path=log_files[idx],
   340→            project_root=project_root,
   341→            run_codex_batch_fn=run_codex_batch_fn,
   342→        )
   343→        for idx in selected_indexes
   344→    }
   345→
   346→
   347→def write_run_summary(
   348→    *,
   349→    summary_path: Path,
   350→    summary_config: BatchRunSummaryConfig,
   351→    batch_status: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
   352→    successful_batches: list[int],
   353→    failed_batches: list[int],
   354→    safe_write_text_fn: Callable[[Path, str], None],
   355→    colorize_fn: Callable[[str, str], str],
   356→    append_run_log_fn: Callable[[str], None],
   357→    interrupted: bool = False,
   358→    interruption_reason: str | None = None,
   359→) -> None:
   360→    run_summary: dict[str, object] = {
   361→        "created_at": summary_config.created_at,
   362→        "run_stamp": summary_config.run_stamp,
   363→        "runner": summary_config.runner,
   364→        "parallel": summary_config.run_parallel,
   365→        "selected_batches": [idx + 1 for idx in summary_config.selected_indexes],
   366→        "successful_batches": successful_batches,
   367→        "failed_batches": failed_batches,
   368→        "allow_partial": summary_config.allow_partial,
   369→        "max_parallel_batches": (
   370→            summary_config.max_parallel_batches if summary_config.run_parallel else 1
   371→        ),
   372→        "batch_timeout_seconds": summary_config.batch_timeout_seconds,
   373→        "batch_max_retries": summary_config.batch_max_retries,
   374→        "batch_retry_backoff_seconds": summary_config.batch_retry_backoff_seconds,
   375→        "batch_heartbeat_seconds": (
   376→            summary_config.heartbeat_seconds if summary_config.run_parallel else None
   377→        ),
   378→        "batch_stall_warning_seconds": (
   379→            summary_config.stall_warning_seconds if summary_config.run_parallel else None
   380→        ),
   381→        "batch_stall_kill_seconds": summary_config.stall_kill_seconds,
   382→        "immutable_packet": str(summary_config.immutable_packet_path),
   383→        "blind_packet": str(summary_config.prompt_packet_path),
   384→        "run_dir": str(summary_config.run_dir),
   385→        "logs_dir": str(summary_config.logs_dir),
   386→        "run_log": str(summary_config.run_log_path),
   387→        "batches": batch_status,
   388→    }
   389→    if isinstance(summary_config.backlog_gate, dict):
   390→        run_summary["backlog_gate"] = summary_config.backlog_gate
   391→    if interrupted:
   392→        run_summary["interrupted"] = True
   393→        if interruption_reason:
   394→            run_summary["interruption_reason"] = interruption_reason
   395→    safe_write_text_fn(summary_path, json.dumps(run_summary, indent=2) + "\n")
   396→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Run summary: {summary_path}", "dim"))
   397→    append_run_log_fn(f"run-summary {summary_path}")
   398→
   399→
   400→def _run_batch_task(
   401→    *,
   402→    batch_index: int,
   403→    prompt_path: Path,
   404→    output_path: Path,
   405→    log_path: Path,
   406→    project_root: Path,
   407→    run_codex_batch_fn: Callable[..., int],
   408→) -> int:
   409→    try:
   410→        prompt = prompt_path.read_text()
   411→    except OSError as exc:
   412→        raise RuntimeError(
   413→            f"unable to read prompt for batch #{batch_index + 1}: {prompt_path}"
   414→        ) from exc
   415→    return run_codex_batch_fn(
   416→        prompt=prompt,
   417→        repo_root=project_root,
   418→        output_file=output_path,
   419→        log_file=log_path,
   420→    )
   421→
   422→
   423→__all__ = [
   424→    "BatchProgressTracker",
   425→    "BatchRunSummaryConfig",
   426→    "build_batch_tasks",
   427→    "make_run_log_writer",
   428→    "resolve_run_log_path",
   429→    "write_run_summary",
   430→]
   431→
```
